    <button class="b s" id="b-release" title="Remet la mesure en évaluation automatique">Remettre en automatique</button>

<script>
const $ = (s, r = document) => r.querySelector(s);
const esc = s => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const LABELS = {level_out_of_range:'Niveau hors plage plausible',inconsistent_levels:'Niveaux Lmin / LAeq / Lmax incohérents',timestamp_in_future:'Horodatage dans le futur',measurement_too_old:'Mesure trop ancienne',gps_accuracy_too_low:'Précision GPS insuffisante',capture_too_short:'Capture trop courte',audio_saturation:'Saturation du micro',moved_during_capture:'Déplacement pendant la mesure',uncalibrated_device:'Téléphone non calibré',neighbor_outlier:'Écart avec les zones voisines',insufficient_neighbors:'Peu de voisines pour comparer',low_score:'Score global trop bas',admin_override:"Décision d'un administrateur"};
const SUBS = {gps:'GPS',duration:'Durée',saturation:'Saturation',movement:'Mouvement',calibration:'Calibration',neighbor:'Voisines'};
const STATUS = {pending:'En évaluation',indicative:'Indicative',rejected:'Rejetée',validated:'Validée'};
const S = {base: localStorage.getItem('ss_api') || 'https://soundmaps.rhmanager.site/api/v1', access: sessionStorage.getItem('ss_at'), refresh: sessionStorage.getItem('ss_rt'),
  me: null, offset: 0, total: 0, items: [], sel: new Set(), open: new Set()};
let refreshing = null;

function toast(msg){ const t = document.createElement('div'); t.className = 'toast'; t.textContent = msg; document.body.append(t); setTimeout(() => t.remove(), 3500); }
function saveTokens(a, r){ S.access = a; S.refresh = r; sessionStorage.setItem('ss_at', a); sessionStorage.setItem('ss_rt', r); }
function logout(){ S.access = S.refresh = null; sessionStorage.clear(); $('#app').hidden = true; $('#login').hidden = false; }

async function refreshTokens(){
  if (!S.refresh) return false;
  refreshing ||= fetch(S.base + '/auth/refresh', {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body: JSON.stringify({refresh_token: S.refresh})})
    .then(async r => { if (!r.ok) return false; const d = await r.json(); saveTokens(d.access_token, d.refresh_token); return true; })
    .catch(() => false).finally(() => { refreshing = null; });
  return refreshing;
}

async function api(path, {method = 'GET', body, query} = {}, retry = true){
  const url = new URL(S.base + path);
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(query || {})) {
    if (Array.isArray(v)) v.forEach(x => url.searchParams.append(k, x));
    else if (v !== '' && v != null) url.searchParams.set(k, v);
  }
  let r;
  try {
    r = await fetch(url, {method, headers: {...(body ? {'Content-Type':'application/json'} : {}), ...(S.access ? {Authorization:'Bearer ' + S.access} : {})}, body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined});
  } catch { throw new Error('Serveur injoignable (réseau ou CORS)'); }
  if (r.status === 401 && retry && await refreshTokens()) return api(path, {method, body, query}, false);
  if (r.status === 401) { logout(); throw new Error('Session expirée'); }
  const data = await r.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!r.ok) throw new Error(typeof data?.detail === 'string' ? data.detail : 'Erreur ' + r.status);
  return data;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ connexion
$('#l-api').value = S.base;
$('#l-go').onclick = async () => {
  $('#l-err').textContent = '';
  S.base = $('#l-api').value.trim().replace(/\/+$/, ''); localStorage.setItem('ss_api', S.base);
  try {
    const r = await fetch(S.base + '/auth/login', {method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body: JSON.stringify({email: $('#l-email').value.trim(), password: $('#l-pass').value})});
    const d = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(typeof d.detail === 'string' ? d.detail : 'Erreur ' + r.status);
    saveTokens(d.access_token, d.refresh_token);
    await start();
  } catch (e) { $('#l-err').textContent = e.message === 'invalid_credentials' ? 'Identifiants incorrects.' : e.message; }
};
$('#l-pass').addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') $('#l-go').click(); });
$('#logout').onclick = async () => { try { await api('/auth/logout', {method:'POST', body:{refresh_token: S.refresh}}); } catch {} logout(); };

async function start(){
  S.me = await api('/users/me');
  if (!['tenant_admin', 'analyst'].includes(S.me.role)) { logout(); throw new Error("Ce compte n'a pas accès à l'administration."); }
  $('#who').textContent = `${S.me.pseudonym} · ${S.me.role === 'tenant_admin' ? 'Administrateur' : 'Analyste'}`;
  $('#login').hidden = true; $('#app').hidden = false;
  $('#th-act').hidden = !isAdmin();
  await Promise.all([loadReasons(), loadQueue()]);
}
const isAdmin = () => S.me?.role === 'tenant_admin';

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ onglets
document.querySelectorAll('nav button').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
  document.querySelectorAll('nav button').forEach(x => x.classList.toggle('on', x === b));
  $('#tab-val').hidden = b.dataset.tab !== 'val'; $('#tab-stats').hidden = b.dataset.tab !== 'stats';
  if (b.dataset.tab === 'stats') loadStats();
  updateBulk();
});

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ file de validation
async function loadReasons(){
  try {
    const s = await api('/validation/stats', {query:{days: 30}});
    const sel = $('#f-reason');
    s.top_reasons.forEach(r => sel.append(new Option(`${LABELS[r.code] || r.code} (${r.n})`, r.code)));
  } catch {}
}

function filters(){
  return {status: [...document.querySelectorAll('#f-status input:checked')].map(i => i.value),
    min_score: $('#f-smin').value, max_score: $('#f-smax').value, calibration: $('#f-cal').value, evaluator: $('#f-eval').value,
    reason: $('#f-reason').value, h3_index: $('#f-h3').value.trim(), date_from: $('#f-from').value, date_to: $('#f-to').value,
    sort: $('#f-sort').value, limit: $('#f-size').value, offset: S.offset};
}

async function loadQueue(){
  try {
    const d = await api('/validation/queue', {query: filters()});
    S.items = d.items; S.total = d.total; S.sel.clear(); S.open.clear();
    renderRows();
  } catch (e) { toast(e.message); }
}

function chip(st){ return `<span class="chip ${esc(st)}">${esc(STATUS[st] || st)}</span>`; }
function renderRows(){
  const body = $('#rows'); const size = +$('#f-size').value;
  body.innerHTML = '';
  S.items.forEach(m => {
    const tr = document.createElement('tr'); tr.className = 'r'; tr.dataset.id = m.id;
    const reasons = (m.reasons || []).filter(r => r.code !== 'insufficient_neighbors').map(r => `<span class="chip info" title="${esc(r.reason || '')}">${esc(LABELS[r.code] || r.code)}</span>`).join('');
    const score = m.quality_score == null ? '–' : `${Math.round(m.quality_score * 100)} %<div class="bar"><i style="width:${Math.round(m.quality_score * 100)}%"></i></div>`;
    tr.innerHTML = `<td><input type="checkbox" class="sel" ${S.sel.has(m.id) ? 'checked' : ''}></td>
      <td>${esc(new Date(m.measured_at).toLocaleString('fr-FR', {dateStyle:'short', timeStyle:'short'}))}</td>
      <td title="${esc(m.h3_index)}">${esc(m.h3_index.slice(-7))}</td><td><b>${esc(m.laeq_db)}</b> dB(A)<div class="kv">max ${esc(m.lmax_db)}</div></td>
      <td>${score}</td><td>${chip(m.quality_status)}</td><td>${reasons || '<span class="kv">—</span>'}</td>
      <td class="kv">${esc(m.evaluator === 'admin' ? 'Admin' : m.evaluator === 'auto' ? 'Auto' : '—')}</td>
      ${isAdmin() ? `<td style="white-space:nowrap"><button class="b ok" data-a="validated" title="Valider">✓</button> <button class="b wa" data-a="indicative" title="Indicative">~</button> <button class="b er" data-a="rejected" title="Rejeter">✗</button></td>` : ''}`;
    body.append(tr);
    const det = document.createElement('tr'); det.className = 'det'; det.dataset.det = m.id; det.hidden = !S.open.has(m.id);
    det.innerHTML = `<td colspan="${isAdmin() ? 9 : 8}">${detailHtml(m)}</td>`;
    body.append(det);
  });
  if (!S.items.length) body.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="9" class="kv" style="padding:24px">Aucune mesure pour ces filtres.</td></tr>';
  $('#count').textContent = `${S.total} mesure(s) · ${S.offset + 1}–${Math.min(S.offset + size, S.total)}`;
  $('#prev').disabled = S.offset === 0; $('#next').disabled = S.offset + size >= S.total;
  $('#all').checked = false; updateBulk();
}

function detailHtml(m){
  const subs = Object.entries(m.sub_scores || {}).map(([k, v]) => `<div class="kv">${esc(SUBS[k] || k)}<b>${Math.round(v * 100)} %</b><div class="bar"><i style="width:${Math.round(v * 100)}%"></i></div></div>`).join('');
  const rs = (m.reasons || []).map(r => `<div class="kv"><b>${esc(LABELS[r.code] || r.code)}</b>${esc(r.severity || '')}${r.reason ? ' · ' + esc(r.reason) : ''}${r.z != null ? ' · z=' + esc(r.z) : ''}${r.value != null ? ' · valeur ' + esc(r.value) : ''}${r.ratio != null ? ' · ratio ' + esc(r.ratio) : ''}</div>`).join('');
  const tech = [['Durée', `${m.effective_duration_seconds}/${m.duration_seconds} s`], ['Précision GPS', `${Math.round(m.gps_accuracy_m)} m`], ['Saturation', `${(m.saturation_ratio * 100).toFixed(1)} %`],
    ['Déplacement', `${Math.round(m.moved_distance_m)} m`], ['Lmin', `${m.lmin_db} dB`], ['Plage', m.day_part], ['Contexte', m.noise_context || '—'], ['Échantillonnage', m.sample_rate_hz ? m.sample_rate_hz + ' Hz' : '—'],
    ['Téléphone', m.calibration_status === 'calibrated' ? 'Calibré' : 'Non calibré'], ['Contributeur', m.contributor_token]].map(([k, v]) => `<div class="kv">${esc(k)}<b>${esc(v)}</b></div>`).join('');
  return `<div class="grid">${subs}</div><div class="grid" style="margin-top:10px">${tech}</div><div style="margin-top:10px">${rs}</div>`;
}

$('#rows').addEventListener('click', async e => {
  const tr = e.target.closest('tr.r'); if (!tr) return;
  const id = tr.dataset.id;
  if (e.target.classList.contains('sel')) { e.target.checked ? S.sel.add(id) : S.sel.delete(id); updateBulk(); return; }
  const a = e.target.closest('button[data-a]');
  if (a) {
    const reason = prompt('Motif (3 caractères min.)', 'Validation manuelle'); if (!reason || reason.trim().length < 3) return;
    await apply([id], a.dataset.a, reason.trim()); return;
  }
  const det = $(`tr[data-det="${CSS.escape(id)}"]`); det.hidden = !det.hidden; det.hidden ? S.open.delete(id) : S.open.add(id);
});
$('#all').onchange = e => { S.items.forEach(m => e.target.checked ? S.sel.add(m.id) : S.sel.delete(m.id)); document.querySelectorAll('.sel').forEach(c => c.checked = e.target.checked); updateBulk(); };

function updateBulk(){
  const show = isAdmin() && !$('#tab-val').hidden && S.sel.size > 0;
  $('#bulk').hidden = !show; $('#b-count').textContent = `${S.sel.size} sélectionnée(s)`;
}
async function apply(ids, status, reason){
  try {
    const r = await api('/validation/bulk', {method:'POST', body:{ids, status, reason}});
    toast(`${r.updated} mesure(s) mise(s) à jour${r.skipped ? ` · ${r.skipped} introuvable(s)` : ''}`);
    await loadQueue();
  } catch (e) { toast(e.message); }
}
$('#b-apply').onclick = () => {
  const reason = $('#b-reason').value.trim();
  if (reason.length < 3) { toast('Indique un motif (3 caractères min.)'); return; }
  if (S.sel.size > 200) { toast('200 mesures maximum par action'); return; }
  apply([...S.sel], $('#b-status').value, reason);
};
$('#b-release').onclick = async () => {
  try { const r = await api('/validation/release', {method:'POST', body:{ids:[...S.sel]}}); toast(`${r.released} mesure(s) remise(s) en évaluation automatique`); await loadQueue(); }
  catch (e) { toast(e.message); }
};
$('#b-clear').onclick = () => { S.sel.clear(); document.querySelectorAll('.sel').forEach(c => c.checked = false); updateBulk(); };
$('#f-go').onclick = () => { S.offset = 0; loadQueue(); };
$('#prev').onclick = () => { S.offset = Math.max(0, S.offset - +$('#f-size').value); loadQueue(); };
$('#next').onclick = () => { S.offset += +$('#f-size').value; loadQueue(); };

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ statistiques
async function loadStats(){
  try {
    const s = await api('/validation/stats', {query:{days: $('#s-days').value}});
    const colors = {validated:'#16a34a', indicative:'#d97706', rejected:'#dc2626', pending:'#64748b'};
    $('#s-totals').innerHTML = Object.entries(s.totals).map(([k, v]) => `<span class="chip ${k}">${esc(STATUS[k])} ${v}</span>`).join('') +
      `<span class="chip info">Décisions admin : ${s.admin_overrides}</span>` + (s.pending_stuck ? `<span class="chip rejected">${s.pending_stuck} en attente depuis > 5 min</span>` : '');
    $('#s-days-list').innerHTML = s.days.map(d => { const t = Object.values({v:d.validated, i:d.indicative, r:d.rejected, p:d.pending}).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 1;
      return `<div class="row" style="align-items:center;margin:4px 0"><span class="kv" style="width:84px">${esc(d.date)}</span><div class="day" style="flex:1">${['validated','indicative','rejected','pending'].map(k => `<i style="width:${d[k] / t * 100}%;background:${colors[k]}" title="${esc(STATUS[k])} ${d[k]}"></i>`).join('')}</div><span class="kv" style="width:40px;text-align:right">${t}</span></div>`; }).join('');
    $('#s-reasons').innerHTML = s.top_reasons.map(r => `<div class="row" style="align-items:center;margin:4px 0"><span style="width:300px">${esc(LABELS[r.code] || r.code)}</span><span class="chip info">${r.n}</span></div>`).join('') || '<span class="kv">Aucune.</span>';
  } catch (e) { toast(e.message); }
  try {
    const q = await api('/quota/overview');
    $('#s-quota').innerHTML = `Utilisateurs avec solde : <b>${q.users}</b> · solde moyen : <b>${q.avg_balance}</b> · objectif moyen/jour : <b>${q.avg_daily_target}</b> · besoin global : <b>${Math.round(q.context.need_global * 100)} %</b> · contributeurs actifs (7 j) : <b>${q.context.supply}</b> · déficit de mesures : <b>${Math.round(q.context.deficit)}</b>`;
  } catch (e) { $('#s-quota').textContent = e.message; }
  try {
    const r = await api('/missions/ranking/active');
    $('#s-rank').innerHTML = r.map(u => `<tr><td>${esc(u.pseudonym)}</td><td>${Math.round(u.score * 100)} %</td><td>${esc(u.activity_14d)}</td><td>${Math.round(u.reliability * 100)} %</td><td>${Math.round(u.completion * 100)} %</td><td>${Math.round(u.acceptance * 100)} %</td><td>${esc(u.load)}</td></tr>`).join('') || '<tr><td colspan="7" class="kv">Aucun utilisateur éligible.</td></tr>';
  } catch (e) { $('#s-rank').innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="7" class="kv">${esc(e.message)}</td></tr>`; }
}
$('#s-days').onchange = loadStats;

if (S.access) start().catch(() => logout());
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
const $=s=>document.querySelector(s),esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const base=localStorage.getItem('ss_api')||'https://soundmaps.rhmanager.site/api/v1';let at=sessionStorage.getItem('ss_at'),rt=sessionStorage.getItem('ss_rt');
if(!at){location.href='index.html'}
const toast=m=>{const t=document.createElement('div');t.className='toast';t.textContent=m;document.body.append(t);setTimeout(()=>t.remove(),3200)};
async function refresh(){const r=await fetch(base+'/auth/refresh',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({refresh_token:rt})});
  if(!r.ok)return false;const d=await r.json();at=d.access_token;rt=d.refresh_token;sessionStorage.setItem('ss_at',at);sessionStorage.setItem('ss_rt',rt);return true}
async function api(p,{method='GET',body}={},retry=true){
  const r=await fetch(base+p,{method,headers:{...(body?{'Content-Type':'application/json'}:{}),Authorization:'Bearer '+at},body:body?JSON.stringify(body):undefined});
  if(r.status===401&&retry&&await refresh())return api(p,{method,body},false);
  if(r.status===401){location.href='index.html';throw new Error('Session expirée')}
  const d=await r.json().catch(()=>null);if(!r.ok)throw new Error(typeof d?.detail==='string'?d.detail:'Erreur '+r.status);return d}
const fd=s=>s?new Date(s).toLocaleString('fr-FR',{dateStyle:'short',timeStyle:'short'}):'—';
async function loadInv(){try{const l=await api('/account/invitations/all');
  $('#inv').innerHTML=l.map(i=>`<tr><td><code>${esc(i.code)}</code></td><td>${esc(i.kind)}</td><td>${i.uses}/${i.max_uses}</td><td>${fd(i.expires_at)}</td><td>${i.active?'Actif':'Inactif'}</td><td>${esc(i.note||'')}</td><td>${i.active?`<button class="e" data-id="${esc(i.id)}">Révoquer</button>`:''}</td></tr>`).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="7">Aucune invitation.</td></tr>'}catch(e){toast(e.message)}}
async function loadAn(){try{const l=await api('/analyst/admin/list');
  $('#an').innerHTML=l.map(a=>`<tr><td>${esc(a.display_name)}<div style="color:var(--mu);font-size:12px">${esc(a.pseudonym)}</div></td><td>${fd(a.since)}</td><td>${a.votes}</td><td>${Math.round(a.agreement*100)} %</td><td>${fd(a.suspended_until)}</td><td><button class="s" data-s="${esc(a.user_id)}">Suspendre 14 j</button> <button class="s" data-r="${esc(a.user_id)}">Réintégrer</button></td></tr>`).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="6">Aucun analyseur.</td></tr>'}catch(e){toast(e.message)}}
$('#go').onclick=async()=>{try{const r=await api('/account/invitations/admin',{method:'POST',body:{count:+$('#n').value,max_uses:+$('#u').value,ttl_days:+$('#d').value,note:$('#note').value||null}});
  toast(r.length+' code(s) créé(s) : '+r.map(i=>i.code).join(', '));loadInv()}catch(e){toast(e.message)}};
$('#inv').onclick=async e=>{const id=e.target.dataset.id;if(!id)return;try{await api('/account/invitations/'+id,{method:'DELETE'});loadInv()}catch(x){toast(x.message)}};
$('#an').onclick=async e=>{try{if(e.target.dataset.s)await api('/analyst/admin/'+e.target.dataset.s+'/suspend',{method:'POST',body:{days:14}});
  if(e.target.dataset.r)await api('/analyst/admin/'+e.target.dataset.r+'/reinstate',{method:'POST'});loadAn()}catch(x){toast(x.message)}};
loadInv();loadAn();
</script></body></html>

from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict


class AccountSettings(BaseSettings):
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_file=".env", env_file_encoding="utf-8", extra="ignore")

    # Comptes
    INVITE_REQUIRED: bool = False
    INVITE_CODE_LENGTH: int = 10
    INVITE_USER_MAX_ACTIVE: int = 3
    INVITE_USER_MAX_USES: int = 5
    INVITE_USER_TTL_DAYS: int = 14
    RECOVERY_CODES_COUNT: int = 8
    LOGIN_EVENTS_LIST: int = 15
    LOGIN_EVENTS_RETENTION_DAYS: int = 180
    RATE_LIMIT_PWRESET_PER_MINUTE: int = 5

    # Analyseurs
    ANALYST_MIN_VALIDATED: int = 20
    ANALYST_MIN_MISSIONS: int = 2
    ANALYST_MIN_RELIABILITY: float = 0.75
    ANALYST_MIN_ACCOUNT_DAYS: int = 30
    ANALYST_MIN_ACTIVE_DAYS: int = 8
    ANALYST_MAX_INACTIVE_DAYS: int = 15
    ANALYST_SUSPEND_MIN_VOTES: int = 20
    ANALYST_SUSPEND_AGREEMENT: float = 0.4
    ANALYST_SUSPEND_DAYS: int = 14

    # Avis en temps réel
    REVIEW_RADIUS_M: float = 500
    REVIEW_TTL_MINUTES: int = 10
    REVIEW_MAX_AGE_MINUTES: int = 10
    REVIEW_MAX_REVIEWERS: int = 7
    REVIEW_MIN_VOTES: int = 3
    REVIEW_MAX_PENDING_PER_USER: int = 5
    REVIEW_CONFIRM_RATIO: float = 0.6
    REVIEW_ADJUST_RATIO: float = 0.5
    REVIEW_STRONG_REJECT_SHARE: float = 0.6
    REVIEW_ADJUST_STEP_DB: float = 2.0
    REVIEW_ADJUST_MAX_DB: float = 3.0
    PRESENCE_TTL_SECONDS: int = 180

    # Classement
    LB_PTS_VALIDATED: float = 10.0
    LB_PTS_INDICATIVE: float = 4.0
    LB_PTS_REJECTED: float = -2.0
    LB_PTS_CELL: float = 3.0
    LB_PTS_DAY: float = 5.0
    LB_PTS_MISSION: float = 40.0
    LB_PTS_MISSION_UNIT: float = 4.0
    LB_PTS_REVIEW: float = 2.0
    LB_PTS_INVITE: float = 25.0
    LB_RELIABILITY_PRIOR: float = 0.6

    BEAT_XP_SECONDS: int = 21600

from functools import lru_cache
from typing import Literal

from pydantic import SecretStr, model_validator
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_file=".env", env_file_encoding="utf-8", extra="ignore")

    # Application
    APP_NAME: str = "SunuSound"
    APP_ENV: Literal["development", "staging", "production"] = "development"
    API_V1_PREFIX: str = "/api/v1"
    LOG_LEVEL: str = "INFO"
    CORS_ORIGINS: str = ""
    ALLOWED_HOSTS: str = "*"
    MAX_BODY_BYTES: int = 1_048_576
    METRICS_TOKEN: SecretStr

    # PostgreSQL
    DATABASE_URL: str
    MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL: str
    APP_DB_USER: str
    DB_POOL_SIZE: int = 10
    DB_MAX_OVERFLOW: int = 20

    # Redis / Celery
    REDIS_URL: str
    CELERY_BROKER_URL: str
    CELERY_RESULT_BACKEND: str
    CELERY_TASK_TIME_LIMIT: int = 300
    BEAT_AGGREGATION_SECONDS: int = 120
    BEAT_SWEEP_SECONDS: int = 120
    BEAT_EXPIRE_PROPOSALS_SECONDS: int = 60
    BEAT_ADAPT_WEIGHTS_SECONDS: int = 3600
    BEAT_PRIVACY_SECONDS: int = 86400
    BEAT_AI_DIGEST_SECONDS: int = 86400
    BEAT_QUOTA_SECONDS: int = 1800
    BEAT_MISSIONS_SECONDS: int = 10800
    BEAT_MISSIONS_RECONCILE_SECONDS: int = 600

    # Sécurité
    JWT_SECRET_KEY: SecretStr
    JWT_ALGORITHM: str = "HS256"
    ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES: int = 15
    REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS: int = 30
    HASH_PEPPER: SecretStr
    PSEUDONYM_SALT: SecretStr
    PLATFORM_ADMIN_KEY: SecretStr
    PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH: int = 10
    LOGIN_MAX_ATTEMPTS: int = 5
    LOGIN_LOCK_SECONDS: int = 900
    RATE_LIMIT_AUTH_PER_MINUTE: int = 10
    RATE_LIMIT_API_PER_MINUTE: int = 120
    RATE_LIMIT_MEASUREMENTS_PER_MINUTE: int = 60
    RATE_LIMIT_AI_PER_MINUTE: int = 10

    # API
    TOMTOM_API_KEY: SecretStr = SecretStr("")
    TOMTOM_BASE_URL: str = "https://api.tomtom.com"
    TOMTOM_COUNTRY_SET: str = "SN"
    TOMTOM_LANGUAGE: str = "fr-FR"
    TOMTOM_TIMEOUT_SECONDS: float = 8
    TOMTOM_TILE_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS: int = 900
    TOMTOM_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS: int = 60
    TOMTOM_SEARCH_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS: int = 3600
    RATE_LIMIT_TOMTOM_PER_MINUTE: int = 60
    RATE_LIMIT_TILES_PER_MINUTE: int = 1200

    # Géographie
    H3_RESOLUTION: int = 9
    TIMEZONE: str = "Africa/Dakar"
    DAYPART_MORNING_START: int = 6
    DAYPART_AFTERNOON_START: int = 12
    DAYPART_EVENING_START: int = 18
    DAYPART_NIGHT_START: int = 22
    MAP_MAX_CELLS: int = 5000
    MAX_RANGE_DAYS: int = 366

    # Collecte
    SESSION_DURATION_SECONDS: int = 15
    PROPOSAL_TTL_MINUTES: int = 30
    PROPOSAL_COMPLETION_GRACE_MINUTES: int = 30
    MIN_BATTERY_LEVEL: int = 15
    MAX_PROPOSALS_PER_USER_PER_DAY: int = 6
    MIN_MINUTES_BETWEEN_PROPOSALS: int = 30
    SCHEDULED_TOLERANCE_MINUTES: int = 15
    CANDIDATE_RING_K: int = 3
    AVAILABILITY_WINDOW_MINUTES: int = 30
    BATCH_MAX_ITEMS: int = 200

    # Algorithme adaptatif
    W_FRESHNESS: float = 0.30
    W_NOISE_HISTORY: float = 0.25
    W_COVERAGE_GAP: float = 0.20
    W_TIME_RELEVANCE: float = 0.15
    W_AVAILABILITY: float = 0.10
    FRESHNESS_TAU_HOURS: float = 12
    NOISE_REF_MIN_DB: float = 40
    NOISE_REF_MAX_DB: float = 85
    NOISE_STD_REF_DB: float = 10
    NOISE_LEVEL_SHARE: float = 0.6
    UNKNOWN_HISTORY_PRIOR: float = 0.5
    COVERAGE_WINDOW_DAYS: int = 7
    HISTORY_WINDOW_DAYS: int = 30
    TARGET_MEASUREMENTS_PER_CELL: int = 20
    PROPOSAL_MIN_SCORE: float = 0.35
    PROPOSAL_PERCENTILE: float = 0.6
    MIN_RING_CELLS_FOR_PERCENTILE: int = 5
    EXPLORATION_EPSILON: float = 0.05
    ACCEPTANCE_PRIOR: float = 0.5
    ACCEPTANCE_PRIOR_STRENGTH: float = 4
    ADAPT_LEARNING_RATE: float = 0.5
    ADAPT_SHRINKAGE: float = 0.1
    ADAPT_MIN_WEIGHT: float = 0.05
    ADAPT_MAX_WEIGHT: float = 0.5
    ADAPT_MIN_SAMPLES: int = 30
    ADAPT_BATCH: int = 500

    # Quota dynamique (solde cumulable)
    QUOTA_ENABLED: bool = True
    QUOTA_INITIAL_BALANCE: float = 3.0
    QUOTA_MIN_DAILY: float = 2.0
    QUOTA_MAX_DAILY: float = 40.0
    QUOTA_COLD_START_DAILY: float = 10.0
    QUOTA_COLD_START_CELLS: int = 10
    QUOTA_MIN_CAP: float = 6.0
    QUOTA_CAP_DAYS: float = 2.5
    QUOTA_COST_MIN: float = 0.5
    QUOTA_COST_MAX: float = 2.0
    QUOTA_COOLDOWN_MIN_MINUTES: float = 3
    QUOTA_COOLDOWN_MAX_MINUTES: float = 180
    QUOTA_RETENTION_MIN: float = 0.55
    QUOTA_RETENTION_MAX: float = 0.92
    QUOTA_RELIABILITY_PRIOR: float = 0.6
    QUOTA_RELIABILITY_STRENGTH: float = 5
    QUOTA_LAZY_REFRESH_SECONDS: int = 300
    QUOTA_HARD_TOLERANCE_MINUTES: int = 15
    QUOTA_CONTEXT_TTL_SECONDS: int = 600
    QUOTA_BATCH: int = 2000
    QUOTA_DAYS_RETENTION: int = 90

    # Missions
    MISSION_OFFER_HOURS: int = 48
    MISSION_COMPLETION_HOURS: int = 72
    MISSION_MAX_RADIUS_KM: float = 25
    MISSION_ZONE_H3_RES: int = 7
    MISSION_MIN_COUNT: int = 2
    MISSION_MAX_COUNT: int = 10
    MISSION_MAX_PENDING: int = 10
    MISSION_MAX_ACTIVE: int = 5
    MISSION_MIN_SCORE: float = 0.35
    MISSION_MIN_ZONE_DEFICIT: int = 6
    MISSION_MAX_ZONES: int = 150
    MISSION_USERS_PER_ZONE: int = 3
    MISSION_MAX_NEW_PER_USER_PER_RUN: int = 2
    MISSION_REWARD_PER_MEASUREMENT: float = 0.5
    MISSION_DECLINE_COOLDOWN_DAYS: int = 7
    MISSION_ACTIVITY_TARGET: int = 20
    MISSION_SYNC_MIN_MINUTES: int = 60
    MS_W_ACTIVITY: float = 0.35
    MS_W_RELIABILITY: float = 0.30
    MS_W_COMPLETION: float = 0.20
    MS_W_ACCEPTANCE: float = 0.15

    # Historique animé
    TIMELAPSE_MAX_FRAMES: int = 120
    TIMELAPSE_MAX_ROWS: int = 200000
    TIMELAPSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS: int = 300

    # Qualité
    VALIDATED_MIN_SCORE: float = 0.75
    INDICATIVE_MIN_SCORE: float = 0.45
    ALLOW_UNCALIBRATED_VALIDATED: bool = False
    GPS_GOOD_M: float = 20
    GPS_REJECT_M: float = 100
    MIN_DURATION_RATIO: float = 0.9
    MAX_SATURATION_RATIO: float = 0.05
    MAX_MOVEMENT_M: float = 30
    LAEQ_MIN_DB: float = 20
    LAEQ_MAX_DB: float = 140
    LEVEL_CONSISTENCY_TOLERANCE_DB: float = 0.5
    TIMESTAMP_FUTURE_TOLERANCE_SECONDS: int = 300
    MAX_MEASUREMENT_AGE_HOURS: int = 168
    NEIGHBOR_Z_MAX: float = 3.0
    NEIGHBOR_MIN_SAMPLES: int = 5
    NEIGHBOR_WINDOW_DAYS: int = 14
    NEIGHBOR_SIGMA_FLOOR_DB: float = 3.0
    QUALITY_UNCALIBRATED_SUBSCORE: float = 0.5
    QUALITY_NEIGHBOR_NEUTRAL: float = 0.7
    QW_GPS: float = 0.25
    QW_DURATION: float = 0.20
    QW_SATURATION: float = 0.15
    QW_MOVEMENT: float = 0.10
    QW_CALIBRATION: float = 0.15
    QW_NEIGHBOR: float = 0.15
    REEVALUATION_DAYS: int = 30
    CONF_TARGET_MEASUREMENTS: int = 20
    CONF_TARGET_DEVICES: int = 5
    CONF_RECENCY_TAU_HOURS: float = 72
    CONF_W_COUNT: float = 0.40
    CONF_W_DEVICES: float = 0.25
    CONF_W_CALIBRATION: float = 0.20
    CONF_W_RECENCY: float = 0.15
    PUBLIC_MIN_DEVICES: int = 3
    DEFAULT_TENANT_SLUG: str = "sunusound"

    # Confidentialité
    LOCATION_RETENTION_DAYS: int = 30
    IDENTITY_UNLINK_DAYS: int = 90
    REPORT_RETENTION_DAYS: int = 30
    AUDIT_RETENTION_DAYS: int = 365
    CONSENT_POLICY_VERSION: str = "1"

    # Groq
    API_GROQ_KEY: SecretStr = SecretStr("")
    GROQ_BASE_URL: str = "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1"
    GROQ_MODEL_ANALYSIS: str = ""
    GROQ_MODEL_REPORT: str = ""
    GROQ_TIMEOUT_SECONDS: float = 30
    GROQ_MAX_TOKENS: int = 1200
    GROQ_TEMPERATURE: float = 0.2
    GROQ_MAX_RETRIES: int = 3
    AI_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS: int = 86400
    ANOMALY_Z_THRESHOLD: float = 2.5
    ANOMALY_MIN_BASELINE_DAYS: int = 3
    ANOMALY_BASELINE_DAYS: int = 7
    AI_DIGEST_MAX_CELLS: int = 10

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _validate(self):
        w = (self.W_FRESHNESS + self.W_NOISE_HISTORY + self.W_COVERAGE_GAP
             + self.W_TIME_RELEVANCE + self.W_AVAILABILITY)
        if abs(w - 1.0) > 1e-6:
            raise ValueError("Les poids W_* doivent totaliser 1.0")
        q = (self.QW_GPS + self.QW_DURATION + self.QW_SATURATION + self.QW_MOVEMENT
             + self.QW_CALIBRATION + self.QW_NEIGHBOR)
        if abs(q - 1.0) > 1e-6:
            raise ValueError("Les poids QW_* doivent totaliser 1.0")
        c = self.CONF_W_COUNT + self.CONF_W_DEVICES + self.CONF_W_CALIBRATION + self.CONF_W_RECENCY
        if abs(c - 1.0) > 1e-6:
            raise ValueError("Les poids CONF_W_* doivent totaliser 1.0")
        ms = self.MS_W_ACTIVITY + self.MS_W_RELIABILITY + self.MS_W_COMPLETION + self.MS_W_ACCEPTANCE
        if abs(ms - 1.0) > 1e-6:
            raise ValueError("Les poids MS_W_* doivent totaliser 1.0")
        if not (0 <= self.DAYPART_MORNING_START < self.DAYPART_AFTERNOON_START
                < self.DAYPART_EVENING_START < self.DAYPART_NIGHT_START <= 23):
            raise ValueError("DAYPART_* doivent être strictement croissants (0-23)")
        if self.INDICATIVE_MIN_SCORE > self.VALIDATED_MIN_SCORE:
            raise ValueError("INDICATIVE_MIN_SCORE doit être <= VALIDATED_MIN_SCORE")
        if (self.QUOTA_COST_MIN > self.QUOTA_COST_MAX
                or self.QUOTA_COOLDOWN_MIN_MINUTES > self.QUOTA_COOLDOWN_MAX_MINUTES
                or self.QUOTA_RETENTION_MIN > self.QUOTA_RETENTION_MAX
                or self.QUOTA_MIN_DAILY > self.QUOTA_MAX_DAILY):
            raise ValueError("Bornes QUOTA_* incohérentes")
        if self.MISSION_ZONE_H3_RES >= self.H3_RESOLUTION:
            raise ValueError("MISSION_ZONE_H3_RES doit être < H3_RESOLUTION")
        if self.MISSION_MAX_RADIUS_KM > 25:
            raise ValueError("MISSION_MAX_RADIUS_KM doit rester <= 25")
        if self.APP_ENV == "production":
            for name in ("JWT_SECRET_KEY", "HASH_PEPPER", "PSEUDONYM_SALT",
                         "PLATFORM_ADMIN_KEY", "METRICS_TOKEN"):
                if len(getattr(self, name).get_secret_value()) < 32:
                    raise ValueError(f"{name} doit contenir au moins 32 caractères en production")
        return self

    @property
    def cors_origins_list(self) -> list[str]:
        return [o.strip() for o in self.CORS_ORIGINS.split(",") if o.strip()]

    @property
    def allowed_hosts_list(self) -> list[str]:
        return [h.strip() for h in self.ALLOWED_HOSTS.split(",") if h.strip()] or ["*"]


@lru_cache
def get_settings() -> Settings:
    return Settings()


settings = get_settings()


################################################################################
# FICHIER: core/deps.py
################################################################################

import hmac
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass

import jwt
from fastapi import Depends, Header, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import HTTPAuthorizationCredentials, HTTPBearer
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.redis import get_redis
from app.core.security import decode_token
from app.db.session import SessionLocal
from app.models.enums import Role
from app.models.tenancy import Tenant, User

bearer = HTTPBearer(auto_error=False)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Principal:
    user_id: uuid.UUID
    tenant_id: uuid.UUID
    role: Role
    jti: str
    exp: int


def _unauthorized(detail: str = "not_authenticated") -> HTTPException:
    return HTTPException(status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail, headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"})


async def get_principal(creds: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials | None = Depends(bearer),
                        redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)) -> Principal:
    if creds is None:
        raise _unauthorized()
    try:
        claims = decode_token(creds.credentials, "access")
        principal = Principal(uuid.UUID(claims["sub"]), uuid.UUID(claims["tid"]),
                              Role(claims["role"]), claims["jti"], int(claims["exp"]))
    except (jwt.PyJWTError, ValueError, KeyError):
        raise _unauthorized("invalid_token")
    if await redis.exists(f"deny:{principal.jti}"):
        raise _unauthorized("token_revoked")
    return principal


async def get_db(principal: Principal = Depends(get_principal)):
    """Session liée au tenant du jeton : toutes les requêtes passent sous RLS."""
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        session.info["tenant_id"] = str(principal.tenant_id)
        yield session


async def get_public_db():
    """Session sans tenant (routes d'authentification). Le tenant est lié ensuite via bind_tenant."""
    async with SessionLocal() as session:
        yield session


async def get_current_user(principal: Principal = Depends(get_principal),
                           db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)) -> User:
    tenant = await db.get(Tenant, principal.tenant_id)
    if tenant is None or not tenant.is_active:
        raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, "tenant_inactive")
    user = await db.get(User, principal.user_id)
    if user is None or not user.is_active or user.tenant_id != principal.tenant_id:
        raise _unauthorized("user_inactive")
    return user


def require_roles(*roles: Role):
    async def _dep(user: User = Depends(get_current_user)) -> User:
        if user.role not in roles:
            raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, "insufficient_role")
        return user
    return _dep


async def verify_platform_key(x_platform_key: str = Header(default="")) -> None:
    expected = settings.PLATFORM_ADMIN_KEY.get_secret_value()
    if not x_platform_key or not hmac.compare_digest(x_platform_key, expected):
        raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, "forbidden")

################################################################################
# FICHIER: core/logging.py
################################################################################

import json
import logging
import sys

from app.core.config import settings


class JsonFormatter(logging.Formatter):
    def format(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> str:
        payload = {
            "ts": self.formatTime(record, "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z"),
            "level": record.levelname,
            "logger": record.name,
            "msg": record.getMessage(),
        }
        for key in ("request_id", "tenant_id", "task"):
            value = getattr(record, key, None)
            if value:
                payload[key] = value
        if record.exc_info:
            payload["exc"] = self.formatException(record.exc_info)
        return json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False)






def configure_logging() -> None:
    handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
    handler.setFormatter(JsonFormatter())
    root = logging.getLogger()
    root.handlers = [handler]
    root.setLevel(settings.LOG_LEVEL)
    for noisy in ("httpx", "httpcore"):          # ces loggers affichent l'URL, donc la clé TomTom
        logging.getLogger(noisy).setLevel(logging.WARNING)


################################################################################
# FICHIER: core/rate_limit.py
################################################################################

import time

from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request, status
from redis.asyncio import Redis

from app.core.deps import Principal, get_principal
from app.core.redis import get_redis
from app.core.security import hash_ip


async def _hit(redis: Redis, key: str, limit: int) -> None:
    window = int(time.time() // 60)
    full = f"{key}:{window}"
    pipe = redis.pipeline()
    pipe.incr(full)
    pipe.expire(full, 70)
    count, _ = await pipe.execute()
    if count > limit:
        raise HTTPException(status.HTTP_429_TOO_MANY_REQUESTS, "rate_limited",
                            headers={"Retry-After": str(60 - int(time.time() % 60))})


def limit_by_ip(name: str, per_minute: int):
    async def _dep(request: Request, redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)) -> None:
        ip = request.client.host if request.client else "unknown"
        await _hit(redis, f"rl:{name}:ip:{hash_ip(ip)}", per_minute)
    return _dep


def limit_by_user(name: str, per_minute: int):
    async def _dep(principal: Principal = Depends(get_principal), redis: Redis = Depends(get_redis)) -> None:
        await _hit(redis, f"rl:{name}:u:{principal.tenant_id}:{principal.user_id}", per_minute)
    return _dep


################################################################################
# FICHIER: core/redis.py
################################################################################

from redis.asyncio import Redis

from app.core.config import settings

_client: Redis | None = None


def new_redis() -> Redis:
    return Redis.from_url(settings.REDIS_URL, decode_responses=True)


def get_redis() -> Redis:
    global _client
    if _client is None:
        _client = new_redis()
    return _client


async def close_redis() -> None:
    global _client
    if _client is not None:
        await _client.aclose()
        _client = None

from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict


class RoadSettings(BaseSettings):
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_file=".env", env_file_encoding="utf-8", extra="ignore")

    # ---- Import du réseau (OpenStreetMap / Overpass)
    ROAD_AUTO_IMPORT: bool = True
    ROAD_OVERPASS_URLS: str = ("https://overpass-api.de/api/interpreter,"
                               "https://overpass.kumi.systems/api/interpreter,"
                               "https://overpass.private.coffee/api/interpreter")
    ROAD_OVERPASS_TIMEOUT_SECONDS: int = 180
    ROAD_HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECONDS: float = 200
    ROAD_COUNTRY_ISO: str = "SN"                       # "" = pas de filtre pays (bbox seule)
    ROAD_BBOX: str = "-17.60,12.25,-11.30,16.75"       # west,south,east,north
    ROAD_TILE_SIZE_DEG: float = 0.25
    ROAD_TILE_MIN_DEG: float = 0.0625
    ROAD_TILE_MAX_WAYS: int = 40000
    ROAD_TILE_SPACING_SECONDS: int = 10
    ROAD_TILE_MAX_ATTEMPTS: int = 5
    ROAD_STALE_MINUTES: int = 120
    ROAD_HIGHWAYS: str = ("motorway,motorway_link,trunk,trunk_link,primary,primary_link,secondary,secondary_link,"
                          "tertiary,tertiary_link,unclassified,residential,living_street")
    ROAD_SEGMENT_TARGET_M: float = 250
    ROAD_SEGMENT_MIN_M: float = 60
    ROAD_INSERT_CHUNK: int = 400

    # ---- Rattachement mesure -> tronçon
    ROAD_MATCH_MAX_M: float = 40
    ROAD_MATCH_BATCH: int = 5000

    # ---- Carte
    ROAD_BASELINE_DB: float = 0.0                      # niveau initial : calme (vert)
    ROAD_MAP_MAX_FEATURES: int = 6000
    ROAD_MAP_MAX_BBOX_DEG: float = 8.0
    ROAD_NETWORK_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS: int = 86400
    ROAD_NETWORK_CLIENT_MAX_AGE: int = 3600
    ROAD_STATE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS: int = 20

    # ---- Missions sur les routes
    ROAD_MISSIONS_ENABLED: bool = True
    ROAD_MISSION_MAX_RANK: int = 6                     # 1 = autoroutes/nationales ... 6 = résidentielles
    ROAD_MISSION_MAX_RADIUS_KM: float = 8
    ROAD_TARGET_RADIUS_M: float = 50                   # tolérance autour du point cible
    ROAD_MISSION_COUNT: int = 1
    ROAD_TARGET_PER_PART: int = 3                      # mesures visées par tronçon et par plage horaire
    ROAD_COVERAGE_WINDOW_DAYS: int = 7
    ROAD_PART_WEIGHTS: str = "morning:1.0,afternoon:0.9,evening:0.85,night:0.5"
    ROAD_LOOKAHEAD_HOURS: float = 6
    ROAD_MIN_WINDOW_MINUTES: int = 45
    ROAD_MIN_NEED: float = 0.25
    ROAD_MAX_NEW_PER_USER_PER_RUN: int = 2
    ROAD_SYNC_MAX_NEW: int = 2
    ROAD_MAX_PENDING_PER_USER: int = 4
    ROAD_MIN_SPACING_M: float = 400
    ROAD_REPEAT_COOLDOWN_HOURS: int = 24
    ROAD_REWARD_PER_MEASUREMENT: float = 0.8
    ROAD_CANDIDATE_LIMIT: int = 6000
    ROAD_MAX_USERS_PER_RUN: int = 400
    ROAD_GROUP_H3_RES: int = 7

    # ---- Tâches planifiées (secondes)
    ROAD_BEAT_MATCH_SECONDS: int = 120
    ROAD_BEAT_ENSURE_SECONDS: int = 21600

    @property
    def bbox(self) -> tuple[float, float, float, float]:
        w, s, e, n = (float(x) for x in self.ROAD_BBOX.split(","))
        return w, s, e, n

    @property
    def urls(self) -> list[str]:
        return [u.strip() for u in self.ROAD_OVERPASS_URLS.split(",") if u.strip()]

    @property
    def highways(self) -> list[str]:
        return [h.strip() for h in self.ROAD_HIGHWAYS.split(",") if h.strip()]

    @property
    def part_weights(self) -> dict[str, float]:
        out: dict[str, float] = {}
        for item in self.ROAD_PART_WEIGHTS.split(","):
            k, _, v = item.partition(":")
            if k.strip():
                out[k.strip()] = float(v or 1.0)
        return out


road_settings = RoadSettings()


################################################################################
# FICHIER: core/security.py
################################################################################

import hashlib
import hmac
import secrets
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import jwt
from argon2 import PasswordHasher
from argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError

from app.core.config import settings

_ph = PasswordHasher(time_cost=3, memory_cost=65536, parallelism=4)
_CODE_ALPHABET = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789"


def _hmac(secret: str, scope: str, value: str) -> str:
    return hmac.new(secret.encode(), f"{scope}:{value}".encode(), hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()


def _pepper() -> str:
    return settings.HASH_PEPPER.get_secret_value()


def _salt() -> str:
    return settings.PSEUDONYM_SALT.get_secret_value()


def _prehash(password: str) -> str:
    """Pré-hachage avec un secret serveur : une fuite de la base seule ne suffit plus à attaquer les mots de passe."""
    return hmac.new(_pepper().encode(), password.encode(), hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()


def hash_password(password: str) -> str:
    return _ph.hash(_prehash(password))


def verify_password_ex(password_hash: str, password: str) -> tuple[bool, bool]:
    """(valide, ancien_schéma). L'ancien schéma (Argon2 sans pepper) reste accepté puis est migré."""
    try:
        if _ph.verify(password_hash, _prehash(password)):
            return True, False
    except InvalidHashError:
        return False, False
    except VerificationError:
        pass
    try:
        if _ph.verify(password_hash, password):
            return True, True
    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):
        pass
    return False, False


def verify_password(password_hash: str, password: str) -> bool:
    return verify_password_ex(password_hash, password)[0]


def password_needs_rehash(password_hash: str) -> bool:
    return _ph.check_needs_rehash(password_hash)


def hash_email(tenant_id: uuid.UUID, email: str) -> str:
    return _hmac(_pepper(), f"email:{tenant_id}", email.strip().lower())


def hash_device(tenant_id: uuid.UUID, install_id: str) -> str:
    return _hmac(_pepper(), f"device:{tenant_id}", install_id.strip())


def hash_ip(ip: str) -> str:
    return _hmac(_pepper(), "ip", ip)


def make_pseudonym(tenant_id: uuid.UUID, user_id: uuid.UUID) -> str:
    return "Contrib-" + _hmac(_salt(), f"pseudo:{tenant_id}", str(user_id))[:10]


def contributor_token(tenant_id: uuid.UUID, user_id: uuid.UUID, period: str) -> str:
    """Jeton pseudonyme à rotation mensuelle, utilisé pour compter les contributeurs distincts."""
    return _hmac(_salt(), f"contrib:{tenant_id}:{period}", str(user_id))


def new_code(length: int) -> str:
    return "".join(secrets.choice(_CODE_ALPHABET) for _ in range(length))


def recovery_hash(user_id: uuid.UUID, code: str) -> str:
    return _hmac(_pepper(), f"recovery:{user_id}", code.strip().upper().replace("-", "").replace(" ", ""))


def create_token(token_type: str, user_id: uuid.UUID, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, role: str) -> tuple[str, str, int]:
    ttl = (timedelta(minutes=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES) if token_type == "access"
           else timedelta(days=settings.REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS))
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    jti = uuid.uuid4().hex
    claims = {"sub": str(user_id), "tid": str(tenant_id), "role": role, "typ": token_type,
              "jti": jti, "iat": now, "exp": now + ttl}
    token = jwt.encode(claims, settings.JWT_SECRET_KEY.get_secret_value(), algorithm=settings.JWT_ALGORITHM)
    return token, jti, int(ttl.total_seconds())


def decode_token(token: str, expected_type: str) -> dict:
    claims = jwt.decode(token, settings.JWT_SECRET_KEY.get_secret_value(),
                        algorithms=[settings.JWT_ALGORITHM],
                        options={"require": ["exp", "sub", "tid", "jti", "typ"]})
    if claims.get("typ") != expected_type:
        raise jwt.InvalidTokenError("type de jeton invalide")
    return claims

from pydantic import model_validator
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict


class SurveySettings(BaseSettings):
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_file=".env", env_file_encoding="utf-8", extra="ignore")

    # Enquêtes
    SV_MAX_QUESTIONS: int = 60
    SV_MAX_SLOTS: int = 8
    SV_COOLDOWN_DEFAULT_DAYS: int = 30
    SV_MAX_PER_DAY: int = 30
    SV_TARGET_PER_CELL: int = 10
    SV_ESTIMATED_SECONDS_PER_QUESTION: float = 6.0

    # Profils
    SV_PROFILES_PER_SLOT: int = 10
    SV_PROFILES_MIN: int = 4
    SV_PROFILES_MAX: int = 16
    SV_SHOWN_MAX: int = 12
    SV_EVOLVE_MIN_NEW: int = 30
    SV_EVOLVE_INTERVAL_HOURS: int = 24
    SV_RETIRE_MIN_SHOWN: int = 60
    SV_RETIRE_RELATIVE: float = 0.25           # part de choix < 25 % de la part uniforme
    SV_CLUSTER_MIN: int = 40
    SV_CLUSTER_K: int = 4
    SV_NONE_RATE_TRIGGER: float = 0.15
    SV_LOCAL_DAYS: int = 90
    SV_NEW_PROFILE_MIN_DIST: float = 0.35      # distance minimale (normalisée) à un profil existant

    # Qualité : poids (somme = 1)
    SQ_W_CONSISTENCY: float = 0.15
    SQ_W_TIMING: float = 0.15
    SQ_W_EFFORT: float = 0.10
    SQ_W_LOCATION: float = 0.10
    SQ_W_PEERS: float = 0.15
    SQ_W_REFERENCE: float = 0.05
    SQ_W_EVIDENCE: float = 0.10
    SQ_W_REPUTATION: float = 0.10
    SQ_W_BEHAVIOR: float = 0.10
    SQ_VALIDATED_MIN: float = 0.72
    SQ_INDICATIVE_MIN: float = 0.45
    SQ_BASE_SECONDS: float = 6.0
    SQ_SECONDS_PER_SLOT: float = 4.0
    SQ_SECONDS_PER_OVERRIDE: float = 3.0
    SQ_SECONDS_PER_DIRECT: float = 4.0
    SQ_TOO_FAST_HARD: float = 0.15
    SQ_GPS_GOOD_M: float = 30
    SQ_GPS_REJECT_M: float = 150
    SQ_SPEED_WARN_KMH: float = 120
    SQ_SPEED_REJECT_KMH: float = 500
    SQ_PEER_MIN_SAMPLES: int = 8
    SQ_PEER_Z_MAX: float = 4.0
    SQ_PEER_WINDOW_DAYS: int = 120
    SQ_PEER_RING_K: int = 1
    SQ_REF_FACTOR: float = 6.0
    SQ_FINGERPRINT_WINDOW_HOURS: int = 24
    SQ_FINGERPRINT_MAX: int = 3
    SQ_STRAIGHT_MIN_SCALE: int = 5
    SQ_REEVAL_AFTER_HOURS: int = 6

    # Preuves photo
    SV_EVIDENCE_MAX_BYTES: int = 900_000
    SV_EVIDENCE_BODY_BYTES: int = 1_100_000
    SV_EVIDENCE_PER_RESPONSE: int = 6
    SV_EVIDENCE_WINDOW_MINUTES: int = 30
    SV_EVIDENCE_MAX_AGE_HOURS: int = 36
    SV_EVIDENCE_RETENTION_DAYS: int = 90
    RATE_LIMIT_EVIDENCE_PER_MINUTE: int = 20

    # Confiance du répondant
    ST_PRIOR: float = 0.6
    ST_STRENGTH: float = 5
    ST_WINDOW_DAYS: int = 90

    # Carte des données
    SM_MIN_CONTRIBUTORS: int = 5
    SM_MIN_CONTRIBUTORS_SENSITIVE: int = 10
    SM_MIN_CONTRIBUTORS_FACILITY: int = 3
    SM_DEFAULT_RES: int = 8
    SM_MAX_FEATURES: int = 3000
    SM_MAX_ROWS: int = 100_000
    SM_TARGET_N: int = 20
    SM_TARGET_CONTRIBUTORS: int = 8
    SM_RECENCY_TAU_DAYS: float = 60
    SM_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS: int = 30

    # Tâches (secondes)
    BEAT_SURVEY_SWEEP_SECONDS: int = 120
    BEAT_SURVEY_REEVAL_SECONDS: int = 3600
    BEAT_SURVEY_EVOLVE_SECONDS: int = 3600
    BEAT_SURVEY_TRUST_SECONDS: int = 21600
    BEAT_SURVEY_PRIVACY_SECONDS: int = 86400

    # IA
    GROQ_MODEL_SURVEY: str = ""

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _validate(self):
        w = (self.SQ_W_CONSISTENCY + self.SQ_W_TIMING + self.SQ_W_EFFORT + self.SQ_W_LOCATION + self.SQ_W_PEERS
             + self.SQ_W_REFERENCE + self.SQ_W_EVIDENCE + self.SQ_W_REPUTATION + self.SQ_W_BEHAVIOR)
        if abs(w - 1.0) > 1e-6:
            raise ValueError("Les poids SQ_W_* doivent totaliser 1.0")
        if self.SQ_INDICATIVE_MIN > self.SQ_VALIDATED_MIN:
            raise ValueError("SQ_INDICATIVE_MIN doit être <= SQ_VALIDATED_MIN")
        if not (5 <= self.SM_DEFAULT_RES <= 9):
            raise ValueError("SM_DEFAULT_RES doit être entre 5 et 9")
        return self


survey_settings = SurveySettings()

################################################################################
# FICHIER: db/base.py

from sqlalchemy import MetaData
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase

NAMING = {
    "ix": "ix_%(column_0_label)s",
    "uq": "uq_%(table_name)s_%(column_0_name)s",
    "ck": "ck_%(table_name)s_%(constraint_name)s",
    "fk": "fk_%(table_name)s_%(column_0_name)s_%(referred_table_name)s",
    "pk": "pk_%(table_name)s",
}


class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    metadata = MetaData(naming_convention=NAMING)


################################################################################
# FICHIER: db/session.py
################################################################################

import uuid

from sqlalchemy import event, text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker, create_async_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import NullPool

from app.core.config import settings

_SET_TENANT = text("SELECT set_config('app.tenant_id', :tid, true)")

engine = create_async_engine(
    settings.DATABASE_URL,
    pool_size=settings.DB_POOL_SIZE,
    max_overflow=settings.DB_MAX_OVERFLOW,
    pool_pre_ping=True,
    hide_parameters=True,
)
SessionLocal = async_sessionmaker(engine, expire_on_commit=False)


@event.listens_for(Session, "after_begin")
def _apply_tenant_context(session, transaction, connection):
    """Chaque transaction démarre avec le tenant de la session (échec fermé si absent : RLS ne renvoie rien)."""
    connection.execute(_SET_TENANT, {"tid": session.info.get("tenant_id") or ""})


async def bind_tenant(session: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID | str) -> None:
    session.info["tenant_id"] = str(tenant_id)
    await session.execute(_SET_TENANT, {"tid": str(tenant_id)})


def task_session_factory():
    """Moteur dédié aux tâches Celery (une boucle asyncio par tâche, donc sans pool)."""
    task_engine = create_async_engine(settings.DATABASE_URL, poolclass=NullPool, hide_parameters=True)
    return task_engine, async_sessionmaker(task_engine, expire_on_commit=False)

# FICHIER: main.py
################################################################################

import logging
import uuid
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.middleware.trustedhost import TrustedHostMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from prometheus_fastapi_instrumentator import Instrumentator

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.logging import configure_logging
from app.core.redis import close_redis
from app.core.road_settings import road_settings
from app.db.session import engine
from app.routers import (account, admin, ai, auth, collection, datamap, devices, guide, health, leaderboard,
                         map as map_router, measurements, missions, platform, quota, reports, reviews, roads,
                         statistics, survey_admin, surveys, tomtom, users, validation)
from app.core.survey_settings import survey_settings as sv

from app.services.tomtom import close_tomtom
from app.tasks.celery_app import celery_app

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    configure_logging()
    if road_settings.ROAD_AUTO_IMPORT:
        try:                                     # import initial du réseau routier (idempotent, verrouillé côté worker)
            celery_app.send_task("roads.ensure_network", countdown=20)
        except Exception:
            log.exception("roads_bootstrap_failed")
    yield
    await close_tomtom()
    await close_redis()
    await engine.dispose()


def create_app() -> FastAPI:
    prod = settings.APP_ENV == "production"
    app = FastAPI(
        title=settings.APP_NAME,
        version="1.0.0",
        lifespan=lifespan,
        docs_url=None if prod else "/docs",
        redoc_url=None,
        openapi_url=None if prod else "/openapi.json",
    )

    app.add_middleware(TrustedHostMiddleware, allowed_hosts=settings.allowed_hosts_list)

    extra_origins = [
        "https://soundmaps.rhmanager.site",
        "http://localhost:8093",
        "http://localhost:8097",
        "http://localhost:8000",
        "http://0.0.0.0:8093",
        "http://127.0.0.1:8093",
        "http://localhost:5173",
    ]
    allowed_origins = list({*settings.cors_origins_list, *extra_origins})

    if allowed_origins:
        app.add_middleware(
            CORSMiddleware,
            allow_origins=allowed_origins,
            allow_credentials=False,
            allow_methods=["GET", "POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE"],
            allow_headers=["Authorization", "Content-Type", "If-None-Match"],
            expose_headers=["ETag"],
        )

    @app.middleware("http")
    async def secure(request: Request, call_next):
        length = request.headers.get("content-length")
        if length and length.isdigit() and int(length) > settings.MAX_BODY_BYTES:
            return JSONResponse({"detail": "payload_too_large"}, status_code=413)
        request_id = request.headers.get("x-request-id") or uuid.uuid4().hex
        response = await call_next(request)
        response.headers["X-Request-ID"] = request_id
        response.headers["X-Content-Type-Options"] = "nosniff"
        response.headers["Referrer-Policy"] = "no-referrer"
        response.headers.setdefault("Cache-Control", "no-store")     # une route peut fixer sa propre politique
        if prod:
            response.headers["Strict-Transport-Security"] = "max-age=63072000; includeSubDomains"
        return response

    prefix = settings.API_V1_PREFIX
    app.include_router(health.router)
    for r in (platform, auth, users, devices, collection, measurements, map_router, statistics, admin, ai, tomtom,
              reports, quota, missions, validation, account, leaderboard, reviews, roads, guide,
              surveys, survey_admin, datamap):
        app.include_router(r.router, prefix=prefix)
    app.include_router(roads.platform_router, prefix=prefix)

    Instrumentator().instrument(app)
    return app


app = create_app()

class CollectionSchedule(Base, IdMixin, TenantMixin, CreatedMixin):
    __tablename__ = "collection_schedules"
    user_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), unique=True)
    days_of_week: Mapped[list[int]] = mapped_column(ARRAY(SmallInteger))     # 0 = lundi
    window_start: Mapped[time] = mapped_column(Time)
    window_end: Mapped[time] = mapped_column(Time)
    preferred_times: Mapped[list[time]] = mapped_column(ARRAY(Time), default=list, server_default=text("'{}'"))
    mode: Mapped[ScheduleMode] = mapped_column(pg_enum(ScheduleMode), default=ScheduleMode.both)
    timezone: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64))
    active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, server_default=text("true"))

    __tablename__ = "collection_proposals"

    cell_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid, ForeignKey("geographic_cells.id", ondelete="CASCADE"))
    h3_index: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))
    trigger: Mapped[TriggerMode] = mapped_column(pg_enum(TriggerMode))
    status: Mapped[ProposalStatus] = mapped_column(pg_enum(ProposalStatus), default=ProposalStatus.pending)
    priority_score: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    decision_score: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    is_exploration: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, server_default=text("false"))
    features: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    weights_used: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    confidence_before: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    expires_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    responded_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    completed_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    learned_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("ix_proposals_user_created", "user_id", "created_at"),
        Index("ix_proposals_status_expires", "tenant_id", "status", "expires_at"),
    )

import uuid
from datetime import date, datetime

from geoalchemy2 import Geometry
from sqlalchemy import (Boolean, CheckConstraint, Date, DateTime, Float, ForeignKey, Index, Integer, String,
                        UniqueConstraint, Uuid, text)
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import DOUBLE_PRECISION, JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.db.base import Base
from app.models.enums import CalibrationStatus, DayPart, NoiseContext, QualityStatus
from app.models.mixins import CreatedMixin, IdMixin, TenantMixin, pg_enum


class Measurement(Base, IdMixin, TenantMixin, CreatedMixin):
    __tablename__ = "measurements"
    user_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID | None] = mapped_column(Uuid, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), index=True)
    device_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID | None] = mapped_column(Uuid, ForeignKey("devices.id", ondelete="SET NULL"))
    device_model_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID | None] = mapped_column(
        Uuid, ForeignKey("device_models.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), index=True)
    proposal_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID | None] = mapped_column(
        Uuid, ForeignKey("collection_proposals.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), unique=True)
    cell_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid, ForeignKey("geographic_cells.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"))
    client_measurement_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid)
    contributor_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)

    measured_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    local_date: Mapped[date] = mapped_column(Date)
    day_part: Mapped[DayPart] = mapped_column(pg_enum(DayPart))

    latitude: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(DOUBLE_PRECISION)
    longitude: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(DOUBLE_PRECISION)
    geom = mapped_column(Geometry("POINT", srid=4326), nullable=False)
    h3_index: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))
    gps_accuracy: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    location_anonymized: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, server_default=text("false"))

    # Rattachement au réseau routier (renseigné par la tâche roads.match)
    road_segment_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID | None] = mapped_column(
        Uuid, ForeignKey("road_segments.id", ondelete="SET NULL"))
    road_distance_m: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float)
    road_matched_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))

    duration_seconds: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    effective_duration_seconds: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    sample_rate_hz: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer)
    laeq: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    lmax: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    lmin: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    saturation_ratio: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    moved_distance_m: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    noise_context: Mapped[NoiseContext | None] = mapped_column(pg_enum(NoiseContext))

    correction_db_applied: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.0, server_default=text("0"))
    calibration_status: Mapped[CalibrationStatus] = mapped_column(
        pg_enum(CalibrationStatus), default=CalibrationStatus.uncalibrated)
    quality_status: Mapped[QualityStatus] = mapped_column(pg_enum(QualityStatus), default=QualityStatus.pending)
    audio_retained: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, server_default=text("false"))
    aggregated_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))

    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("tenant_id", "user_id", "client_measurement_id", name="uq_measurement_client_id"),
        CheckConstraint("audio_retained = false", name="no_raw_audio"),
        Index("ix_meas_tenant_status_time", "tenant_id", "quality_status", "measured_at"),
        Index("ix_meas_tenant_h3_time", "tenant_id", "h3_index", "measured_at"),
        Index("ix_meas_tenant_aggregated", "tenant_id", "aggregated_at"),
        Index("ix_meas_tenant_road_time", "tenant_id", "road_segment_id", "measured_at"),
        Index("ix_meas_tenant_road_matched", "tenant_id", "road_matched_at"),
    )

    override_by: Mapped[uuid.UUID | None] = mapped_column(Uuid)

################################################################################
# FICHIER: models/mixins.py
################################################################################

import uuid
from datetime import datetime

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Enum as SAEnum, ForeignKey, Uuid, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column


def pg_enum(enum_cls):
    return SAEnum(enum_cls, native_enum=False, length=24, validate_strings=True,
                  values_callable=lambda e: [m.value for m in e])


class IdMixin:
    id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid, primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)


class TenantMixin:
    tenant_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
        Uuid, ForeignKey("tenants.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False, index=True)


class CreatedMixin:
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), nullable=False)

# FICHIER: models/quota.py
################################################################################

import uuid
from datetime import date, datetime

from sqlalchemy import Date, DateTime, Float, ForeignKey, Index, Integer, String, UniqueConstraint, Uuid, func, text
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.db.base import Base
from app.models.enums import DayPart, MissionStatus
from app.models.mixins import CreatedMixin, IdMixin, TenantMixin, pg_enum


class UserQuota(Base, TenantMixin):
    """Solde cumulable de prélèvements d'un utilisateur (crédits décimaux)."""
    __tablename__ = "user_quotas"
    user_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), primary_key=True)
    balance: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.0, server_default=text("0"))
    daily_target: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.0, server_default=text("0"))
    rate_per_hour: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.0, server_default=text("0"))
    reliability: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.6, server_default=text("0.6"))
    utilization: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.7, server_default=text("0.7"))
    mission_rate: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.5, server_default=text("0.5"))
    retention_per_day: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.8, server_default=text("0.8"))
    cap: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=6.0, server_default=text("6"))
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    __table_args__ = (Index("ix_user_quotas_updated", "tenant_id", "updated_at"),)


class QuotaDay(Base, IdMixin, TenantMixin):
    """Agrégat journalier (crédité / utilisé / perdu) : sert au calcul du taux d'utilisation."""
    __tablename__ = "quota_days"
    user_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"))
    local_date: Mapped[date] = mapped_column(Date)
    credited: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.0, server_default=text("0"))
    used: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.0, server_default=text("0"))
    decayed: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.0, server_default=text("0"))
    measurements: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    target: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.0, server_default=text("0"))
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("user_id", "local_date", name="uq_quota_days_user_date"),)


class Mission(Base, IdMixin, TenantMixin, CreatedMixin):
    __tablename__ = "missions"
    user_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"))
    kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), default="zone", server_default=text("'zone'"))   # zone | road
    status: Mapped[MissionStatus] = mapped_column(pg_enum(MissionStatus), default=MissionStatus.offered)
    zone_h3: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))
    center_lat: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)       # mission route : point cible précis
    center_lon: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    radius_m: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)         # mission route : tolérance autour du point cible
    distance_km: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.0, server_default=text("0"))
    required_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    done_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    confirmed_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    reward_credits: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.0, server_default=text("0"))
    priority: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.0, server_default=text("0"))
    features: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(JSONB, default=dict, server_default=text("'{}'::jsonb"))
    road_segment_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID | None] = mapped_column(
        Uuid, ForeignKey("road_segments.id", ondelete="SET NULL"))
    road_name: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(160))
    road_class: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(24))
    target_day_part: Mapped[DayPart | None] = mapped_column(pg_enum(DayPart))
    window_start_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    window_end_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    offer_expires_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    accepted_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    deadline_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    finished_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("ix_missions_user_status", "user_id", "status"),
        Index("ix_missions_tenant_status_expires", "tenant_id", "status", "offer_expires_at"),
        Index("ix_missions_tenant_kind_status", "tenant_id", "kind", "status"),
    )


################################################################################
# FICHIER: models/roads.py
################################################################################

import uuid
from datetime import datetime

from geoalchemy2 import Geometry
from sqlalchemy import (BigInteger, Boolean, DateTime, Float, Index, Integer, SmallInteger, String,
                        UniqueConstraint, func, text)
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import DOUBLE_PRECISION
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.db.base import Base
from app.models.mixins import IdMixin


class RoadSegment(Base, IdMixin):
    """Tronçon de route réel (OpenStreetMap). Donnée géographique publique, partagée par tous les tenants
    (sans tenant_id ni RLS) : l'état de bruit par tenant est calculé à partir des mesures."""
    __tablename__ = "road_segments"
    osm_way_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    seq: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    start_node: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger)          # nœuds OSM : conservent la connectivité
    end_node: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    highway: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24))
    road_rank: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)          # 1 = autoroute/nationale ... 6 = résidentielle
    class_weight: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)            # poids de trafic attendu (0-1)
    name: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(160))
    ref: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(60))
    oneway: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, server_default=text("false"))
    maxspeed: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(20))
    lanes: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    length_m: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    mid_lat: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(DOUBLE_PRECISION)      # point cible des missions
    mid_lon: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(DOUBLE_PRECISION)
    h3_index: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))
    geom = mapped_column(Geometry("LINESTRING", srid=4326), nullable=False)
    active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, server_default=text("true"))
    imported_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("osm_way_id", "seq", name="uq_road_segments_way_seq"),
        Index("ix_road_segments_start_node", "start_node"),
        Index("ix_road_segments_end_node", "end_node"),
        Index("ix_road_segments_rank", "road_rank"),
        Index("ix_road_segments_h3", "h3_index"),
    )


class RoadImportTile(Base):
    """Suivi de l'import par tuiles (reprise possible)."""
    __tablename__ = "road_import_tiles"
    key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), primary_key=True)
    west: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(DOUBLE_PRECISION)
    south: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(DOUBLE_PRECISION)
    east: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(DOUBLE_PRECISION)
    north: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(DOUBLE_PRECISION)
    size_deg: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(10), default="pending")   # pending|queued|running|done|split|failed
    ways: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    segments: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    attempts: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    error: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(200))
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    finished_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    __table_args__ = (Index("ix_road_tiles_status", "status"),)

import uuid
from datetime import date, datetime

from sqlalchemy import (Boolean, Date, DateTime, Float, ForeignKey, Index, Integer, SmallInteger, String,
                        UniqueConstraint, Uuid, func, text)
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.db.base import Base
from app.models.mixins import CreatedMixin, IdMixin, TenantMixin


def _user_fk(nullable: bool = False, set_null: bool = False):
    return mapped_column(Uuid, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="SET NULL" if set_null else "CASCADE"),
                         nullable=nullable)


class UserProfile(Base, TenantMixin):
    """Profil enrichi : données privées (nom, téléphone…) jamais exposées aux autres utilisateurs."""
    __tablename__ = "user_profiles"
    user_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), primary_key=True)
    display_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32))
    display_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64))
    first_name: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(60))
    last_name: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(60))
    phone: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(24))
    city: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80))
    district: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80))
    birth_year: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(SmallInteger)
    invited_by: Mapped[uuid.UUID | None] = _user_fk(nullable=True, set_null=True)
    invitation_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID | None] = mapped_column(Uuid)
    invite_credited_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    terms_accepted_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    show_in_leaderboard: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, server_default=text("true"))
    analyst_enabled: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, server_default=text("false"))
    analyst_since: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    analyst_suspended_until: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    xp: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    xp_through: Mapped[date | None] = mapped_column(Date)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("tenant_id", "display_key", name="uq_profiles_tenant_display"),
                      Index("ix_profiles_invited_by", "invited_by"))


class Invitation(Base, IdMixin, TenantMixin, CreatedMixin):

    max_uses: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1)
    uses: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    expires_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, server_default=text("true"))

                      Index("ix_lb_entry_rank", "tenant_id", "period_kind", "period_key", "rank"))

    """Une manche d'avis en temps réel sur une mesure."""

                                                      unique=True)

    outcome: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(20))               # confirmed | adjusted_up | adjusted_down | rejected | no_quorum | admin_skip
    adjust_db: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.0, server_default=text("0"))
    requested: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    votes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    agree_ratio: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float)
    down_ratio: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float)
    up_ratio: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float)
    expires_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    closed_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))

    review_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid, ForeignKey("measurement_reviews.id", ondelete="CASCADE"))

    level_db: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    measured_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    distance_m: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    weight: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    matched: Mapped[bool | None] = mapped_column(Boolean)
    expires_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    voted_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("review_id", "reviewer_id", name="uq_review_vote"),
                      Index("ix_review_votes_reviewer", "reviewer_id", "status"))

# FICHIER: models/survey.py

import uuid
from datetime import date, datetime

from sqlalchemy import (Boolean, Date, DateTime, Float, ForeignKey, Index, Integer, LargeBinary, SmallInteger,
                        String, Text, UniqueConstraint, Uuid, func, text)
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.db.base import Base
from app.models.enums import (EvidenceStatus, ProfileStatus, QualityStatus, SurveyDomain, SurveyStatus)
from app.models.mixins import CreatedMixin, IdMixin, TenantMixin, pg_enum


class Survey(Base, IdMixin, TenantMixin, CreatedMixin):
    __tablename__ = "surveys"
    created_by: Mapped[uuid.UUID | None] = mapped_column(Uuid)
    domain: Mapped[SurveyDomain] = mapped_column(pg_enum(SurveyDomain))
    title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160))
    description: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text)
    status: Mapped[SurveyStatus] = mapped_column(pg_enum(SurveyStatus), default=SurveyStatus.draft)
    unit: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="household")
    current_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    geo_scope: Mapped[dict | None] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    cooldown_days: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=30, server_default=text("30"))
    starts_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    ends_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    target_responses: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    allow_sensitive_axes: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, server_default=text("false"))
    ai_generated: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, server_default=text("false"))
    profile_generation: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    last_evolved_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    responses_at_evolution: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    __table_args__ = (Index("ix_surveys_tenant_status", "tenant_id", "status"),)


class SurveyVersion(Base, IdMixin, TenantMixin, CreatedMixin):

    definition: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("survey_id", "version", name="uq_survey_versions"),)


class SurveyProfile(Base, IdMixin, TenantMixin, CreatedMixin):
    """Profil-réponse : un ensemble de réponses cohérentes qu'un répondant peut reconnaître comme les siennes."""
    __tablename__ = "survey_profiles"
    survey_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid, ForeignKey("surveys.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), index=True)
    slot: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(48))
    key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))
    label: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120))
    description: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(300))
    axes: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(JSONB, default=dict)
    answers: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    origin: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(12))           # deterministic | cluster | ai | admin
    status: Mapped[ProfileStatus] = mapped_column(pg_enum(ProfileStatus), default=ProfileStatus.active)
    generation: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    shown_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    picked_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    override_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    meta: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(JSONB, default=dict)
    retired_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("survey_id", "key", name="uq_survey_profile_key"),
                      Index("ix_survey_profiles_slot", "survey_id", "slot", "status"))
