import uuid
from datetime import date, datetime, time, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import delete, func, select, update
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import insert as pg_insert
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.account_settings import account_settings as acs
from app.models.enums import MissionStatus, QualityStatus
from app.models.measurement import Measurement
from app.models.quota import Mission
from app.models.social import LeaderboardEntry, LeaderboardSnapshot, ReviewVote, UserBadge, UserProfile
from app.models.tenancy import User
from app.services.geo import TZ, to_local

PERIODS = ("week", "month", "season")
LABELS = {"week": "Semaine", "month": "Mois", "season": "Saison (90 j)"}
LEVELS = [(0, "Écouteur"), (100, "Mesureur"), (300, "Observateur"), (700, "Cartographe"),
          (1500, "Expert"), (3000, "Maître du son"), (6000, "Légende")]
BADGES = {  # code: (libellé, description, icône, palier)
    "first_measure": ("Premier relevé", "Une première mesure validée", "flag", 1),
    "measures_50": ("Collecteur", "50 mesures validées sur la saison", "bolt", 2),
    "measures_200": ("Bourreau de travail", "200 mesures validées sur la saison", "bolt", 4),
    "reliable": ("Fiable", "Fiabilité d'au moins 85 % sur 20 mesures", "verified", 3),
    "explorer": ("Explorateur", "25 zones couvertes sur la saison", "explore", 3),
    "regular": ("Régulier", "20 jours d'activité sur la saison", "event", 3),
    "missions_5": ("Missionnaire", "5 missions réussies sur la saison", "flag", 3),
    "sponsor": ("Parrain", "3 filleuls actifs sur la saison", "group", 3),
    "analyst": ("Oreille experte", "20 avis concordants sur la saison", "hearing", 4),
    "podium": ("Podium", "Dans le top 3 de la semaine", "trophy", 5),
}


def level_info(xp: int) -> dict:
    idx = max(i for i, (t, _) in enumerate(LEVELS) if xp >= t)
    nxt = LEVELS[idx + 1][0] if idx + 1 < len(LEVELS) else None
    prog = 1.0 if nxt is None else (xp - LEVELS[idx][0]) / (nxt - LEVELS[idx][0])
    return {"index": idx, "name": LEVELS[idx][1], "xp": xp, "next_xp": nxt, "progress": round(prog, 3)}


def bounds(kind: str, now: datetime) -> tuple[datetime, datetime, str]:
    local = to_local(now)
    if kind == "week":
        d = local.date() - timedelta(days=local.weekday())
        y, w, _ = d.isocalendar()
        key = f"{y}-W{w:02d}"
    elif kind == "month":
        d = local.date().replace(day=1)
        key = f"{d:%Y-%m}"
    else:
        d = local.date() - timedelta(days=89)
        key = "season"
    return datetime.combine(d, time.min, TZ).astimezone(timezone.utc), now, key


async def points_for_window(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, start: datetime, end: datetime) -> dict:
    out: dict = {}

    def c(uid):
        return out.setdefault(uid, {"v": 0, "i": 0, "r": 0, "days": 0, "cells": 0, "missions": 0, "units": 0,
                                    "reviews": 0, "invites": 0})
    base = [Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.user_id.is_not(None),
            Measurement.measured_at >= start, Measurement.measured_at < end]
    keymap = {QualityStatus.validated: "v", QualityStatus.indicative: "i", QualityStatus.rejected: "r"}
    for uid, st, n in (await db.execute(select(Measurement.user_id, Measurement.quality_status, func.count())
                                        .where(*base, Measurement.quality_status != QualityStatus.pending)
                                        .group_by(Measurement.user_id, Measurement.quality_status))).all():
        c(uid)[keymap[st]] += int(n)
    for uid, days, cells in (await db.execute(
            select(Measurement.user_id, func.count(func.distinct(Measurement.local_date)),
                   func.count(func.distinct(Measurement.h3_index)))
            .where(*base, Measurement.quality_status.in_([QualityStatus.validated, QualityStatus.indicative]))
            .group_by(Measurement.user_id))).all():
        c(uid)["days"], c(uid)["cells"] = int(days), int(cells)
    for uid, n, units in (await db.execute(
            select(Mission.user_id, func.count(), func.coalesce(func.sum(Mission.required_count), 0))
            .where(Mission.tenant_id == tenant_id, Mission.status == MissionStatus.completed,
                   Mission.finished_at >= start, Mission.finished_at < end).group_by(Mission.user_id))).all():
        c(uid)["missions"], c(uid)["units"] = int(n), int(units)
    for uid, n in (await db.execute(
            select(ReviewVote.reviewer_id, func.count()).where(
                ReviewVote.tenant_id == tenant_id, ReviewVote.matched.is_(True),
                ReviewVote.voted_at >= start, ReviewVote.voted_at < end).group_by(ReviewVote.reviewer_id))).all():
        c(uid)["reviews"] = int(n)
    for uid, n in (await db.execute(
            select(UserProfile.invited_by, func.count()).where(
                UserProfile.tenant_id == tenant_id, UserProfile.invited_by.is_not(None),
                UserProfile.invite_credited_at >= start, UserProfile.invite_credited_at < end)
            .group_by(UserProfile.invited_by))).all():
        c(uid)["invites"] = int(n)

    s, p0 = acs.LB_RELIABILITY_STRENGTH, acs.LB_RELIABILITY_PRIOR
    for d in out.values():
        n = d["v"] + d["i"] + d["r"]
        d["reliability"] = (d["v"] + 0.5 * d["i"] + s * p0) / (n + s)
        d["raw"] = (d["v"] * acs.LB_PTS_VALIDATED + d["i"] * acs.LB_PTS_INDICATIVE + d["r"] * acs.LB_PTS_REJECTED
                    + d["cells"] * acs.LB_PTS_CELL + d["days"] * acs.LB_PTS_DAY + d["missions"] * acs.LB_PTS_MISSION
                    + d["units"] * acs.LB_PTS_MISSION_UNIT + d["reviews"] * acs.LB_PTS_REVIEW
                    + d["invites"] * acs.LB_PTS_INVITE)
        d["score"] = round(max(0.0, d["raw"]) * (0.6 + 0.8 * d["reliability"]), 1)
    return out


async def credit_invites(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, now: datetime) -> None:
    rows = (await db.execute(select(UserProfile.user_id).where(
        UserProfile.tenant_id == tenant_id, UserProfile.invited_by.is_not(None),
        UserProfile.invite_credited_at.is_(None)).limit(2000))).scalars().all()
    if not rows:
        return
    counts = dict((await db.execute(select(Measurement.user_id, func.count()).where(
        Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.user_id.in_(rows),
        Measurement.quality_status == QualityStatus.validated).group_by(Measurement.user_id))).all())
    ok = [u for u in rows if counts.get(u, 0) >= 3]
    if ok:
        await db.execute(update(UserProfile).where(UserProfile.user_id.in_(ok)).values(invite_credited_at=now))


async def refresh_tenant(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    await credit_invites(db, tenant_id, now)
    visible = dict((await db.execute(select(UserProfile.user_id, UserProfile.show_in_leaderboard).where(
        UserProfile.tenant_id == tenant_id))).all())
    season = {}
    week_ranks: dict = {}
    total = 0
    for kind in PERIODS:
        start, end, key = bounds(kind, now)
        comps = await points_for_window(db, tenant_id, start, now)
        ranked = sorted(((u, d) for u, d in comps.items() if d["score"] > 0),
                        key=lambda t: (-t[1]["score"], -t[1]["v"], str(t[0])))
        rows, rank = [], 0
        for uid, d in ranked:
            shown = visible.get(uid, True)
            rank += 1 if shown else 0
            rows.append({"id": uuid.uuid4(), "tenant_id": tenant_id, "period_kind": kind, "period_key": key,
                         "user_id": uid, "score": d["score"], "rank": rank if shown else 0,
                         "reliability": round(d["reliability"], 4),
                         "components": {k: (round(v, 2) if isinstance(v, float) else v) for k, v in d.items()},
                         "updated_at": now})
        await db.execute(delete(LeaderboardEntry).where(
            LeaderboardEntry.tenant_id == tenant_id, LeaderboardEntry.period_kind == kind,
            LeaderboardEntry.period_key == key))
        for i in range(0, len(rows), 1000):
            await db.execute(pg_insert(LeaderboardEntry).values(rows[i:i + 1000]))
        total += len(rows)
        if kind == "season":
            season = comps
        if kind == "week":
            week_ranks = {r["user_id"]: r["rank"] for r in rows if r["rank"] > 0}
        await _snapshot(db, tenant_id, kind, rows, now)
    await _award_badges(db, tenant_id, season, week_ranks)
    return total


async def _snapshot(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id, kind: str, rows: list, now: datetime) -> None:
    today = to_local(now).date()
    snaps = [{"id": uuid.uuid4(), "tenant_id": tenant_id, "user_id": r["user_id"], "period_kind": kind,
              "snap_date": today, "rank": r["rank"], "score": r["score"]} for r in rows if r["rank"] > 0]
    for i in range(0, len(snaps), 1000):
        await db.execute(pg_insert(LeaderboardSnapshot).values(snaps[i:i + 1000]).on_conflict_do_update(
            constraint="uq_lb_snapshot", set_={"rank": pg_insert(LeaderboardSnapshot).excluded.rank,
                                               "score": pg_insert(LeaderboardSnapshot).excluded.score}))


async def _award_badges(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id, comps: dict, week_ranks: dict) -> None:
    earned = []
    for uid, d in comps.items():
        n = d["v"] + d["i"] + d["r"]
        codes = []
        if d["v"] >= 1: codes.append("first_measure")
        if d["v"] >= 50: codes.append("measures_50")
        if d["v"] >= 200: codes.append("measures_200")
        if n >= 20 and d["reliability"] >= 0.85: codes.append("reliable")
        if d["cells"] >= 25: codes.append("explorer")
        if d["days"] >= 20: codes.append("regular")
        if d["missions"] >= 5: codes.append("missions_5")
        if d["invites"] >= 3: codes.append("sponsor")
        if d["reviews"] >= 20: codes.append("analyst")
        earned += [(uid, c) for c in codes]
    earned += [(uid, "podium") for uid, r in week_ranks.items() if r <= 3]
    rows = [{"id": uuid.uuid4(), "tenant_id": tenant_id, "user_id": u, "code": c} for u, c in earned]
    for i in range(0, len(rows), 1000):
        await db.execute(pg_insert(UserBadge).values(rows[i:i + 1000]).on_conflict_do_nothing(constraint="uq_user_badge"))


async def xp_catchup(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:
    """Crédite l'XP jour par jour (fenêtres locales complètes), sans dépendre de la rétention des identités."""
    today = to_local(datetime.now(timezone.utc)).date()
    yesterday = today - timedelta(days=1)
    rows = (await db.execute(select(UserProfile.user_id, UserProfile.xp_through, UserProfile.created_at)
                             .where(UserProfile.tenant_id == tenant_id))).all()
    pending = [(u, t or (to_local(c).date() - timedelta(days=1))) for u, t, c in rows]
    pending = [(u, t) for u, t in pending if t < yesterday]
    if not pending:
        return 0
    first = max(min(t for _, t in pending) + timedelta(days=1), yesterday - timedelta(days=6))
    delta: dict = {}
    d = first
    while d <= yesterday:
        start = datetime.combine(d, time.min, TZ).astimezone(timezone.utc)
        comps = await points_for_window(db, tenant_id, start, start + timedelta(days=1))
        for u, t in pending:
            if t < d and u in comps:
                delta[u] = delta.get(u, 0.0) + max(0.0, comps[u]["raw"])
        d += timedelta(days=1)
    for u, _ in pending:
        await db.execute(update(UserProfile).where(UserProfile.user_id == u).values(
            xp=UserProfile.xp + int(round(delta.get(u, 0.0))), xp_through=yesterday))
    return len(pending)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ lecture

def _badge_codes(codes: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    return sorted(set(codes), key=lambda c: -BADGES.get(c, ("", "", "", 0))[3])


async def board(db: AsyncSession, user: User, kind: str, limit: int) -> dict:
    if kind not in PERIODS:
        kind = "week"
    limit = max(1, min(limit, acs.LB_MAX_LIMIT))
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    _, _, key = bounds(kind, now)
    base = (select(LeaderboardEntry, User.pseudonym, UserProfile.display_name, UserProfile.xp)
            .join(User, User.id == LeaderboardEntry.user_id)
            .outerjoin(UserProfile, UserProfile.user_id == LeaderboardEntry.user_id)
            .where(LeaderboardEntry.tenant_id == user.tenant_id, LeaderboardEntry.period_kind == kind,
                   LeaderboardEntry.period_key == key))
    top = (await db.execute(base.where(LeaderboardEntry.rank > 0, LeaderboardEntry.rank <= limit)
                            .order_by(LeaderboardEntry.rank))).all()
    me = (await db.execute(base.where(LeaderboardEntry.user_id == user.id))).first()
    total = (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(LeaderboardEntry).where(
        LeaderboardEntry.tenant_id == user.tenant_id, LeaderboardEntry.period_kind == kind,
        LeaderboardEntry.period_key == key, LeaderboardEntry.rank > 0))).scalar_one()
    around = []
    if me and me[0].rank > limit:
        around = (await db.execute(base.where(LeaderboardEntry.rank.between(me[0].rank - 2, me[0].rank + 2))
                                   .order_by(LeaderboardEntry.rank))).all()
    rows = list({r[0].user_id: r for r in [*top, *around, *([me] if me else [])]}.values())
    ids = [r[0].user_id for r in rows]
    prev_date = (await db.execute(select(func.max(LeaderboardSnapshot.snap_date)).where(
        LeaderboardSnapshot.tenant_id == user.tenant_id, LeaderboardSnapshot.period_kind == kind,
        LeaderboardSnapshot.snap_date < to_local(now).date()))).scalar_one()
    prev = {}
    if prev_date and ids:
        prev = dict((await db.execute(select(LeaderboardSnapshot.user_id, LeaderboardSnapshot.rank).where(
            LeaderboardSnapshot.tenant_id == user.tenant_id, LeaderboardSnapshot.period_kind == kind,
            LeaderboardSnapshot.snap_date == prev_date, LeaderboardSnapshot.user_id.in_(ids)))).all())
    badges: dict = {}
    if ids:
        for uid, code in (await db.execute(select(UserBadge.user_id, UserBadge.code).where(
                UserBadge.user_id.in_(ids)))).all():
            badges.setdefault(uid, []).append(code)

    def entry(r):
        e, pseudo, dname, xp = r
        comp = e.components or {}
        p = prev.get(e.user_id)
        return {"rank": e.rank, "display_name": dname or pseudo, "level": level_info(xp or 0), "score": e.score,
                "reliability": round(e.reliability, 3), "delta": (p - e.rank) if p and e.rank > 0 else None,
                "is_me": e.user_id == user.id, "badges": _badge_codes(badges.get(e.user_id, []))[:4],
                "validated": comp.get("v", 0), "missions": comp.get("missions", 0), "days": comp.get("days", 0)}
    return {"kind": kind, "label": LABELS[kind], "total": int(total), "entries": [entry(r) for r in top],
            "around": [entry(r) for r in around], "me": entry(me) if me else None}


async def my_detail(db: AsyncSession, user: User) -> dict:
    from app.services.accounts import ensure_profile
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    profile = await ensure_profile(db, user)
    periods = {}
    for kind in PERIODS:
        _, _, key = bounds(kind, now)
        e = (await db.execute(select(LeaderboardEntry).where(
            LeaderboardEntry.user_id == user.id, LeaderboardEntry.period_kind == kind,
            LeaderboardEntry.period_key == key))).scalar_one_or_none()
        periods[kind] = None if e is None else {"rank": e.rank, "score": e.score, "reliability": e.reliability,
                                                "components": e.components}
    have = dict((await db.execute(select(UserBadge.code, UserBadge.awarded_at).where(UserBadge.user_id == user.id))).all())
    badges = [{"code": c, "label": v[0], "description": v[1], "icon": v[2], "tier": v[3],
               "earned": c in have, "awarded_at": have.get(c)} for c, v in BADGES.items()]
    hist = (await db.execute(select(LeaderboardSnapshot.snap_date, LeaderboardSnapshot.rank, LeaderboardSnapshot.score)
                             .where(LeaderboardSnapshot.user_id == user.id, LeaderboardSnapshot.period_kind == "season")
                             .order_by(LeaderboardSnapshot.snap_date.desc()).limit(60))).all()
    return {"level": level_info(profile.xp or 0), "periods": periods, "badges": badges,
            "history": [{"date": d.isoformat(), "rank": r, "score": s} for d, r, s in reversed(hist)]}

# FICHIER: services/map.py

import math
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from fastapi import HTTPException
from sqlalchemy import Float, case, func, literal_column, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
import uuid
from sqlalchemy import or_
from app.models.measurement import Measurement, MeasurementQuality
from app.core.config import settings
from app.models.enums import CalibrationStatus, DayPart, QualityStatus
from app.models.geo import GeographicCell, NoiseAggregation
from app.models.measurement import Measurement
from app.schemas.schemas import TimeFilter
from app.services.acoustics import confidence_index
from app.services.geo import (cell_center, cell_margin_degrees, cell_polygon, is_valid_cell, period_dates,
                              resolve_period)

_TEN = literal_column("10.0")


def statuses(include_indicative: bool) -> list[QualityStatus]:
    return [QualityStatus.validated] + ([QualityStatus.indicative] if include_indicative else [])


def confidence_of(count: int, devices: int, calibrated: int, last: datetime | None, now: datetime) -> float:
    hours = (now - last).total_seconds() / 3600 if last else 1e9
    return round(confidence_index(
        count, devices, (calibrated / count) if count else 0.0, hours,
        target_count=settings.CONF_TARGET_MEASUREMENTS, target_devices=settings.CONF_TARGET_DEVICES,
        recency_tau_hours=settings.CONF_RECENCY_TAU_HOURS, w_count=settings.CONF_W_COUNT,
        w_devices=settings.CONF_W_DEVICES, w_calibration=settings.CONF_W_CALIBRATION,
        w_recency=settings.CONF_W_RECENCY), 3)


def _agg_cols():
    corrected = Measurement.laeq + Measurement.correction_db_applied
    energy = func.sum(Measurement.duration_seconds * func.power(_TEN, corrected / _TEN))
    duration = func.sum(Measurement.duration_seconds)
    return {
        "n": func.count(Measurement.id),
        "devices": func.count(func.distinct(Measurement.contributor_hash)),
        "laeq": _TEN * func.log(energy / duration, type_=Float),
        "lmax": func.max(Measurement.lmax + Measurement.correction_db_applied),
        "last": func.max(Measurement.measured_at),
        "calibrated": func.sum(case((Measurement.calibration_status == CalibrationStatus.calibrated, 1), else_=0)),
    }


def _conditions(tenant_id, start, end, day_part: str, stats: list[QualityStatus]):
    conds = [Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.measured_at >= start, Measurement.measured_at < end,
             Measurement.quality_status.in_(stats)]
    if day_part != "all":
        conds.append(Measurement.day_part == DayPart(day_part))
    return conds


async def cell_rows(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, tf: TimeFilter, include_indicative: bool,
                    min_devices: int, bbox: tuple[float, float, float, float] | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    start, end = resolve_period(tf)
    cols = _agg_cols()
    stmt = (select(Measurement.h3_index, *[c.label(k) for k, c in cols.items()])
            .where(*_conditions(tenant_id, start, end, tf.day_part, statuses(include_indicative)))
            .group_by(Measurement.h3_index).limit(settings.MAP_MAX_CELLS))
    if bbox:
        m = cell_margin_degrees()
        stmt = stmt.join(GeographicCell, GeographicCell.id == Measurement.cell_id).where(
            func.ST_Intersects(GeographicCell.center, func.ST_MakeEnvelope(
                bbox[0] - m, bbox[1] - m, bbox[2] + m, bbox[3] + m, 4326)))
    #if min_devices > 1:
    #    stmt = stmt.having(cols["devices"] >= min_devices)

    if min_devices > 1:
        stmt = stmt.outerjoin(MeasurementQuality, MeasurementQuality.measurement_id == Measurement.id)
        admin_ok = func.bool_or(MeasurementQuality.evaluator == 'admin')
        stmt = stmt.having(or_(cols["devices"] >= min_devices, admin_ok))
        
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    out = []
    for r in (await db.execute(stmt)).all():
        out.append({"h3_index": r.h3_index, "laeq_db": round(float(r.laeq), 1), "lmax_db": round(float(r.lmax), 1),
                    "measurement_count": r.n, "device_count": r.devices, "last_measurement_at": r.last,
                    "calibrated_share": round((r.calibrated or 0) / r.n, 3),
                    "confidence": confidence_of(r.n, r.devices, int(r.calibrated or 0), r.last, now)})
    return out


async def noise_geojson(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, tf: TimeFilter, include_indicative: bool,
                        min_confidence: float, min_devices: int, bbox) -> dict:
    rows = await cell_rows(db, tenant_id, tf, include_indicative, min_devices, bbox)
    features = [{
        "type": "Feature",
        "geometry": {"type": "Polygon", "coordinates": [cell_polygon(r["h3_index"])]},
        "properties": {**r, "last_measurement_at": r["last_measurement_at"].isoformat()},
    } for r in rows if r["confidence"] >= min_confidence]
    return {"type": "FeatureCollection", "features": features}


async def cell_detail(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, h3_index: str, tf: TimeFilter,
                      include_indicative: bool, min_devices: int) -> dict | None:
    if not is_valid_cell(h3_index):
        raise HTTPException(422, "invalid_h3_index")
    start, end = resolve_period(tf)
    stats = statuses(include_indicative)
    cols = _agg_cols()
    r = (await db.execute(
        select(*[c.label(k) for k, c in cols.items()]).where(
            Measurement.h3_index == h3_index, *_conditions(tenant_id, start, end, tf.day_part, stats)))).one()
    if not r.n or r.devices < min_devices:
        return None
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    ctx_rows = (await db.execute(
        select(Measurement.noise_context, func.count()).where(
            Measurement.h3_index == h3_index, *_conditions(tenant_id, start, end, tf.day_part, stats),
            Measurement.noise_context.is_not(None)).group_by(Measurement.noise_context))).all()

    d_from, d_to = period_dates(start, end)
    cell_ids = select(GeographicCell.id).where(GeographicCell.tenant_id == tenant_id,
                                               GeographicCell.h3_index == h3_index).scalar_subquery()
    agg_conds = [NoiseAggregation.tenant_id == tenant_id, NoiseAggregation.cell_id == cell_ids,
                 NoiseAggregation.bucket_date >= d_from, NoiseAggregation.bucket_date <= d_to,
                 NoiseAggregation.quality_status.in_(stats)]
    if tf.day_part != "all":
        agg_conds.append(NoiseAggregation.day_part == DayPart(tf.day_part))
    en, du = func.sum(NoiseAggregation.energy_sum), func.sum(NoiseAggregation.duration_total)
    daily = (await db.execute(
        select(NoiseAggregation.bucket_date, _TEN * func.log(en / du, type_=Float), func.max(NoiseAggregation.lmax_db),
               func.sum(NoiseAggregation.measurement_count)).where(*agg_conds)
        .group_by(NoiseAggregation.bucket_date).order_by(NoiseAggregation.bucket_date))).all()
    parts = (await db.execute(
        select(NoiseAggregation.day_part, _TEN * func.log(en / du, type_=Float),
               func.sum(NoiseAggregation.measurement_count)).where(*agg_conds)
        .group_by(NoiseAggregation.day_part))).all()

    lat, lon = cell_center(h3_index)
    return {
        "h3_index": h3_index, "center": {"lat": lat, "lon": lon},
        "polygon": cell_polygon(h3_index),
        "summary": {"laeq_db": round(float(r.laeq), 1), "lmax_db": round(float(r.lmax), 1),
                    "measurement_count": r.n, "device_count": r.devices, "last_measurement_at": r.last,
                    "calibrated_share": round((r.calibrated or 0) / r.n, 3),
                    "confidence": confidence_of(r.n, r.devices, int(r.calibrated or 0), r.last, now)},
        "by_day_part": [{"day_part": p.value, "laeq_db": round(float(l), 1), "measurement_count": int(n)}
                        for p, l, n in parts],
        "daily": [{"date": d.isoformat(), "laeq_db": round(float(l), 1), "lmax_db": round(float(mx), 1),
                   "measurement_count": int(n)} for d, l, mx, n in daily],
        "context_breakdown": {c.value: n for c, n in ctx_rows},
        "period": {"from": d_from.isoformat(), "to": d_to.isoformat(), "day_part": tf.day_part,
                   "include_indicative": include_indicative},
    }


################################################################################
# FICHIER: services/measurement.py
################################################################################

import logging
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from fastapi import HTTPException
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.redis import get_redis
from app.core.security import contributor_token
from app.models.collection import CollectionProposal
from app.models.devices import Device, DeviceModel
from app.models.enums import ProposalStatus, QualityStatus
from app.models.measurement import Measurement, MeasurementQuality
from app.models.tenancy import User
from app.schemas.schemas import (BatchItemResult, BatchResult, HistoryItem, MeasurementAck,
                                      MeasurementBatch, MeasurementCreate)
from app.services import quota as quota_service
from app.services.geo import cell_for, day_part_of, get_or_create_cell, to_local
from app.services.user import require_consents
from app.tasks.celery_app import celery_app

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _enqueue_evaluation(tenant_id: uuid.UUID, measurement_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
    try:
        celery_app.send_task("measurements.evaluate", args=[str(tenant_id), str(measurement_id)])
    except Exception:
        log.exception("enqueue_failed", extra={"tenant_id": str(tenant_id)})   # le balayeur reprendra la mesure


async def _find_existing(db: AsyncSession, user: User, client_id: uuid.UUID) -> Measurement | None:
    return (await db.execute(select(Measurement).where(
        Measurement.tenant_id == user.tenant_id, Measurement.user_id == user.id,
        Measurement.client_measurement_id == client_id))).scalar_one_or_none()


async def ingest(db: AsyncSession, user: User, p: MeasurementCreate) -> MeasurementAck:
    existing = await _find_existing(db, user, p.client_measurement_id)
    if existing is not None:
        return MeasurementAck(id=existing.id, client_measurement_id=p.client_measurement_id,
                              quality_status=existing.quality_status, duplicate=True)
    if user.participation_paused:
        raise HTTPException(403, "participation_paused")
    await require_consents(db, user)

    device = await db.get(Device, p.device_id)
    if device is None or device.user_id != user.id:
        raise HTTPException(404, "device_not_found")

    proposal = None
    if p.proposal_id is not None:
        proposal = await db.get(CollectionProposal, p.proposal_id, with_for_update=True)
        if proposal is None or proposal.user_id != user.id or proposal.status != ProposalStatus.accepted:
            raise HTTPException(409, "proposal_not_accepted")

    if settings.QUOTA_ENABLED:
        hard = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - p.measured_at) <= timedelta(minutes=settings.QUOTA_HARD_TOLERANCE_MINUTES)
        decision = await quota_service.evaluate(
            db, get_redis(), user, p.latitude, p.longitude, p.measured_at,
            proposal=p.proposal_id is not None, hard=hard, consume=True)
        if not decision.allowed:
            raise HTTPException(429, decision.reason)

    local = to_local(p.measured_at)
    h3_index = cell_for(p.latitude, p.longitude)
    cell = await get_or_create_cell(db, user.tenant_id, h3_index)
    m = Measurement(
        id=uuid.uuid4(), tenant_id=user.tenant_id, user_id=user.id, device_id=device.id,
        device_model_id=device.device_model_id, proposal_id=p.proposal_id, cell_id=cell.id,
        client_measurement_id=p.client_measurement_id,
        contributor_hash=contributor_token(user.tenant_id, user.id, f"{local:%Y-%m}"),
        measured_at=p.measured_at, local_date=local.date(), day_part=day_part_of(local),
        latitude=p.latitude, longitude=p.longitude,
        geom=func.ST_SetSRID(func.ST_MakePoint(p.longitude, p.latitude), 4326),
        h3_index=h3_index, gps_accuracy=p.gps_accuracy_m,
        duration_seconds=p.duration_seconds, effective_duration_seconds=p.effective_duration_seconds,
        sample_rate_hz=p.sample_rate_hz, laeq=p.laeq, lmax=p.lmax, lmin=p.lmin,
        saturation_ratio=p.saturation_ratio, moved_distance_m=p.moved_distance_m,
        noise_context=p.noise_context, audio_retained=False)
    db.add(m)
    if proposal is not None:
        proposal.status = ProposalStatus.completed
        proposal.completed_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    try:
        await db.commit()
    except IntegrityError:
        await db.rollback()                      # annule aussi le débit du quota
        existing = await _find_existing(db, user, p.client_measurement_id)
        if existing is None:
            raise HTTPException(409, "conflict")
        return MeasurementAck(id=existing.id, client_measurement_id=p.client_measurement_id,
                              quality_status=existing.quality_status, duplicate=True)
    _enqueue_evaluation(user.tenant_id, m.id)
    return MeasurementAck(id=m.id, client_measurement_id=p.client_measurement_id,
                          quality_status=QualityStatus.pending, duplicate=False)


async def ingest_batch(db: AsyncSession, user: User, batch: MeasurementBatch) -> BatchResult:
    results: list[BatchItemResult] = []
    for item in batch.items:
        try:
            ack = await ingest(db, user, item)
            results.append(BatchItemResult(client_measurement_id=item.client_measurement_id,
                                           result="duplicate" if ack.duplicate else "accepted", id=ack.id))
        except HTTPException as exc:
            await db.rollback()
            results.append(BatchItemResult(client_measurement_id=item.client_measurement_id,
                                           result="error", error=str(exc.detail)))
    return BatchResult(results=results)


async def history(db: AsyncSession, user: User, limit: int, offset: int) -> list[HistoryItem]:
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(Measurement, MeasurementQuality)
        .outerjoin(MeasurementQuality, MeasurementQuality.measurement_id == Measurement.id)
        .where(Measurement.tenant_id == user.tenant_id, Measurement.user_id == user.id)
        .order_by(Measurement.measured_at.desc()).limit(limit).offset(offset))).all()
    return [HistoryItem(
        id=m.id, measured_at=m.measured_at, h3_index=m.h3_index,
        laeq_db=round(m.laeq + m.correction_db_applied, 1), lmax_db=round(m.lmax + m.correction_db_applied, 1),
        day_part=m.day_part, calibration_status=m.calibration_status, quality_status=m.quality_status,
        quality_score=q.score if q else None, reason_codes=[r["code"] for r in (q.reasons if q else [])],
        noise_context=m.noise_context, location_anonymized=m.location_anonymized) for m, q in rows]


################################################################################
# FICHIER: services/missions.py
################################################################################

import json
import math
import uuid
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import h3
from fastapi import HTTPException
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy import case, func, or_, select, update
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.road_settings import road_settings as rs
from app.models.enums import MissionStatus, QualityStatus
from app.models.geo import GeographicCell, NoiseAggregation
from app.models.measurement import Measurement
from app.models.quota import Mission
from app.models.tenancy import User, UserConsent
from app.services import quota as quota_service
from app.services import road_missions
from app.services.acoustics import clamp01
from app.services.geo import TZ

_ACTIVE = (MissionStatus.offered, MissionStatus.accepted)
_USABLE = [QualityStatus.validated, QualityStatus.indicative]


@dataclass
class Zone:
    zone_id: str
    lat: float
    lon: float
    deficit: float
    need: float
    radius_m: float


@dataclass
class Profile:
    user_id: uuid.UUID
    anchor: tuple[float, float] | None
    n14: int
    R: float
    C: float
    acc: float
    load: int
    score: float


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ zones en manque

async def zone_deficits(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, now: datetime) -> list[Zone]:
    key = f"t:{tenant_id}:mission:zones"
    hit = await redis.get(key)
    if hit:
        return [Zone(**z) for z in json.loads(hit)]
    since = (now.astimezone(TZ) - timedelta(days=settings.COVERAGE_WINDOW_DAYS)).date()
    n = func.coalesce(func.sum(NoiseAggregation.measurement_count), 0)
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(GeographicCell.h3_index, func.ST_Y(GeographicCell.center), func.ST_X(GeographicCell.center), n)
        .outerjoin(NoiseAggregation, (NoiseAggregation.cell_id == GeographicCell.id)
                   & (NoiseAggregation.bucket_date >= since) & NoiseAggregation.quality_status.in_(_USABLE))
        .where(GeographicCell.tenant_id == tenant_id).group_by(GeographicCell.id).limit(50000))).all()
    target = settings.TARGET_MEASUREMENTS_PER_CELL
    acc: dict = {}
    for h, lat, lon, cnt in rows:
        zid = h3.cell_to_parent(h, settings.MISSION_ZONE_H3_RES)
        a = acc.setdefault(zid, {"cells": 0, "deficit": 0.0, "sl": 0.0, "so": 0.0, "pts": []})
        a["cells"] += 1
        d = max(0, target - int(cnt))
        if d > 0:
            a["deficit"] += d
            a["sl"] += lat * d
            a["so"] += lon * d
            a["pts"].append((lat, lon))
    zones: list[Zone] = []
    for zid, a in acc.items():
        if a["deficit"] < settings.MISSION_MIN_ZONE_DEFICIT:
            continue
        need = clamp01(a["deficit"] / (a["cells"] * target))
        if need < 0.25:
            continue
        lat, lon = a["sl"] / a["deficit"], a["so"] / a["deficit"]
        far = max(quota_service.haversine_m(lat, lon, p[0], p[1]) for p in a["pts"])
        zones.append(Zone(zid, lat, lon, a["deficit"], need, min(2500.0, max(400.0, far + 250.0))))
    zones.sort(key=lambda z: z.deficit * z.need, reverse=True)
    zones = zones[:settings.MISSION_MAX_ZONES]
    await redis.set(key, json.dumps([asdict(z) for z in zones]), ex=300)
    return zones


async def _committed(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, now: datetime):
    """Engagements sur les missions de zone uniquement (les missions routes ont leur propre logique)."""
    committed: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    pairs: set = set()
    rows = (await db.execute(select(Mission.zone_h3, Mission.user_id, Mission.status, Mission.required_count,
                                    Mission.done_count, Mission.created_at).where(
        Mission.tenant_id == tenant_id, Mission.kind == "zone",
        or_(Mission.status.in_(_ACTIVE),
            Mission.created_at >= now - timedelta(days=settings.MISSION_DECLINE_COOLDOWN_DAYS))))).all()
    for zone, uid, st, req, done, _ in rows:
        pairs.add((uid, zone))
        if st in _ACTIVE:
            committed[zone] += max(0, req - done)
    return committed, pairs


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ profils d'utilisateurs

async def profiles(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, now: datetime,
                   only: list[uuid.UUID] | None = None) -> dict[uuid.UUID, Profile]:
    q = select(User.id).where(User.tenant_id == tenant_id, User.is_active.is_(True),
                              User.participation_paused.is_(False))
    if only:
        q = q.where(User.id.in_(only))
    ids = list((await db.execute(q.limit(settings.QUOTA_BATCH * 5))).scalars())
    if not ids:
        return {}
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(UserConsent.user_id, UserConsent.consent_type, UserConsent.granted)
        .where(UserConsent.tenant_id == tenant_id, UserConsent.user_id.in_(ids))
        .order_by(UserConsent.user_id, UserConsent.consent_type, UserConsent.created_at.desc())
        .distinct(UserConsent.user_id, UserConsent.consent_type))).all()
    granted: dict = defaultdict(int)
    for uid, _, g in rows:
        granted[uid] += 1 if g else 0
    ids = [u for u in ids if granted[u] >= 2]
    if not ids:
        return {}

    last = {uid: (lat, lon) for uid, lat, lon in (await db.execute(
        select(Measurement.user_id, Measurement.latitude, Measurement.longitude)
        .where(Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.user_id.in_(ids),
               Measurement.measured_at >= now - timedelta(days=30),
               Measurement.quality_status != QualityStatus.rejected)
        .distinct(Measurement.user_id).order_by(Measurement.user_id, Measurement.measured_at.desc()))).all()}
    n14 = dict((await db.execute(
        select(Measurement.user_id, func.count()).where(
            Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.user_id.in_(ids),
            Measurement.measured_at >= now - timedelta(days=14),
            Measurement.quality_status != QualityStatus.rejected).group_by(Measurement.user_id))).all())
    mets = await quota_service.metrics_bulk(db, tenant_id, ids, now)

    pos: dict = defaultdict(int)
    neg: dict = defaultdict(int)
    load: dict = defaultdict(int)
    for uid, st, n in (await db.execute(
            select(Mission.user_id, Mission.status, func.count()).where(
                Mission.tenant_id == tenant_id, Mission.user_id.in_(ids),
                Mission.created_at >= now - timedelta(days=60)).group_by(Mission.user_id, Mission.status))).all():
        if st in (MissionStatus.accepted, MissionStatus.completed, MissionStatus.failed):
            pos[uid] += n
        elif st in (MissionStatus.declined, MissionStatus.expired):
            neg[uid] += n
        if st in _ACTIVE:
            load[uid] += n

    out = {}
    for uid in ids:
        acc = (pos[uid] + 1.5) / (pos[uid] + neg[uid] + 3)
        act = min(1.0, int(n14.get(uid, 0)) / settings.MISSION_ACTIVITY_TARGET)
        score = (settings.MS_W_ACTIVITY * act + settings.MS_W_RELIABILITY * mets[uid]["R"]
                 + settings.MS_W_COMPLETION * mets[uid]["C"] + settings.MS_W_ACCEPTANCE * acc)
        out[uid] = Profile(uid, last.get(uid), int(n14.get(uid, 0)), mets[uid]["R"], mets[uid]["C"], acc,
                           load[uid], score)
    return out


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ affectation (missions de zone)

async def _assign(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, zones: list[Zone], profs: dict[uuid.UUID, Profile],
                  now: datetime, max_new: int) -> list[Mission]:
    committed, pairs = await _committed(db, tenant_id, now)
    created: list[Mission] = []
    new_count: dict = defaultdict(int)
    mx = settings.MISSION_MAX_COUNT
    mn = settings.MISSION_MIN_COUNT
    for z in zones:
        remaining = z.deficit - committed.get(z.zone_id, 0)
        if remaining < mn:
            continue
        cands = []
        for uid, p in profs.items():
            if p.anchor is None or p.score < settings.MISSION_MIN_SCORE:
                continue
            used = p.load + new_count[uid]
            if used >= settings.MISSION_MAX_PENDING or new_count[uid] >= max_new or (uid, z.zone_id) in pairs:
                continue
            d_km = quota_service.haversine_m(p.anchor[0], p.anchor[1], z.lat, z.lon) / 1000
            if d_km > settings.MISSION_MAX_RADIUS_KM:
                continue
            fit = (p.score * (1 - d_km / settings.MISSION_MAX_RADIUS_KM) ** 0.7
                   * max(0.1, 1 - used / settings.MISSION_MAX_PENDING))
            cands.append((fit, str(uid), uid, d_km))
        cands.sort(key=lambda c: c[0], reverse=True)
        for fit, _, uid, d_km in cands[:settings.MISSION_USERS_PER_ZONE]:
            if remaining < mn:
                break
            p = profs[uid]
            size = mn + (mx - mn) * p.score * (0.6 + 0.4 * z.need)
            size = int(max(mn, min(round(size), math.ceil(remaining), mx)))
            db.add(m := Mission(
                tenant_id=tenant_id, user_id=uid, kind="zone", status=MissionStatus.offered, zone_h3=z.zone_id,
                center_lat=z.lat, center_lon=z.lon, radius_m=z.radius_m, distance_km=round(d_km, 1),
                required_count=size, done_count=0, confirmed_count=0,
                reward_credits=round(size * settings.MISSION_REWARD_PER_MEASUREMENT * (0.75 + 0.5 * z.need), 1),
                priority=round(100 * clamp01(0.6 * z.need + 0.4 * fit), 1),
                features={"zone_deficit": z.deficit, "zone_need": round(z.need, 3), "score": round(p.score, 3),
                          "fit": round(fit, 3)},
                offer_expires_at=now + timedelta(hours=settings.MISSION_OFFER_HOURS)))
            created.append(m)
            remaining -= size
            committed[z.zone_id] += size
            new_count[uid] += 1
            pairs.add((uid, z.zone_id))
    await db.flush()
    return created


async def generate_for_tenant(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    zones = await zone_deficits(db, redis, tenant_id, now)
    profs = await profiles(db, tenant_id, now)
    if not profs:
        return 0
    created: list[Mission] = []
    if zones:
        created = await _assign(db, tenant_id, zones, profs, now, settings.MISSION_MAX_NEW_PER_USER_PER_RUN)
        for m in created:
            profs[m.user_id].load += 1
    if rs.ROAD_MISSIONS_ENABLED:
        created += await road_missions.assign(db, tenant_id, profs, now, rs.ROAD_MAX_NEW_PER_USER_PER_RUN)
    return len(created)


async def sync_for_user(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, user: User, lat: float, lon: float) -> None:
    """Génération à la demande depuis la position courante (non stockée), au plus 1 fois par heure."""
    if not await redis.set(f"t:{user.tenant_id}:msync:{user.id}", "1", nx=True,
                           ex=settings.MISSION_SYNC_MIN_MINUTES * 60):
        return
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    zones = await zone_deficits(db, redis, user.tenant_id, now)
    profs = await profiles(db, user.tenant_id, now, only=[user.id])
    p = profs.get(user.id)
    if p is None:
        return
    p.anchor = (lat, lon)
    if zones:
        created = await _assign(db, user.tenant_id, zones, {user.id: p}, now, max_new=2)
        p.load += len(created)
    if rs.ROAD_MISSIONS_ENABLED:
        await road_missions.assign(db, user.tenant_id, {user.id: p}, now, rs.ROAD_SYNC_MAX_NEW)


async def ranking(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, limit: int = 50) -> list[dict]:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    profs = await profiles(db, tenant_id, now)
    pseudo = dict((await db.execute(select(User.id, User.pseudonym).where(User.id.in_(list(profs))))).all()) if profs else {}
    ordered = sorted(profs.values(), key=lambda p: p.score, reverse=True)[:limit]
    return [{"pseudonym": pseudo.get(p.user_id), "score": round(p.score, 3), "activity_14d": p.n14,
             "reliability": round(p.R, 3), "completion": round(p.C, 3), "acceptance": round(p.acc, 3),
             "load": p.load} for p in ordered]


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ cycle de vie

async def expire(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    res = await db.execute(update(Mission).where(
        Mission.tenant_id == tenant_id, Mission.status == MissionStatus.offered,
        Mission.offer_expires_at <= now).values(status=MissionStatus.expired, finished_at=now))
    return res.rowcount or 0


async def reconcile(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, user_id: uuid.UUID | None = None) -> int:
    """Recalcule la progression depuis les mesures réelles ; clôture (réussite + récompense, ou échec).
    Mission route : mesures dans la tolérance du point cible, dans la plage horaire et la fenêtre visées."""
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    conds = [Mission.tenant_id == tenant_id, Mission.status == MissionStatus.accepted]
    if user_id:
        conds.append(Mission.user_id == user_id)
    missions = (await db.execute(select(Mission).where(*conds).with_for_update(skip_locked=True).limit(1000))).scalars().all()
    for m in missions:
        dist = func.ST_DistanceSphere(Measurement.geom,
                                      func.ST_SetSRID(func.ST_MakePoint(m.center_lon, m.center_lat), 4326))
        since = m.accepted_at
        if m.window_start_at is not None and m.window_start_at > since:
            since = m.window_start_at
        mconds = [Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.user_id == m.user_id,
                  Measurement.measured_at >= since, Measurement.quality_status != QualityStatus.rejected,
                  dist <= m.radius_m]
        if m.kind == "road":
            if m.target_day_part is not None:
                mconds.append(Measurement.day_part == m.target_day_part)
            if m.window_end_at is not None:
                mconds.append(Measurement.measured_at <= m.window_end_at)
        total, ok = (await db.execute(select(
            func.count(), func.coalesce(func.sum(case((Measurement.quality_status.in_(_USABLE), 1), else_=0)), 0)
        ).where(*mconds))).one()
        m.done_count, m.confirmed_count = int(total), int(ok)
        if m.confirmed_count >= m.required_count:
            m.status, m.finished_at = MissionStatus.completed, now
            await quota_service.grant(db, tenant_id, m.user_id, m.reward_credits, now)
        elif m.deadline_at is not None and m.deadline_at <= now:
            m.status, m.finished_at = MissionStatus.failed, now
    return len(missions)


async def list_for_user(db: AsyncSession, user: User) -> list[Mission]:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    return list((await db.execute(
        select(Mission).where(Mission.user_id == user.id, or_(
            Mission.status.in_(_ACTIVE), Mission.finished_at >= now - timedelta(days=7)))
        .order_by(Mission.created_at.desc()).limit(50))).scalars())


async def respond(db: AsyncSession, user: User, mission_id: uuid.UUID, accept: bool) -> Mission:
    m = await db.get(Mission, mission_id, with_for_update=True)
    if m is None or m.user_id != user.id:
        raise HTTPException(404, "mission_not_found")
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if m.status != MissionStatus.offered or m.offer_expires_at <= now:
        raise HTTPException(409, "mission_not_offered")
    if accept:
        active = (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(Mission).where(
            Mission.user_id == user.id, Mission.status == MissionStatus.accepted))).scalar_one()
        if active >= settings.MISSION_MAX_ACTIVE:
            raise HTTPException(409, "mission_limit_reached")
        m.status, m.accepted_at = MissionStatus.accepted, now
        m.deadline_at = now + timedelta(hours=settings.MISSION_COMPLETION_HOURS)
        if m.window_end_at is not None:                    # mission route : jamais au-delà de la plage visée
            m.deadline_at = min(m.deadline_at, m.window_end_at)
    else:
        m.status, m.finished_at = MissionStatus.declined, now
    await db.commit()
    return m


################################################################################
# FICHIER: services/noise_field.py
################################################################################

import asyncio
import base64
import hashlib
import json
import math
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import h3
from fastapi import HTTPException
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.field_settings import field_settings as fs
from app.schemas.schemas import TimeFilter
from app.services import weather
from app.services.geo import cell_center
from app.services.map import cell_rows

_DB0, _DBSTEP = 20.0, 0.5            # niveau = 20 + octet x 0,5 dB (0 = pas de donnée)


def _compute(sources: list, bounds: tuple, cols: int, rows: int, wind: dict | None, sigma0: float) -> dict:
    west, south, east, north = bounds
    lat0, lon0 = (south + north) / 2, (west + east) / 2
    mx, my = 111320.0 * math.cos(math.radians(lat0)), 110574.0
    width, height = (east - west) * mx, (north - south) * my
    sx, sy = width / cols, height / rows
    x0, y0 = -width / 2, -height / 2
    sigma = max(sigma0, 1.5 * max(sx, sy))

    speed = wind["speed_ms"] if wind else 0.0
    ux = (wind["u"] / speed) if speed > 0.05 else 0.0
    uy = (wind["v"] / speed) if speed > 0.05 else 0.0
    asym = min(1.0, speed / 3.0)

    num = [0.0] * (cols * rows)
    den = [0.0] * (cols * rows)
    for lat, lon, level, w in sources:
        px, py = (lon - lon0) * mx, (lat - lat0) * my
        loud = min(1.0, max(0.0, (level - 45.0) / 45.0))
        stretch = 1.0 + min(1.5, 0.12 * speed * (0.6 + loud))
        s_dn = sigma * stretch * (1.0 + 0.25 * asym)          # côté aval : le nuage s'étire
        s_up = sigma * (1.0 - 0.25 * asym)                    # côté amont : bruit atténué
        s_pp = sigma * max(0.65, 1.0 - 0.04 * speed)          # largeur transversale
        shift = min(fs.FIELD_MAX_DRIFT_M, speed * fs.FIELD_ADVECTION_SECONDS * (0.5 + 0.5 * loud), 0.6 * s_dn)
        cx, cy = px + ux * shift, py + uy * shift
        reach = 3.0 * max(s_dn, s_up, s_pp)
        i0, i1 = max(0, int((cx - reach - x0) / sx)), min(cols - 1, int((cx + reach - x0) / sx))
        j0, j1 = max(0, int((cy - reach - y0) / sy)), min(rows - 1, int((cy + reach - y0) / sy))
        if i0 > i1 or j0 > j1:
            continue
        e = 10.0 ** (level / 10.0)
        for j in range(j0, j1 + 1):
            dy = y0 + (j + 0.5) * sy - cy
            base = j * cols
            for i in range(i0, i1 + 1):
                dx = x0 + (i + 0.5) * sx - cx
                par = dx * ux + dy * uy
                per = -dx * uy + dy * ux
                sp = s_dn if par >= 0 else s_up
                k = w * math.exp(-0.5 * ((par / sp) ** 2 + (per / s_pp) ** 2))
                num[base + i] += k * e
                den[base + i] += k

    w0 = fs.FIELD_BACKGROUND_WEIGHT
    level_b, support_b = bytearray(cols * rows), bytearray(cols * rows)
    lo, hi = 999.0, -999.0
    for idx in range(cols * rows):
        d = den[idx]
        if d <= 0:
            continue
        support = d / (d + w0)
        if support < 0.03:
            continue
        db = 10.0 * math.log10(max(num[idx] / (d + w0), 1e-12))
        if db < 30.0:
            continue
        level_b[idx] = max(1, min(255, int(round((db - _DB0) / _DBSTEP))))
        support_b[idx] = int(round(255 * support))
        lo, hi = min(lo, db), max(hi, db)
    return {"level": bytes(level_b), "support": bytes(support_b),
            "min_db": None if lo > hi else round(lo, 1), "max_db": None if lo > hi else round(hi, 1)}


async def build(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, tf: TimeFilter, include_indicative: bool,
                min_devices: int, bbox: tuple[float, float, float, float] | None) -> dict:
    if bbox is None:
        raise HTTPException(422, "bbox_required")
    if bbox[2] - bbox[0] > fs.FIELD_MAX_BBOX_DEG or bbox[3] - bbox[1] > fs.FIELD_MAX_BBOX_DEG:
        raise HTTPException(422, "bbox_too_large")
    snap = 0.01                                   # bbox calée : tous les utilisateurs partagent le même résultat en cache
    b = (math.floor(bbox[0] / snap) * snap, math.floor(bbox[1] / snap) * snap,
         math.ceil(bbox[2] / snap) * snap, math.ceil(bbox[3] / snap) * snap)
    clat, clon = (b[1] + b[3]) / 2, (b[0] + b[2]) / 2
    wind = await weather.wind_at(redis, clat, clon)

    raw = json.dumps([str(tenant_id), tf.period.value, str(tf.date_from), str(tf.date_to), tf.day_part,
                      include_indicative, min_devices, [round(v, 4) for v in b],
                      None if wind is None else [round(wind["speed_kmh"]), round(wind["dir_from"] / 5)]])
    key = f"t:{tenant_id}:field:{hashlib.sha256(raw.encode()).hexdigest()}"
    hit = await redis.get(key)
    if hit:
        return json.loads(hit)

    mx = 111320.0 * math.cos(math.radians(clat))
    width_m, height_m = (b[2] - b[0]) * mx, (b[3] - b[1]) * 110574.0
    cols = int(min(fs.FIELD_MAX_GRID, max(fs.FIELD_MIN_GRID, math.ceil(width_m / fs.FIELD_TARGET_STEP_M))))
    rows = int(min(fs.FIELD_MAX_GRID, max(fs.FIELD_MIN_GRID, math.ceil(height_m / fs.FIELD_TARGET_STEP_M))))
    cell_r = h3.average_hexagon_edge_length(settings.H3_RESOLUTION, unit="m")
    sigma0 = fs.FIELD_SIGMA_FACTOR * cell_r
    sigma_eff = max(sigma0, 1.5 * max(width_m / cols, height_m / rows))
    margin_m = 11.5 * sigma_eff                    # portée maximale d'une source (étirement + décalage compris)
    mdx = min(0.5, margin_m / (111320.0 * math.cos(math.radians(clat))))
    mdy = min(0.5, margin_m / 110574.0)
    ebbox = (max(-179.9, b[0] - mdx), max(-89.9, b[1] - mdy), min(179.9, b[2] + mdx), min(89.9, b[3] + mdy))

    cells = await cell_rows(db, tenant_id, tf, include_indicative, min_devices, ebbox)
    cells.sort(key=lambda r: r["measurement_count"], reverse=True)
    sources = []
    for r in cells[:fs.FIELD_MAX_SOURCES]:
        lat, lon = cell_center(r["h3_index"])
        sources.append((lat, lon, float(r["laeq_db"]), max(0.1, float(r["confidence"]))))

    res = await asyncio.to_thread(_compute, sources, b, cols, rows, wind, sigma0)
    out = {
        "bounds": [b[0], b[1], b[2], b[3]], "cols": cols, "rows": rows,
        "db_offset": _DB0, "db_step": _DBSTEP,
        "level_b64": base64.b64encode(res["level"]).decode(), "support_b64": base64.b64encode(res["support"]).decode(),
        "min_db": res["min_db"], "max_db": res["max_db"], "sources": len(sources),
        "wind": None if wind is None else {k: wind[k] for k in ("speed_kmh", "dir_from", "dir_to", "u", "v")},
        "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}
    await redis.set(key, json.dumps(out), ex=fs.FIELD_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS)
    return out
