# FICHIER: services/privacy.py
################################################################################

import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import delete, func, select, update
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.models.collection import CollectionProposal
from app.models.enums import ProposalStatus, QualityStatus
from app.models.geo import GeographicCell
from app.models.insights import Report
from app.models.measurement import Measurement
from app.models.tenancy import AuditLog

_BATCH = 10_000


def _center_values():
    lat = select(func.ST_Y(GeographicCell.center)).where(GeographicCell.id == Measurement.cell_id).scalar_subquery()
    lon = select(func.ST_X(GeographicCell.center)).where(GeographicCell.id == Measurement.cell_id).scalar_subquery()
    geom = select(GeographicCell.center).where(GeographicCell.id == Measurement.cell_id).scalar_subquery()
    return {"latitude": lat, "longitude": lon, "geom": geom, "location_anonymized": True}


async def round_locations(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, where: list) -> int:
    """Remplace la position précise par le centre de la cellule H3."""
    total = 0
    while True:
        ids = select(Measurement.id).where(
            Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.location_anonymized.is_(False), *where
        ).limit(_BATCH)
        res = await db.execute(update(Measurement).where(Measurement.id.in_(ids)).values(**_center_values()))
        total += res.rowcount or 0
        if (res.rowcount or 0) < _BATCH:
            return total


async def anonymize_tenant(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> dict:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    done = and_ready = [Measurement.quality_status != QualityStatus.pending, Measurement.aggregated_at.is_not(None)]

    # 1) Position précise -> centre de cellule
    loc_cutoff = now - timedelta(days=settings.LOCATION_RETENTION_DAYS)
    rounded = await round_locations(db, tenant_id, [Measurement.measured_at < loc_cutoff, *and_ready])

    # 2) Rupture du lien d'identité (utilisateur, appareil, proposition)
    id_cutoff = now - timedelta(days=settings.IDENTITY_UNLINK_DAYS)
    unlinked = 0
    while True:
        ids = select(Measurement.id).where(
            Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.measured_at < id_cutoff,
            Measurement.user_id.is_not(None), *and_ready).limit(_BATCH)
        res = await db.execute(update(Measurement).where(Measurement.id.in_(ids)).values(
            user_id=None, device_id=None, proposal_id=None))
        unlinked += res.rowcount or 0
        if (res.rowcount or 0) < _BATCH:
            break

    # 3) Propositions terminées et anciennes (elles portent user_id)
    purged = await db.execute(delete(CollectionProposal).where(
        CollectionProposal.tenant_id == tenant_id, CollectionProposal.created_at < id_cutoff,
        CollectionProposal.status.in_([ProposalStatus.completed, ProposalStatus.declined, ProposalStatus.expired]),
        CollectionProposal.learned_at.is_not(None) | (CollectionProposal.status != ProposalStatus.completed)))
    return {"locations_rounded": rounded, "identities_unlinked": unlinked,
            "proposals_purged": purged.rowcount or 0}


async def purge_expired(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> dict:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    reports = await db.execute(delete(Report).where(
        Report.tenant_id == tenant_id, Report.created_at < now - timedelta(days=settings.REPORT_RETENTION_DAYS)))
    audits = await db.execute(delete(AuditLog).where(
        AuditLog.tenant_id == tenant_id, AuditLog.created_at < now - timedelta(days=settings.AUDIT_RETENTION_DAYS)))
    return {"reports": reports.rowcount or 0, "audit_logs": audits.rowcount or 0}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: services/quality.py
################################################################################

import statistics
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import func, select, update
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import insert as pg_insert
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.models.devices import DeviceModel
from app.models.enums import CalibrationStatus, QualityStatus
from app.models.geo import GeographicCell
from app.models.measurement import Measurement, MeasurementQuality
from app.models.tenancy import Tenant
from app.services.acoustics import clamp01
from app.services.geo import ring
from app.services.thresholds import Thresholds, build_thresholds


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MeasurementFacts:
    laeq: float
    lmax: float
    lmin: float
    gps_accuracy_m: float
    duration_seconds: float
    effective_duration_seconds: float
    saturation_ratio: float
    moved_distance_m: float
    measured_at: datetime
    calibrated: bool
    correction_db: float


@dataclass
class QualityResult:
    status: QualityStatus
    score: float
    sub_scores: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    reasons: list = field(default_factory=list)


def evaluate(facts: MeasurementFacts, neighbor_levels: list[float], th: Thresholds,
             now: datetime | None = None) -> QualityResult:
    """Algorithme B : décide si une mesure est assez fiable pour alimenter la carte (fonction pure)."""
    now = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    reasons: list[dict] = []
    hard = False
    sub: dict[str, float] = {}

    def reject(code: str, **detail):
        nonlocal hard
        hard = True
        reasons.append({"code": code, "severity": "reject", **detail})

    corrected = facts.laeq + facts.correction_db
    if not (th.laeq_min_db <= corrected <= th.laeq_max_db):
        reject("level_out_of_range", value=round(corrected, 1))
    tol = th.level_tolerance_db
    if not (facts.lmin - tol <= facts.laeq <= facts.lmax + tol):
        reject("inconsistent_levels")

    if facts.measured_at > now + timedelta(seconds=th.future_tolerance_s):
        reject("timestamp_in_future")
    if now - facts.measured_at > timedelta(hours=th.max_age_hours):
        reject("measurement_too_old")

    # GPS
    if facts.gps_accuracy_m > th.gps_reject_m:
        reject("gps_accuracy_too_low", value=facts.gps_accuracy_m)
        sub["gps"] = 0.0
    elif facts.gps_accuracy_m <= th.gps_good_m:
        sub["gps"] = 1.0
    else:
        sub["gps"] = clamp01(1 - (facts.gps_accuracy_m - th.gps_good_m) / (th.gps_reject_m - th.gps_good_m))

    # Durée effective
    ratio = facts.effective_duration_seconds / facts.duration_seconds if facts.duration_seconds > 0 else 0.0
    if ratio < th.min_duration_ratio:
        reject("capture_too_short", ratio=round(ratio, 3))
        sub["duration"] = 0.0
    else:
        sub["duration"] = 1.0

    # Saturation
    if facts.saturation_ratio > th.max_saturation_ratio:
        reject("audio_saturation", value=facts.saturation_ratio)
        sub["saturation"] = 0.0
    else:
        sub["saturation"] = clamp01(1 - facts.saturation_ratio / th.max_saturation_ratio)

    # Déplacement pendant la capture
    if facts.moved_distance_m > th.max_movement_m:
        reject("moved_during_capture", value=facts.moved_distance_m)
        sub["movement"] = 0.0
    else:
        sub["movement"] = clamp01(1 - facts.moved_distance_m / th.max_movement_m)

    # Calibration
    sub["calibration"] = 1.0 if facts.calibrated else th.uncalibrated_subscore
    if not facts.calibrated:
        reasons.append({"code": "uncalibrated_device", "severity": "info"})

    # Cohérence avec les voisines (z-score robuste médiane / MAD)
    outlier = False
    if len(neighbor_levels) >= th.neighbor_min_samples:
        med = statistics.median(neighbor_levels)
        mad = statistics.median(abs(v - med) for v in neighbor_levels)
        sigma = max(1.4826 * mad, th.neighbor_sigma_floor_db)
        z = abs(corrected - med) / sigma
        if z > th.neighbor_z_max:
            outlier = True
            sub["neighbor"] = clamp01(0.5 - 0.1 * (z - th.neighbor_z_max))
            reasons.append({"code": "neighbor_outlier", "severity": "warn", "z": round(z, 2)})
        else:
            sub["neighbor"] = 1.0 - 0.5 * (z / th.neighbor_z_max)
    else:
        sub["neighbor"] = th.neighbor_neutral
        reasons.append({"code": "insufficient_neighbors", "severity": "info", "count": len(neighbor_levels)})

    score = sum(th.weights[k] * sub[k] for k in th.weights)
    cap_indicative = outlier or (not facts.calibrated and not th.allow_uncalibrated_validated)
    if hard:
        status, score = QualityStatus.rejected, 0.0
    elif score >= th.validated_min_score and not cap_indicative:
        status = QualityStatus.validated
    elif score >= th.indicative_min_score:
        status = QualityStatus.indicative
    else:
        status = QualityStatus.rejected
        reasons.append({"code": "low_score", "severity": "reject", "score": round(score, 3)})
    return QualityResult(status=status, score=round(score, 4), sub_scores={k: round(v, 3) for k, v in sub.items()},
                         reasons=reasons)


async def _neighbor_levels(db: AsyncSession, m: Measurement, th: Thresholds) -> list[float]:
    window = timedelta(days=th.neighbor_window_days)
    q = select(Measurement.laeq + Measurement.correction_db_applied).where(
        Measurement.tenant_id == m.tenant_id, Measurement.h3_index.in_(ring(m.h3_index, 1)),
        Measurement.day_part == m.day_part, Measurement.quality_status == QualityStatus.validated,
        Measurement.measured_at >= m.measured_at - window, Measurement.measured_at <= m.measured_at + window,
        Measurement.id != m.id).limit(500)
    return [float(v) for v in (await db.execute(q)).scalars()]


async def evaluate_measurement(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, measurement_id: uuid.UUID,
                               force: bool = False) -> QualityStatus | None:
    m = await db.get(Measurement, measurement_id, with_for_update=True)
    if m is None or m.tenant_id != tenant_id:
        return None
    existing = (await db.execute(select(MeasurementQuality).where(
        MeasurementQuality.measurement_id == m.id))).scalar_one_or_none()
    if existing is not None and existing.evaluator in ("admin", "peer"):
        return existing.status                       # une décision admin n'est jamais écrasée
    if existing is not None and not force and m.quality_status != QualityStatus.pending:
        pass
    tenant = await db.get(Tenant, tenant_id)
    th = build_thresholds(tenant.settings if tenant else None)

    correction, calibrated = 0.0, False
    if m.device_model_id:
        dm = await db.get(DeviceModel, m.device_model_id)
        if dm is not None and dm.calibration_status == CalibrationStatus.calibrated and dm.correction_db is not None:
            correction, calibrated = float(dm.correction_db), True

    facts = MeasurementFacts(m.laeq, m.lmax, m.lmin, m.gps_accuracy, m.duration_seconds,
                             m.effective_duration_seconds, m.saturation_ratio, m.moved_distance_m,
                             m.measured_at, calibrated, correction)
    result = evaluate(facts, await _neighbor_levels(db, m, th), th)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    await db.execute(pg_insert(MeasurementQuality).values(
        id=uuid.uuid4(), tenant_id=tenant_id, measurement_id=m.id, status=result.status, score=result.score,
        sub_scores=result.sub_scores, reasons=result.reasons, evaluator="auto", evaluated_at=now,
    ).on_conflict_do_update(index_elements=["measurement_id"], set_={
        "status": result.status, "score": result.score, "sub_scores": result.sub_scores,
        "reasons": result.reasons, "evaluator": "auto", "override_by": None, "evaluated_at": now}))

    m.correction_db_applied = correction
    m.calibration_status = CalibrationStatus.calibrated if calibrated else CalibrationStatus.uncalibrated
    m.quality_status = result.status
    m.aggregated_at = None                           # force le recalcul des agrégats concernés
    if result.status != QualityStatus.rejected:
        await db.execute(update(GeographicCell).where(GeographicCell.id == m.cell_id).values(
            last_measurement_at=func.greatest(func.coalesce(GeographicCell.last_measurement_at, m.measured_at),
                                              m.measured_at)))
    return result.status


################################################################################
# FICHIER: services/quota.py
################################################################################

import json
import math
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import insert as pg_insert
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.models.enums import DayPart, MissionStatus, QualityStatus, Role
from app.models.geo import GeographicCell, NoiseAggregation
from app.models.measurement import Measurement
from app.models.quota import Mission, QuotaDay, UserQuota
from app.models.tenancy import User
from app.services.acoustics import clamp01
from app.services.collection import compute_features, load_cell_stats
from app.services.geo import TZ, cell_for, day_part_of, ring, to_local

HOUR_WEIGHTS = [0.25] * 6 + [1.0] * 16 + [0.25] * 2      # nuit 0,25 ; jour 1,0 (somme = 18)
HOUR_WEIGHT_SUM = sum(HOUR_WEIGHTS)
_USABLE = [QualityStatus.validated, QualityStatus.indicative]
_REWARD = {QualityStatus.validated: 1.0, QualityStatus.indicative: 0.5, QualityStatus.rejected: 0.0}


def haversine_m(lat1: float, lon1: float, lat2: float, lon2: float) -> float:
    p1, p2 = math.radians(lat1), math.radians(lat2)
    dp, dl = p2 - p1, math.radians(lon2 - lon1)
    a = math.sin(dp / 2) ** 2 + math.cos(p1) * math.cos(p2) * math.sin(dl / 2) ** 2
    return 2 * 6371000.0 * math.asin(math.sqrt(a))


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ contexte global (besoins du territoire)

async def global_context(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, now: datetime) -> dict:
    key = f"t:{tenant_id}:quota:ctx"
    hit = await redis.get(key)
    if hit:
        return json.loads(hit)
    since = (now.astimezone(TZ) - timedelta(days=settings.COVERAGE_WINDOW_DAYS)).date()
    target = settings.TARGET_MEASUREMENTS_PER_CELL
    part_target = max(1.0, target / len(DayPart))
    total_cells = (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(GeographicCell).where(
        GeographicCell.tenant_id == tenant_id))).scalar_one()
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(NoiseAggregation.cell_id, NoiseAggregation.day_part, func.sum(NoiseAggregation.measurement_count))
        .where(NoiseAggregation.tenant_id == tenant_id, NoiseAggregation.bucket_date >= since,
               NoiseAggregation.quality_status.in_(_USABLE))
        .group_by(NoiseAggregation.cell_id, NoiseAggregation.day_part))).all()
    per_cell: dict = {}
    cover = {p.value: 0.0 for p in DayPart}
    for cell_id, part, n in rows:
        per_cell[cell_id] = per_cell.get(cell_id, 0) + int(n)
        cover[part.value] += min(1.0, int(n) / part_target)
    deficit = (sum(max(0, target - n) for n in per_cell.values())
               + max(0, total_cells - len(per_cell)) * target)
    need_by_part = {p: (clamp01(1 - c / total_cells) if total_cells else 0.5) for p, c in cover.items()}
    need_global = sum(need_by_part.values()) / len(need_by_part)
    supply = (await db.execute(select(func.count(func.distinct(Measurement.user_id))).where(
        Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.user_id.is_not(None),
        Measurement.measured_at >= now - timedelta(days=7)))).scalar_one()
    demand = deficit / (settings.COVERAGE_WINDOW_DAYS * max(1, supply))
    daily = min(max(demand, settings.QUOTA_MIN_DAILY), settings.QUOTA_MAX_DAILY)
    if total_cells < settings.QUOTA_COLD_START_CELLS:            # démarrage : on ne bride pas la collecte
        daily = max(daily, settings.QUOTA_COLD_START_DAILY)
    ctx = {"need_by_part": need_by_part, "need_global": need_global, "supply": int(supply),
           "global_daily": daily, "total_cells": int(total_cells), "deficit": float(deficit)}
    await redis.set(key, json.dumps(ctx), ex=settings.QUOTA_CONTEXT_TTL_SECONDS)
    return ctx


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ indicateurs par utilisateur

async def metrics_bulk(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, user_ids: list[uuid.UUID],
                       now: datetime) -> dict[uuid.UUID, dict]:
    """R = fiabilité (30 j, bayésienne), U = utilisation du solde (7 j), C = réussite des missions (60 j)."""
    out = {u: {"R": settings.QUOTA_RELIABILITY_PRIOR, "U": 0.7, "C": 0.5} for u in user_ids}
    if not user_ids:
        return out
    s, p0 = settings.QUOTA_RELIABILITY_STRENGTH, settings.QUOTA_RELIABILITY_PRIOR
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(Measurement.user_id, Measurement.quality_status, func.count()).where(
            Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.user_id.in_(user_ids),
            Measurement.measured_at >= now - timedelta(days=30),
            Measurement.quality_status != QualityStatus.pending)
        .group_by(Measurement.user_id, Measurement.quality_status))).all()
    acc: dict = {}
    for uid, st, n in rows:
        a = acc.setdefault(uid, [0.0, 0])
        a[0] += _REWARD[st] * n
        a[1] += n
    for uid, (rw, n) in acc.items():
        out[uid]["R"] = (rw + s * p0) / (n + s)

    today = to_local(now).date()
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(QuotaDay.user_id, func.sum(QuotaDay.credited), func.sum(QuotaDay.used)).where(
            QuotaDay.tenant_id == tenant_id, QuotaDay.user_id.in_(user_ids),
            QuotaDay.local_date >= today - timedelta(days=7)).group_by(QuotaDay.user_id))).all()
    for uid, cr, us in rows:
        cr, us = float(cr or 0), float(us or 0)
        if cr >= 1.0:
            out[uid]["U"] = min(1.5, us / cr)

    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(Mission.user_id, Mission.status, func.count()).where(
            Mission.tenant_id == tenant_id, Mission.user_id.in_(user_ids),
            Mission.status.in_([MissionStatus.completed, MissionStatus.failed]),
            Mission.created_at >= now - timedelta(days=60)).group_by(Mission.user_id, Mission.status))).all()
    done: dict = {}
    for uid, st, n in rows:
        d = done.setdefault(uid, [0, 0])
        d[0 if st == MissionStatus.completed else 1] += n
    for uid, (ok, ko) in done.items():
        out[uid]["C"] = (ok + 1.5) / (ok + ko + 3)
    return out


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ formules

def allowance_target(ctx: dict, r: float, u: float, c: float) -> float:
    v = ctx["global_daily"] * (0.5 + r) * (0.6 + 0.6 * min(1.0, u)) * (0.9 + 0.3 * c)
    return min(max(v, settings.QUOTA_MIN_DAILY), settings.QUOTA_MAX_DAILY)


def retention_per_day(need: float, util: float) -> float:
    base = settings.QUOTA_RETENTION_MIN + (settings.QUOTA_RETENTION_MAX - settings.QUOTA_RETENTION_MIN) * need
    idle = 0.6 + 0.4 * min(1.0, util / 0.5)
    return min(max(base * idle, 0.2), 0.98)


def step(balance: float, target: float, need: float, util: float, hour: int, dt_h: float, cap: float):
    keep = retention_per_day(need, util) ** (dt_h / 24.0)
    decayed = balance * (1 - keep) if balance > 0 else 0.0     # une dette ne s'efface pas toute seule
    base = balance - decayed
    credit = target * HOUR_WEIGHTS[hour] / HOUR_WEIGHT_SUM * dt_h * (0.7 + 0.6 * need)
    new = min(base + credit, cap)
    return new, max(0.0, new - base), decayed


def project(balance: float, start: datetime, hours: float, target: float, util: float, ctx: dict, cap: float):
    n = max(1, math.ceil(hours))
    sdt = hours / n
    bal, cr, dc = balance, 0.0, 0.0
    for i in range(n):
        lt = to_local(start + timedelta(hours=sdt * (i + 0.5)))
        need = ctx["need_by_part"][day_part_of(lt).value]
        bal, c, d = step(bal, target, need, util, lt.hour, sdt, cap)
        cr, dc = cr + c, dc + d
    return bal, cr, dc


def cooldown_minutes(need: float) -> float:
    lo, hi = settings.QUOTA_COOLDOWN_MIN_MINUTES, settings.QUOTA_COOLDOWN_MAX_MINUTES
    return lo + (hi - lo) * (1 - need) ** 1.5


def cost_of(need: float) -> float:
    return settings.QUOTA_COST_MIN + (settings.QUOTA_COST_MAX - settings.QUOTA_COST_MIN) * (1 - need)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ persistance

async def _bump_days(db: AsyncSession, rows: list[dict]) -> None:
    if not rows:
        return
    stmt = pg_insert(QuotaDay).values(rows)
    stmt = stmt.on_conflict_do_update(constraint="uq_quota_days_user_date", set_={
        "credited": QuotaDay.credited + stmt.excluded.credited,
        "used": QuotaDay.used + stmt.excluded.used,
        "decayed": QuotaDay.decayed + stmt.excluded.decayed,
        "measurements": QuotaDay.measurements + stmt.excluded.measurements,
        "target": func.coalesce(func.nullif(stmt.excluded.target, 0), QuotaDay.target)})
    await db.execute(stmt)


def _day_row(q: UserQuota, now: datetime, *, credited=0.0, used=0.0, decayed=0.0, measurements=0, target=0.0) -> dict:
    return {"id": uuid.uuid4(), "tenant_id": q.tenant_id, "user_id": q.user_id,
            "local_date": to_local(now).date(), "credited": credited, "used": used, "decayed": decayed,
            "measurements": measurements, "target": target}


def _apply(q: UserQuota, ctx: dict, m: dict, now: datetime, paused: bool) -> dict | None:
    local = to_local(now)
    target = allowance_target(ctx, m["R"], m["U"], m["C"])
    cap = max(settings.QUOTA_MIN_CAP, target * settings.QUOTA_CAP_DAYS)
    need_now = ctx["need_by_part"][day_part_of(local).value]
    credited = decayed = 0.0
    if not paused:
        dt_h = min(24.0, max(0.0, (now - q.updated_at).total_seconds() / 3600))
        if dt_h > 0:
            q.balance, credited, decayed = project(q.balance, q.updated_at, dt_h, target, m["U"], ctx, cap)
    q.daily_target, q.cap = round(target, 2), round(cap, 2)
    q.rate_per_hour = target * HOUR_WEIGHTS[local.hour] / HOUR_WEIGHT_SUM * (0.7 + 0.6 * need_now)
    q.reliability, q.utilization, q.mission_rate = m["R"], m["U"], m["C"]
    q.retention_per_day = retention_per_day(need_now, m["U"])
    q.updated_at = now
    if not (credited or decayed):
        return None
    return _day_row(q, now, credited=credited, decayed=decayed, target=target)


async def ensure_fresh(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, user: User, now: datetime, lock: bool = False) -> UserQuota:
    await db.execute(pg_insert(UserQuota).values(
        user_id=user.id, tenant_id=user.tenant_id, balance=settings.QUOTA_INITIAL_BALANCE, updated_at=now,
    ).on_conflict_do_nothing(index_elements=["user_id"]))
    stmt = select(UserQuota).where(UserQuota.user_id == user.id)
    if lock:
        stmt = stmt.with_for_update()
    q = (await db.execute(stmt)).scalar_one()
    if q.daily_target <= 0 or (now - q.updated_at).total_seconds() >= settings.QUOTA_LAZY_REFRESH_SECONDS:
        ctx = await global_context(db, redis, user.tenant_id, now)
        m = (await metrics_bulk(db, user.tenant_id, [user.id], now))[user.id]
        row = _apply(q, ctx, m, now, user.participation_paused)
        if row:
            await _bump_days(db, [row])
    return q


async def grant(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, user_id: uuid.UUID, amount: float, now: datetime) -> None:
    """Bonus (récompense de mission) : peut dépasser le plafond de 50 %."""
    await db.execute(pg_insert(UserQuota).values(
        user_id=user_id, tenant_id=tenant_id, balance=settings.QUOTA_INITIAL_BALANCE, updated_at=now,
    ).on_conflict_do_nothing(index_elements=["user_id"]))
    q = (await db.execute(select(UserQuota).where(UserQuota.user_id == user_id).with_for_update())).scalar_one()
    q.balance = min(q.balance + amount, max(q.cap, settings.QUOTA_MIN_CAP) * 1.5)
    await _bump_days(db, [_day_row(q, now, credited=amount)])


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ décision par prélèvement

@dataclass
class Decision:
    allowed: bool
    reason: str
    cost: float = 0.0
    balance: float = 0.0
    cooldown_minutes: float = 0.0
    retry_at: datetime | None = None
    cell_need: float = 0.0
    free: bool = False


async def _cell_need(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, h3_index: str, now: datetime) -> float:
    part = day_part_of(to_local(now))
    key = f"t:{tenant_id}:cneed:{h3_index}:{part.value}"
    hit = await redis.get(key)
    if hit is not None:
        return float(hit)
    stats = await load_cell_stats(db, tenant_id, [h3_index], now)
    f = compute_features(stats.get(h3_index), now, part, 0)
    need = clamp01(0.5 * f["coverage_gap"] + 0.3 * f["time_relevance"] + 0.2 * f["freshness"])
    await redis.set(key, f"{need:.4f}", ex=60)
    return need


async def _last_in_ring(db: AsyncSession, user: User, h3_index: str, until: datetime, horizon_min: float) -> dict:
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(Measurement.h3_index, func.max(Measurement.measured_at)).where(
            Measurement.tenant_id == user.tenant_id, Measurement.user_id == user.id,
            Measurement.h3_index.in_(ring(h3_index, 1)),
            Measurement.measured_at >= until - timedelta(minutes=horizon_min),
            Measurement.measured_at <= until).group_by(Measurement.h3_index))).all()
    return {h: t for h, t in rows}


async def _mission_for(db: AsyncSession, user: User, lat: float, lon: float, now: datetime) -> Mission | None:
    rows = (await db.execute(select(Mission).where(
        Mission.user_id == user.id, Mission.status == MissionStatus.accepted, Mission.deadline_at > now,
        Mission.done_count < Mission.required_count).order_by(Mission.deadline_at))).scalars()
    for m in rows:
        if haversine_m(lat, lon, m.center_lat, m.center_lon) <= m.radius_m:
            return m
    return None


async def evaluate(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, user: User, lat: float, lon: float, measured_at: datetime,
                   *, proposal: bool, hard: bool, consume: bool) -> Decision:
    if user.role != Role.contributor:
        return Decision(True, "staff", free=True)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    q = await ensure_fresh(db, redis, user, now, lock=consume)
    h3_index = cell_for(lat, lon)
    need = await _cell_need(db, redis, user.tenant_id, h3_index, now)
    cd, cost = cooldown_minutes(need), cost_of(need)
    mission = await _mission_for(db, user, lat, lon, now)
    base = dict(balance=q.balance, cooldown_minutes=cd, cell_need=need)

    if proposal or mission is not None:
        d = Decision(True, "proposal_free" if proposal else "mission_free", 0.0, free=True, **base)
    else:
        wait = 0.0
        for h, t in (await _last_in_ring(db, user, h3_index, measured_at, cd)).items():
            wait = max(wait, cd * (1.0 if h == h3_index else 0.5) - (measured_at - t).total_seconds() / 60)
        if wait > 0 and hard:
            return Decision(False, "cell_cooldown", cost, retry_at=measured_at + timedelta(minutes=wait), **base)
        if q.balance + 1e-9 < cost and hard:
            hours = min(48.0, (cost - q.balance) / max(q.rate_per_hour, 0.05))
            return Decision(False, "quota_exhausted", cost, retry_at=now + timedelta(hours=hours), **base)
        d = Decision(True, "ok", cost, **base)

    if consume:
        if d.cost > 0:
            q.balance = max(-q.cap, q.balance - d.cost)       # une synchro hors-ligne peut passer en négatif
            d.balance = q.balance
        await _bump_days(db, [_day_row(q, now, used=d.cost, measurements=1)])
        if mission is not None:
            mission.done_count += 1
    return d


async def state(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, user: User) -> dict:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    q = await ensure_fresh(db, redis, user, now)
    ctx = await global_context(db, redis, user.tenant_id, now)
    need_now = ctx["need_by_part"][day_part_of(to_local(now)).value]
    day = (await db.execute(select(QuotaDay).where(
        QuotaDay.user_id == user.id, QuotaDay.local_date == to_local(now).date()))).scalar_one_or_none()
    proj, _, _ = project(q.balance, now, 24, q.daily_target, q.utilization, ctx, q.cap)
    return {"balance": round(q.balance, 2), "measurements_left": max(0, int(q.balance)),
            "daily_target": q.daily_target, "rate_per_hour": round(q.rate_per_hour, 3), "cap": q.cap,
            "reliability": round(q.reliability, 3), "utilization": round(q.utilization, 3),
            "mission_rate": round(q.mission_rate, 3), "retention_per_day": round(q.retention_per_day, 3),
            "need_now": round(need_now, 3), "projected_24h": round(proj, 2),
            "today_credited": round(day.credited, 2) if day else 0.0,
            "today_used": round(day.used, 2) if day else 0.0,
            "today_decayed": round(day.decayed, 2) if day else 0.0}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ tâches de fond

async def rebalance_tenant(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    ctx = await global_context(db, redis, tenant_id, now)
    done = 0
    while True:
        rows = (await db.execute(
            select(UserQuota, User.participation_paused).join(User, User.id == UserQuota.user_id)
            .where(UserQuota.tenant_id == tenant_id, User.is_active.is_(True),
                   UserQuota.updated_at < now - timedelta(minutes=10))
            .order_by(UserQuota.updated_at).limit(settings.QUOTA_BATCH)
            .with_for_update(of=UserQuota, skip_locked=True))).all()
        if not rows:
            break
        mets = await metrics_bulk(db, tenant_id, [r[0].user_id for r in rows], now)
        days = [d for q, paused in rows if (d := _apply(q, ctx, mets[q.user_id], now, paused))]
        await _bump_days(db, days)
        await db.flush()
        done += len(rows)
        if len(rows) < settings.QUOTA_BATCH:
            break
    return done


async def cleanup_tenant(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:
    from sqlalchemy import delete
    cutoff = to_local(datetime.now(timezone.utc)).date() - timedelta(days=settings.QUOTA_DAYS_RETENTION)
    res = await db.execute(delete(QuotaDay).where(QuotaDay.tenant_id == tenant_id, QuotaDay.local_date < cutoff))
    return res.rowcount or 0

# FICHIER: services/report.py
################################################################################

import csv
import io
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.models.enums import ReportKind, ReportStatus
from app.models.insights import Report
from app.schemas.schemas import TimeFilter
from app.services import ai as ai_service
from app.services.map import cell_rows

COLUMNS = ["h3_index", "laeq_db", "lmax_db", "measurement_count", "device_count", "confidence",
           "calibrated_share", "last_measurement_at"]


def build_csv(rows: list[dict]) -> bytes:
    buf = io.StringIO()
    w = csv.writer(buf)
    w.writerow(COLUMNS)
    for r in rows:
        w.writerow([r["h3_index"], r["laeq_db"], r["lmax_db"], r["measurement_count"], r["device_count"],
                    r["confidence"], r["calibrated_share"], r["last_measurement_at"].isoformat()])
    return buf.getvalue().encode("utf-8-sig")


def build_pdf(rows: list[dict], params: dict, narrative: dict | None) -> bytes:
    from reportlab.lib import colors
    from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
    from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet
    from reportlab.platypus import Paragraph, SimpleDocTemplate, Spacer, Table, TableStyle

    styles = getSampleStyleSheet()
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    doc = SimpleDocTemplate(buf, pagesize=A4, title="Rapport SunuSound")
    story = [Paragraph("Rapport SunuSound — cartographie sonore indicative", styles["Title"]),
             Paragraph(f"Période : {params['period']} — plage : {params['day_part']} — "
                       f"indicatives incluses : {'oui' if params['include_indicative'] else 'non'}", styles["Normal"]),
             Paragraph("Mesures participatives : indicatives, non certifiées réglementairement.", styles["Italic"]),
             Spacer(1, 12)]
    if narrative:
        story.append(Paragraph("Synthèse (générée par IA)", styles["Heading2"]))
        story.append(Paragraph(narrative["executive_summary"], styles["Normal"]))
        for title, key in (("Constats", "key_findings"), ("Recommandations", "recommendations"),
                           ("Limites", "limitations")):
            if narrative.get(key):
                story.append(Paragraph(title, styles["Heading3"]))
                for item in narrative[key]:
                    story.append(Paragraph(f"• {item}", styles["Normal"]))
        story.append(Spacer(1, 12))
    data = [["Cellule H3", "LAeq (dB)", "Lmax (dB)", "Mesures", "Appareils", "Confiance"]]
    for r in rows[:2000]:
        data.append([r["h3_index"], r["laeq_db"], r["lmax_db"], r["measurement_count"], r["device_count"], r["confidence"]])
    table = Table(data, repeatRows=1)
    table.setStyle(TableStyle([("BACKGROUND", (0, 0), (-1, 0), colors.lightgrey), ("FONTSIZE", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 8),
                               ("GRID", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0.25, colors.grey)]))
    story.append(table)
    doc.build(story)
    return buf.getvalue()


async def generate(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, report_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
    report = await db.get(Report, report_id, with_for_update=True)
    if report is None or report.status not in (ReportStatus.pending, ReportStatus.failed):
        return
    report.status = ReportStatus.running
    await db.commit()
    try:
        p = report.params
        tf = TimeFilter(period=p["period"], date_from=_d(p.get("date_from")), date_to=_d(p.get("date_to")),
                        day_part=p["day_part"])
        rows = await cell_rows(db, tenant_id, tf, p["include_indicative"], min_devices=1)
        rows.sort(key=lambda r: r["laeq_db"], reverse=True)
        if report.kind == ReportKind.csv:
            report.content, report.content_type = build_csv(rows), "text/csv"
            report.filename = f"sunusound-{report.id}.csv"
        else:
            narrative = None
            if p.get("include_ai_summary") and rows:
                narrative = await ai_service.summarize_report(redis, tenant_id, {
                    "period": {"period": p["period"], "day_part": p["day_part"]},
                    "cells_count": len(rows),
                    "top_cells": [{k: r[k] for k in ("laeq_db", "lmax_db", "measurement_count", "device_count", "confidence")}
                                  for r in rows[:20]],
                    "overall_median_laeq_db": sorted(r["laeq_db"] for r in rows)[len(rows) // 2]})
            report.content, report.content_type = build_pdf(rows, p, narrative), "application/pdf"
            report.filename = f"sunusound-{report.id}.pdf"
        report.status = ReportStatus.done
    except Exception as exc:                       # message court, sans donnée sensible
        report.status, report.error = ReportStatus.failed, f"{type(exc).__name__}"
    report.finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    await db.commit()


def _d(value):
    from datetime import date
    return date.fromisoformat(value) if value else None

# FICHIER: services/reviews.py

import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from fastapi import HTTPException
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy import case, func, select, update
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.account_settings import account_settings as acs
from app.core.config import settings
from app.models.enums import MissionStatus, QualityStatus
from app.models.measurement import Measurement, MeasurementQuality
from app.models.quota import Mission
from app.models.social import MeasurementReview, ReviewVote, UserProfile
from app.models.tenancy import User
from app.services.geo import to_local

_P = "t:{}:presence"


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ éligibilité

def _crit(key, label, value, required, reverse=False):
    ok = value <= required if reverse else value >= required
    return {"key": key, "label": label, "value": value, "required": required, "ok": bool(ok), "reverse": reverse}


async def eligibility_bulk(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, user_ids: list[uuid.UUID], now: datetime) -> dict:
    out = {u: {"v": 0, "n": 0, "reward": 0.0, "last": None, "days": 0, "missions": 0, "created": None} for u in user_ids}
    if not user_ids:
        return out
    since = now - timedelta(days=90)
    reward = {QualityStatus.validated: 1.0, QualityStatus.indicative: 0.5, QualityStatus.rejected: 0.0}
    for uid, st, n in (await db.execute(select(Measurement.user_id, Measurement.quality_status, func.count()).where(
            Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.user_id.in_(user_ids), Measurement.measured_at >= since,
            Measurement.quality_status != QualityStatus.pending).group_by(Measurement.user_id, Measurement.quality_status))).all():
        out[uid]["n"] += n
        out[uid]["reward"] += reward[st] * n
        out[uid]["v"] += n if st == QualityStatus.validated else 0
    for uid, last in (await db.execute(select(Measurement.user_id, func.max(Measurement.measured_at)).where(
            Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.user_id.in_(user_ids),
            Measurement.measured_at >= since).group_by(Measurement.user_id))).all():
        out[uid]["last"] = last
    for uid, d in (await db.execute(select(Measurement.user_id, func.count(func.distinct(Measurement.local_date))).where(
            Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.user_id.in_(user_ids),
            Measurement.measured_at >= now - timedelta(days=60),
            Measurement.quality_status != QualityStatus.rejected).group_by(Measurement.user_id))).all():
        out[uid]["days"] = d
    for uid, n in (await db.execute(select(Mission.user_id, func.count()).where(
            Mission.tenant_id == tenant_id, Mission.user_id.in_(user_ids),
            Mission.status == MissionStatus.completed).group_by(Mission.user_id))).all():
        out[uid]["missions"] = n
    for uid, c in (await db.execute(select(User.id, User.created_at).where(User.id.in_(user_ids)))).all():
        out[uid]["created"] = c
    res = {}
    for uid, d in out.items():
        rel = (d["reward"] + 3.0) / (d["n"] + 5.0)
        since_last = (now - d["last"]).days if d["last"] else 999
        crit = [_crit("validated", "Mesures validées (90 j)", d["v"], acs.ANALYST_MIN_VALIDATED),
                _crit("missions", "Missions réussies", d["missions"], acs.ANALYST_MIN_MISSIONS),
                _crit("reliability", "Fiabilité (%)", round(rel * 100), round(acs.ANALYST_MIN_RELIABILITY * 100)),
                _crit("account_days", "Ancienneté du compte (jours)",
                      (now - d["created"]).days if d["created"] else 0, acs.ANALYST_MIN_ACCOUNT_DAYS),
                _crit("active_days", "Jours d'activité (60 j)", d["days"], acs.ANALYST_MIN_ACTIVE_DAYS),
                _crit("recent", "Jours depuis la dernière mesure (maximum)", since_last,
                      acs.ANALYST_MAX_INACTIVE_DAYS, reverse=True)]
        res[uid] = {"eligible": all(c["ok"] for c in crit), "criteria": crit, "reliability": rel}
    return res


async def agreement_bulk(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, user_ids: list[uuid.UUID], now: datetime) -> dict:
    rows = (await db.execute(select(ReviewVote.reviewer_id, func.sum(case((ReviewVote.matched.is_(True), 1), else_=0)),
                                    func.count()).where(
        ReviewVote.tenant_id == tenant_id, ReviewVote.reviewer_id.in_(user_ids), ReviewVote.matched.is_not(None),
        ReviewVote.voted_at >= now - timedelta(days=60)).group_by(ReviewVote.reviewer_id))).all()
    return {u: (int(m or 0), int(n)) for u, m, n in rows}


async def analyst_state(db: AsyncSession, user: User) -> dict:
    from app.services.accounts import ensure_profile
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    p = await ensure_profile(db, user)
    el = (await eligibility_bulk(db, user.tenant_id, [user.id], now))[user.id]
    m, n = (await agreement_bulk(db, user.tenant_id, [user.id], now)).get(user.id, (0, 0))
    return {"enabled": p.analyst_enabled, "suspended_until": p.analyst_suspended_until,
            "eligibility": {"eligible": el["eligible"], "criteria": el["criteria"]},
            "stats": {"votes": n, "matched": m, "agreement": round((m + 3) / (n + 5), 3)}}


async def set_analyst(db: AsyncSession, user: User, enable: bool) -> None:
    from app.services.accounts import ensure_profile
    from app.services.user import latest_consents
    from app.models.enums import ConsentType
    p = await ensure_profile(db, user)
    if enable:
        c = await latest_consents(db, user.id)
        if ConsentType.geolocation not in c or not c[ConsentType.geolocation].granted:
            raise HTTPException(403, "consent_required")
        el = (await eligibility_bulk(db, user.tenant_id, [user.id], datetime.now(timezone.utc)))[user.id]
        if not el["eligible"]:
            raise HTTPException(409, "analyst_not_eligible")
        if not p.analyst_enabled:
            p.analyst_since = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    p.analyst_enabled = enable
    await db.commit()


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ présence (éphémère, Redis)

async def heartbeat(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, user: User, lat: float, lon: float) -> int:
    from app.services.accounts import ensure_profile
    p = await ensure_profile(db, user)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if not p.analyst_enabled or (p.analyst_suspended_until and p.analyst_suspended_until > now):
        raise HTTPException(403, "not_analyst")
    key = _P.format(user.tenant_id)
    pipe = redis.pipeline()
    pipe.geoadd(key, (round(lon, 4), round(lat, 4), str(user.id)))
    pipe.zadd(key + ":ts", {str(user.id): now.timestamp()})
    pipe.expire(key, 3600)
    pipe.expire(key + ":ts", 3600)
    await pipe.execute()
    return (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(ReviewVote).where(
        ReviewVote.reviewer_id == user.id, ReviewVote.status == "pending", ReviewVote.expires_at > now))).scalar_one()


async def purge_presence(redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
    key = _P.format(tenant_id)
    stale = await redis.zrangebyscore(key + ":ts", 0, datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp() - acs.PRESENCE_TTL_SECONDS)
    if stale:
        await redis.zrem(key, *stale)
        await redis.zrem(key + ":ts", *stale)


async def _nearby(redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, lat: float, lon: float) -> list[tuple[uuid.UUID, float]]:
    key = _P.format(tenant_id)
    res = await redis.geosearch(key, longitude=lon, latitude=lat, radius=acs.REVIEW_RADIUS_M, unit="m",
                                sort="ASC", count=60, withdist=True)
    if not res:
        return []
    ids = [r[0] for r in res]
    ts = await redis.zmscore(key + ":ts", ids)
    limit = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp() - acs.PRESENCE_TTL_SECONDS
    return [(uuid.UUID(i), float(d)) for (i, d), t in zip(res, ts) if t is not None and t >= limit]


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ envoi des demandes d'avis

async def dispatch(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, measurement_id: uuid.UUID) -> str:
    m = await db.get(Measurement, measurement_id)
    if m is None or m.user_id is None:
        return "gone"
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if now - m.measured_at > timedelta(minutes=acs.REVIEW_MAX_AGE_MINUTES):
        return "stale"                       # on ne juge que le volume du moment
    if m.quality_status not in (QualityStatus.validated, QualityStatus.indicative):
        return "not_eligible"
    q = (await db.execute(select(MeasurementQuality).where(MeasurementQuality.measurement_id == m.id))).scalar_one_or_none()
    if q is not None and q.evaluator in ("admin", "peer"):
        return "admin_priority"              # la décision d'un admin prime : aucun avis demandé
    if (await db.execute(select(MeasurementReview.id).where(MeasurementReview.measurement_id == m.id))).first():
        return "exists"
    near = [(u, d) for u, d in await _nearby(redis, tenant_id, m.latitude, m.longitude) if u != m.user_id]
    if len(near) < acs.REVIEW_MIN_VOTES:
        return "no_reviewers"
    ids = [u for u, _ in near]
    profiles = {p.user_id: p for p in (await db.execute(select(UserProfile).where(UserProfile.user_id.in_(ids)))).scalars()}
    pend = dict((await db.execute(select(ReviewVote.reviewer_id, func.count()).where(
        ReviewVote.reviewer_id.in_(ids), ReviewVote.status == "pending", ReviewVote.expires_at > now)
        .group_by(ReviewVote.reviewer_id))).all())
    cand = [(u, d) for u, d in near if (p := profiles.get(u)) and p.analyst_enabled
            and not (p.analyst_suspended_until and p.analyst_suspended_until > now)
            and pend.get(u, 0) < acs.REVIEW_MAX_PENDING_PER_USER]
    el = await eligibility_bulk(db, tenant_id, [u for u, _ in cand], now)
    agr = await agreement_bulk(db, tenant_id, [u for u, _ in cand], now)
    scored = []
    for u, d in cand:
        if not el[u]["eligible"]:
            continue
        mt, n = agr.get(u, (0, 0))
        w = (0.5 + el[u]["reliability"]) * (1.0 - 0.4 * d / acs.REVIEW_RADIUS_M) * (0.7 + 0.6 * (mt + 3) / (n + 5))
        scored.append((w, u, d))
    scored.sort(key=lambda t: t[0], reverse=True)
    chosen = scored[:acs.REVIEW_MAX_REVIEWERS]
    if len(chosen) < acs.REVIEW_MIN_VOTES:
        return "no_reviewers"
    exp = now + timedelta(minutes=acs.REVIEW_TTL_MINUTES)
    level = round(m.laeq + m.correction_db_applied, 1)
    review = MeasurementReview(tenant_id=tenant_id, measurement_id=m.id, status="open", requested=len(chosen),
                               expires_at=exp)
    db.add(review)
    await db.flush()
    for w, u, d in chosen:
        db.add(ReviewVote(tenant_id=tenant_id, review_id=review.id, reviewer_id=u, status="pending", level_db=level,
                          measured_at=m.measured_at, distance_m=d, weight=w, expires_at=exp))
    return "dispatched"


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ vote et décision

def decide(votes: list[tuple[str, bool, float]]) -> dict:
    tw = sum(w for _, _, w in votes) or 1.0
    a = sum(w for v, _, w in votes if v == "agree") / tw
    d = sum(w for v, _, w in votes if v == "down") / tw
    u = sum(w for v, _, w in votes if v == "up") / tw
    base = {"agree": a, "down": d, "up": u, "adjust": 0.0}
    if a >= acs.REVIEW_CONFIRM_RATIO:
        return {**base, "outcome": "confirmed"}
    direction, ratio = ("down", d) if d >= u else ("up", u)
    if ratio >= acs.REVIEW_ADJUST_RATIO:
        dir_w = sum(w for v, _, w in votes if v == direction)
        strong_w = sum(w for v, s, w in votes if v == direction and s)
        if dir_w and strong_w / dir_w >= acs.REVIEW_STRONG_REJECT_SHARE:
            return {**base, "outcome": "rejected"}          # écart important : trop grand pour un simple ajustement
        mag = min(acs.REVIEW_ADJUST_MAX_DB, acs.REVIEW_ADJUST_STEP_DB * (1 + 4 * (ratio - 0.5)))
        mag = round(mag * 2) / 2
        return {**base, "outcome": f"adjusted_{direction}", "adjust": -mag if direction == "down" else mag}
    return {**base, "outcome": "rejected"}                  # réponses contradictoires


async def finalize(db: AsyncSession, review_id: uuid.UUID, now: datetime) -> str | None:
    r = await db.get(MeasurementReview, review_id, with_for_update=True)
    if r is None or r.status != "open":
        return None
    votes = list((await db.execute(select(ReviewVote).where(ReviewVote.review_id == r.id))).scalars())
    for v in votes:
        if v.status == "pending":
            v.status = "expired"
    voted = [v for v in votes if v.status == "voted"]
    m = await db.get(Measurement, r.measurement_id, with_for_update=True)
    q = (await db.execute(select(MeasurementQuality).where(MeasurementQuality.measurement_id == r.measurement_id))).scalar_one_or_none()
    r.status, r.closed_at, r.votes = "closed", now, len(voted)
    if m is None or q is None or q.evaluator == "admin":
        r.outcome = "admin_skip"
        return r.outcome
    if len(voted) < acs.REVIEW_MIN_VOTES:
        r.outcome = "no_quorum"
        return r.outcome
    res = decide([(v.vote, v.strong, v.weight) for v in voted])
    r.outcome, r.adjust_db = res["outcome"], res["adjust"]
    r.agree_ratio, r.down_ratio, r.up_ratio = round(res["agree"], 3), round(res["down"], 3), round(res["up"], 3)
    reason = {"code": f"peer_{res['outcome']}" if res["outcome"] != "confirmed" else "peer_confirmed",
              "severity": "info", "votes": len(voted), "agree": r.agree_ratio, "adjust_db": res["adjust"]}
    if res["outcome"] == "rejected":
        reason["code"], reason["severity"] = "peer_rejected", "reject"
        m.quality_status, q.status, q.score = QualityStatus.rejected, QualityStatus.rejected, 0.0
    else:
        m.quality_status, q.status = QualityStatus.validated, QualityStatus.validated
        q.score = max(q.score, settings.VALIDATED_MIN_SCORE)
        if res["adjust"]:
            m.correction_db_applied = float(m.correction_db_applied or 0) + res["adjust"]
    q.evaluator, q.override_by, q.evaluated_at = "peer", None, now
    q.reasons = [*(q.reasons or []), reason]
    m.aggregated_at = None                                   # recalcul des agrégats concernés
    for v in voted:
        v.matched = (v.vote == "agree") if res["outcome"] in ("confirmed", "rejected") and res["outcome"] == "confirmed" \
            else (v.vote != "agree") if res["outcome"] == "rejected" else (v.vote == res["outcome"].split("_")[1])
    return r.outcome


async def cast_vote(db: AsyncSession, user: User, vote_id: uuid.UUID, vote: str, strong: bool) -> dict:
    v = await db.get(ReviewVote, vote_id, with_for_update=True)
    if v is None or v.reviewer_id != user.id:
        raise HTTPException(404, "review_not_found")
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if v.status != "pending" or v.expires_at <= now:
        raise HTTPException(409, "review_expired")
    v.vote, v.strong, v.status, v.voted_at = vote, strong and vote != "agree", "voted", now
    await db.flush()
    left = (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(ReviewVote).where(
        ReviewVote.review_id == v.review_id, ReviewVote.status == "pending"))).scalar_one()
    outcome = await finalize(db, v.review_id, now) if left == 0 else None
    await db.commit()
    return {"ok": True, "closed": outcome is not None}


async def close_expired(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    ids = (await db.execute(select(MeasurementReview.id).where(
        MeasurementReview.tenant_id == tenant_id, MeasurementReview.status == "open",
        MeasurementReview.expires_at <= now).limit(200))).scalars().all()
    for rid in ids:
        await finalize(db, rid, now)
    await purge_presence(redis, tenant_id)
    return len(ids)


async def cancel_rounds(db: AsyncSession, measurement_ids: list[uuid.UUID]) -> None:
    """Une décision admin saute le processus d'avis : manches ouvertes closes, demandes annulées."""
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    rids = (await db.execute(select(MeasurementReview.id).where(
        MeasurementReview.measurement_id.in_(measurement_ids), MeasurementReview.status == "open"))).scalars().all()
    if rids:
        await db.execute(update(ReviewVote).where(ReviewVote.review_id.in_(rids), ReviewVote.status == "pending")
                         .values(status="cancelled"))
        await db.execute(update(MeasurementReview).where(MeasurementReview.id.in_(rids))
                         .values(status="closed", outcome="admin_skip", closed_at=now))


async def audit_analysts(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    rows = (await db.execute(select(ReviewVote.reviewer_id, func.sum(case((ReviewVote.matched.is_(True), 1), else_=0)),
                                    func.count()).where(
        ReviewVote.tenant_id == tenant_id, ReviewVote.matched.is_not(None),
        ReviewVote.voted_at >= now - timedelta(days=60)).group_by(ReviewVote.reviewer_id)
        .having(func.count() >= acs.ANALYST_SUSPEND_MIN_VOTES))).all()
    bad = [u for u, m, n in rows if (m or 0) / n < acs.ANALYST_SUSPEND_AGREEMENT]
    if not bad:
        return 0
    res = await db.execute(update(UserProfile).where(
        UserProfile.user_id.in_(bad), UserProfile.analyst_enabled.is_(True),
        (UserProfile.analyst_suspended_until.is_(None)) | (UserProfile.analyst_suspended_until < now))
        .values(analyst_suspended_until=now + timedelta(days=acs.ANALYST_SUSPEND_DAYS)))
    return res.rowcount or 0


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ lecture

async def pending_for(db: AsyncSession, user: User) -> list[dict]:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    rows = (await db.execute(select(ReviewVote).where(
        ReviewVote.reviewer_id == user.id, ReviewVote.status == "pending", ReviewVote.expires_at > now)
        .order_by(ReviewVote.expires_at))).scalars().all()
    return [{"id": str(v.id), "level_db": v.level_db, "distance_m": round(v.distance_m, -1),
             "measured_at": v.measured_at, "expires_at": v.expires_at} for v in rows]


async def history_for(db: AsyncSession, user: User, limit: int) -> list[dict]:
    rows = (await db.execute(select(ReviewVote, MeasurementReview.outcome)
                             .join(MeasurementReview, MeasurementReview.id == ReviewVote.review_id)
                             .where(ReviewVote.reviewer_id == user.id, ReviewVote.status.in_(["voted", "expired"]))
                             .order_by(ReviewVote.created_at.desc()).limit(limit))).all()
    return [{"id": str(v.id), "vote": v.vote, "strong": v.strong, "level_db": v.level_db, "outcome": o,
             "matched": v.matched, "voted_at": v.voted_at or v.expires_at, "status": v.status} for v, o in rows]


async def analysts_admin(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> list[dict]:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    rows = (await db.execute(select(UserProfile, User.pseudonym).join(User, User.id == UserProfile.user_id).where(
        UserProfile.tenant_id == tenant_id, UserProfile.analyst_enabled.is_(True)))).all()
    agr = await agreement_bulk(db, tenant_id, [p.user_id for p, _ in rows], now) if rows else {}
    return [{"user_id": str(p.user_id), "pseudonym": ps, "display_name": p.display_name, "since": p.analyst_since,
             "suspended_until": p.analyst_suspended_until, "votes": agr.get(p.user_id, (0, 0))[1],
             "agreement": round((agr.get(p.user_id, (0, 0))[0] + 3) / (agr.get(p.user_id, (0, 0))[1] + 5), 3)}
            for p, ps in rows]

import math
import uuid
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, time, timedelta, timezone

import h3
from sqlalchemy import or_, select, text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.road_settings import road_settings as rs
from app.models.enums import DayPart, MissionStatus
from app.models.quota import Mission
from app.services.acoustics import clamp01
from app.services.geo import TZ, to_local
from app.services.quota import haversine_m

_ACTIVE = (MissionStatus.offered, MissionStatus.accepted)

_CAND = text("""
SELECT s.id, s.highway, s.name, s.ref, s.road_rank, s.class_weight, s.length_m, s.mid_lat, s.mid_lon
FROM road_segments s
WHERE s.active AND s.road_rank <= :max_rank
  AND ST_DWithin(s.geom, ST_SetSRID(ST_MakePoint(:lon, :lat), 4326), :deg)
ORDER BY s.class_weight DESC, s.length_m DESC
LIMIT :lim""")

_COUNTS = text("""
SELECT m.road_segment_id, m.day_part, count(*) AS n
FROM measurements m JOIN road_segments s ON s.id = m.road_segment_id
WHERE m.tenant_id = :tid AND m.measured_at >= :since AND m.quality_status IN ('validated', 'indicative')
  AND s.active AND s.road_rank <= :max_rank
  AND ST_DWithin(s.geom, ST_SetSRID(ST_MakePoint(:lon, :lat), 4326), :deg)
GROUP BY m.road_segment_id, m.day_part""")


@dataclass
class Candidate:
    id: uuid.UUID
    highway: str
    name: str | None
    ref: str | None
    rank: int
    weight: float
    length_m: float
    lat: float
    lon: float
    counts: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ créneaux horaires

def _bounds() -> dict[str, tuple[int, int]]:
    return {"morning": (settings.DAYPART_MORNING_START, settings.DAYPART_AFTERNOON_START),
            "afternoon": (settings.DAYPART_AFTERNOON_START, settings.DAYPART_EVENING_START),
            "evening": (settings.DAYPART_EVENING_START, settings.DAYPART_NIGHT_START),
            "night": (settings.DAYPART_NIGHT_START, settings.DAYPART_MORNING_START)}


def next_window(now: datetime, part: str) -> tuple[datetime, datetime]:
    """Créneau en cours ou prochain créneau de la plage horaire (UTC)."""
    local = to_local(now)
    s_h, e_h = _bounds()[part]
    for off in (-1, 0, 1):
        d0 = local.date() + timedelta(days=off)
        start = datetime.combine(d0, time(s_h), TZ)
        end = datetime.combine(d0 + timedelta(days=1 if e_h <= s_h else 0), time(e_h), TZ)
        if end > local:
            return max(start, local).astimezone(timezone.utc), end.astimezone(timezone.utc)
    raise RuntimeError("window_not_found")


def eligible_windows(now: datetime) -> dict[str, tuple[datetime, datetime]]:
    out = {}
    for part in _bounds():
        start, end = next_window(now, part)
        if start - now > timedelta(hours=rs.ROAD_LOOKAHEAD_HOURS):
            continue
        if end - max(start, now) < timedelta(minutes=rs.ROAD_MIN_WINDOW_MINUTES):
            continue
        out[part] = (start, end)
    return out


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ besoins

def slots(c: Candidate, wins: dict) -> list[tuple[float, str]]:
    """Besoin de mesure (0-1) du tronçon pour chaque plage encore atteignable, du plus fort au plus faible."""
    pw = rs.part_weights
    out = []
    for part in wins:
        gap = clamp01(1.0 - c.counts.get(part, 0) / max(1, rs.ROAD_TARGET_PER_PART))
        need = c.weight * pw.get(part, 1.0) * gap
        if need >= rs.ROAD_MIN_NEED:
            out.append((need, part))
    out.sort(reverse=True)
    return out


def _deg(radius_m: float) -> float:
    return radius_m / 111320.0 * 1.6


async def load_candidates(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, lat: float, lon: float, radius_km: float,
                          now: datetime) -> list[Candidate]:
    p = {"max_rank": rs.ROAD_MISSION_MAX_RANK, "lat": lat, "lon": lon, "deg": _deg(radius_km * 1000)}
    rows = (await db.execute(_CAND, {**p, "lim": rs.ROAD_CANDIDATE_LIMIT})).all()
    cands = {r[0]: Candidate(r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3], r[4], r[5], r[6], r[7], r[8]) for r in rows}
    if not cands:
        return []
    since = now - timedelta(days=rs.ROAD_COVERAGE_WINDOW_DAYS)
    for sid, part, n in (await db.execute(_COUNTS, {**p, "tid": tenant_id, "since": since})).all():
        c = cands.get(sid)
        if c is not None:
            c.counts[str(part)] = int(n)
    return list(cands.values())


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ affectation

def _make(tenant_id, p, c: Candidate, part: str, win, d_km: float, need: float, fit: float) -> Mission:
    start, end = win
    reward = round(rs.ROAD_MISSION_COUNT * rs.ROAD_REWARD_PER_MEASUREMENT * (0.75 + 0.5 * need)
                   * (0.8 + 0.4 * c.weight), 1)
    return Mission(
        tenant_id=tenant_id, user_id=p.user_id, kind="road", status=MissionStatus.offered,
        zone_h3=h3.latlng_to_cell(c.lat, c.lon, settings.MISSION_ZONE_H3_RES),
        center_lat=c.lat, center_lon=c.lon, radius_m=rs.ROAD_TARGET_RADIUS_M, distance_km=round(d_km, 1),
        required_count=rs.ROAD_MISSION_COUNT, done_count=0, confirmed_count=0, reward_credits=reward,
        priority=round(100 * clamp01(0.65 * need + 0.35 * fit), 1),
        features={"segment_need": round(need, 3), "fit": round(fit, 3), "score": round(p.score, 3),
                  "length_m": c.length_m},
        road_segment_id=c.id, road_name=c.name or c.ref, road_class=c.highway, target_day_part=DayPart(part),
        window_start_at=start, window_end_at=end, offer_expires_at=end)


async def assign(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, profs: dict, now: datetime, max_new: int) -> list[Mission]:
    """Propose à chaque utilisateur (position connue) des points précis sur des routes qui manquent de mesures
    pour une plage horaire atteignable. Les profils sont typés comme missions.Profile (user_id, anchor, score, load)."""
    elig = [p for p in profs.values() if p.anchor is not None and p.score >= settings.MISSION_MIN_SCORE]
    wins = eligible_windows(now)
    if not elig or not wins:
        return []
    rows = (await db.execute(select(Mission.user_id, Mission.road_segment_id, Mission.target_day_part,
                                    Mission.status).where(
        Mission.tenant_id == tenant_id, Mission.kind == "road",
        or_(Mission.status.in_(_ACTIVE),
            Mission.created_at >= now - timedelta(hours=rs.ROAD_REPEAT_COOLDOWN_HOURS))))).all()
    committed: set = set()
    pairs: set = set()
    road_load: dict = defaultdict(int)
    for uid, sid, part, st in rows:
        pairs.add((uid, sid))
        if st in _ACTIVE:
            committed.add((sid, part.value if part else None))
            road_load[uid] += 1

    radius_km = rs.ROAD_MISSION_MAX_RADIUS_KM
    edge_km = h3.average_hexagon_edge_length(rs.ROAD_GROUP_H3_RES, unit="km")
    cache: dict = {}
    created: list[Mission] = []
    for p in sorted(elig, key=lambda x: x.score, reverse=True)[:rs.ROAD_MAX_USERS_PER_RUN]:
        uid = p.user_id
        cap = min(max_new, rs.ROAD_MAX_PENDING_PER_USER - road_load[uid], settings.MISSION_MAX_PENDING - p.load)
        if cap <= 0:
            continue
        gk = h3.latlng_to_cell(p.anchor[0], p.anchor[1], rs.ROAD_GROUP_H3_RES)
        if gk not in cache:                                    # une requête par groupe de proximité
            clat, clon = h3.cell_to_latlng(gk)
            cands = await load_candidates(db, tenant_id, clat, clon, radius_km + edge_km + 0.5, now)
            cache[gk] = [(c, s) for c in cands if (s := slots(c, wins))]
        ranked = []
        for c, sl in cache[gk]:
            if (uid, c.id) in pairs:
                continue
            pick = next(((n, part) for n, part in sl if (c.id, part) not in committed), None)
            if pick is None:
                continue
            d_km = haversine_m(p.anchor[0], p.anchor[1], c.lat, c.lon) / 1000
            if d_km > radius_km:
                continue
            need, part = pick
            fit = (1 - d_km / radius_km) ** 0.7
            ranked.append((need * (0.35 + 0.65 * fit) * (0.6 + 0.4 * p.score), need, fit, c, part, d_km))
        ranked.sort(key=lambda t: t[0], reverse=True)
        chosen: list[Candidate] = []
        for _, need, fit, c, part, d_km in ranked:
            if len(chosen) >= cap:
                break
            if (c.id, part) in committed:
                continue
            if any(haversine_m(c.lat, c.lon, o.lat, o.lon) < rs.ROAD_MIN_SPACING_M for o in chosen):
                continue
            chosen.append(c)
            created.append(_make(tenant_id, p, c, part, wins[part], d_km, need, fit))
            committed.add((c.id, part))
            pairs.add((uid, c.id))
            road_load[uid] += 1
            p.load += 1
    if created:
        db.add_all(created)
        await db.flush()
    return created


async def needs_near(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, lat: float, lon: float, radius_km: float,
                     limit: int) -> list[dict]:
    """Vue admin : routes qui manquent le plus de mesures autour d'un point, avec la plage conseillée."""
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    wins = eligible_windows(now)
    out = []
    for c in await load_candidates(db, tenant_id, lat, lon, radius_km, now):
        sl = slots(c, wins)
        d = haversine_m(lat, lon, c.lat, c.lon)
        if not sl or d > radius_km * 1000:
            continue
        need, part = sl[0]
        out.append({"segment_id": str(c.id), "name": c.name or c.ref, "highway": c.highway, "rank": c.rank,
                    "lat": c.lat, "lon": c.lon, "distance_m": round(d), "need": round(need, 3),
                    "best_day_part": part, "window_start_at": wins[part][0], "window_end_at": wins[part][1],
                    "counts_by_day_part": c.counts})
    out.sort(key=lambda r: r["need"], reverse=True)
    return out[:limit]

import hashlib
import json
import logging
import math
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from sqlalchemy import or_
from app.models.measurement import Measurement, MeasurementQuality
import h3
import httpx
from fastapi import HTTPException

from sqlalchemy import func, or_, select, text
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import insert as pg_insert
