import json
import math
import random
import uuid
from collections import Counter
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from itertools import combinations

from sqlalchemy import func, select, update
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.survey_settings import survey_settings as sv
from app.models.enums import ProfileStatus, QualityStatus
from app.models.survey import ProfileEvent, Survey, SurveyProfile, SurveyResponse
from app.services import catalog
from app.services.geo import ring
from app.services.survey_schema import (QuestionDef, SurveyDefinition, all_rules, check_rules, questions_of,
                                        summarize, validate_answers)

_USABLE = [QualityStatus.validated, QualityStatus.indicative]


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ fonctions pures

def _norm(ind, x: float) -> float:
    if ind.hi / max(ind.lo, 1.0) > 50:
        return math.log1p(max(x, 0)) / math.log1p(ind.hi)
    return (x - ind.lo) / (ind.hi - ind.lo) if ind.hi > ind.lo else 0.0


def encode(qs: list[QuestionDef], answers: dict) -> list[float]:
    v: list[float] = []
    for q in qs:
        ind, a = catalog.INDICATORS[q.indicator], answers.get(q.key)
        if q.kind == "single_choice":
            v += [1.0 if a == o.value else 0.0 for o in q.options]
        elif q.kind == "multi_choice":
            v += [1.0 if a and o.value in a else 0.0 for o in q.options]
        elif q.kind == "number":
            v.append(_norm(ind, float(a)) if a is not None else 0.0)
        elif q.kind == "scale":
            v.append((a - 1) / 4 if a is not None else 0.0)
        else:
            v.append(1.0 if a else 0.0)
    return v


def dist(a: list[float], b: list[float]) -> float:
    return math.sqrt(sum((x - y) ** 2 for x, y in zip(a, b)))


def candidate_values(q: QuestionDef) -> list:
    ind = catalog.INDICATORS[q.indicator]
    if q.kind == "single_choice":
        return [o.value for o in q.options]
    if q.kind == "multi_choice":
        vals = [o.value for o in q.options]
        return [[v] for v in vals] + [list(c) for c in list(combinations(vals[:6], 2))[:6]]
    if q.kind == "scale":
        return [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
    if q.kind == "yes_no":
        return [True, False]
    lo = max(q.min if q.min is not None else ind.lo, ind.lo)
    hi = min(q.max if q.max is not None else ind.hi, ind.hi)
    base = q.anchors or list(ind.anchors) or [lo + (hi - lo) * t for t in (0.05, 0.15, 0.3, 0.5, 0.8)]
    out = []
    for x in base:
        if lo <= x <= hi:
            out.append(int(round(x)) if ind.int_ else round(float(x), 2))
    return out or [lo]


def _bucket(x: float, lo: float, hi: float) -> str:
    t = (x - lo) / (hi - lo) if hi > lo else 0
    return "faible" if t < 0.34 else "moyen" if t < 0.67 else "élevé"


def axes_of(qs: list[QuestionDef], answers: dict) -> dict:
    out = {}
    for q in qs:
        if not q.axis or q.key not in answers:
            continue
        ind, a = catalog.INDICATORS[q.indicator], answers[q.key]
        if q.kind == "single_choice":
            out[q.axis] = next((o.label for o in q.options if o.value == a), str(a))
        elif q.kind == "yes_no":
            out[q.axis] = "oui" if a else "non"
        elif q.kind == "scale":
            out[q.axis] = _bucket(a, 1, 5)
        elif q.kind == "number":
            out[q.axis] = _bucket(math.log1p(a), 0, math.log1p(ind.hi)) if ind.hi / max(ind.lo, 1) > 50 \
                else _bucket(a, ind.lo, ind.hi)
    return out


def auto_label(qs: list[QuestionDef], answers: dict) -> tuple[str, str]:
    from app.services.survey_schema import describe_answer
    axis_qs = [q for q in qs if q.axis and q.key in answers] or [q for q in qs if q.key in answers]
    label = " · ".join(describe_answer(q, answers[q.key]) for q in axis_qs[:3])[:110]
    desc = "; ".join(f"{q.short or q.text}: {describe_answer(q, answers[q.key])}" for q in qs[:4] if q.key in answers)
    return label, desc[:290]


def kmedoids(vecs: list[list[float]], k: int, rng: random.Random, iters: int = 10):
    n = len(vecs)
    k = max(1, min(k, n))
    med = [rng.randrange(n)]
    while len(med) < k:
        med.append(max(range(n), key=lambda i: min(dist(vecs[i], vecs[m]) for m in med)))
    clusters: dict[int, list[int]] = {}
    for _ in range(iters):
        clusters = {m: [] for m in med}
        for i in range(n):
            clusters[min(med, key=lambda m: dist(vecs[i], vecs[m]))].append(i)
        new = [min(idx, key=lambda c: sum(dist(vecs[c], vecs[j]) for j in idx)) for idx in clusters.values() if idx]
        if set(new) == set(med):
            break
        med = new
    sizes = {m: len(idx) for m, idx in clusters.items()}
    return med, sizes


def generate_deterministic(defn: SurveyDefinition, slot: str, n: int, rng: random.Random,
                           existing: list[list[float]]) -> list[dict]:
    """Archétypes couvrant l'espace des réponses : tirage cohérent (règles) puis échantillonnage de diversité maximale."""
    qs = questions_of(defn, slot)
    rules = all_rules(defn)
    cands = {q.key: candidate_values(q) for q in qs}
    pool, seen = [], set()
    for _ in range(3000):
        combo = {q.key: rng.choice(cands[q.key]) for q in qs}
        key = json.dumps(combo, sort_keys=True)
        if key in seen or any(h["severity"] == "reject" for h in check_rules(rules, combo)):
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        pool.append(combo)
        if len(pool) >= 1200:
            break
    if not pool:
        return []
    vecs = [encode(qs, c) for c in pool]
    targets = {(q.key, o) for q in qs if q.axis and q.kind in ("single_choice", "yes_no")
               for o in (cands[q.key] if q.kind == "yes_no" else [x.value for x in q.options])}
    chosen: list[int] = []
    pts = list(existing)

    def covers(i):
        return {(q.key, pool[i][q.key]) for q in qs if q.axis and q.kind in ("single_choice", "yes_no")} & targets

    uncovered = set(targets)
    while uncovered and len(chosen) < n:
        i = max((i for i in range(len(pool)) if i not in chosen),
                key=lambda i: (len(covers(i) & uncovered),
                               min((dist(vecs[i], p) for p in pts), default=1.0)), default=None)
        if i is None or not (covers(i) & uncovered):
            break
        chosen.append(i)
        pts.append(vecs[i])
        uncovered -= covers(i)
    while len(chosen) < n and len(chosen) < len(pool):
        i = max((i for i in range(len(pool)) if i not in chosen),
                key=lambda i: min((dist(vecs[i], p) for p in pts), default=1.0))
        chosen.append(i)
        pts.append(vecs[i])
    out = []
    for i in chosen:
        label, desc = auto_label(qs, pool[i])
        out.append({"answers": pool[i], "axes": axes_of(qs, pool[i]), "label": label, "description": desc,
                    "origin": "deterministic", "meta": {}})
    return out


def cluster_candidates(defn: SurveyDefinition, slot: str, observed: list[dict], k: int, rng: random.Random) -> list[dict]:
    qs = questions_of(defn, slot)
    complete = [o for o in observed if all(q.key in o for q in qs if q.required)]
    if len(complete) < sv.SV_CLUSTER_MIN:
        return []
    sample = rng.sample(complete, min(400, len(complete)))
    vecs = [encode(qs, o) for o in sample]
    med, sizes = kmedoids(vecs, k, rng)
    total = sum(sizes.values()) or 1
    out = []
    for m in med:
        a = sample[m]
        label, desc = auto_label(qs, a)
        out.append({"answers": a, "axes": axes_of(qs, a), "label": label, "description": desc, "origin": "cluster",
                    "meta": {"support": sizes.get(m, 0), "support_share": round(sizes.get(m, 0) / total, 3)}})
    return out


def profile_valid(defn: SurveyDefinition, slot: str, answers: dict) -> dict | None:
    """Vérifie types, bornes, complétude et cohérence d'un profil ; renvoie les réponses normalisées ou None."""
    try:
        clean = validate_answers(defn, answers)
    except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
        return None
    qs = questions_of(defn, slot)
    if any(k not in {q.key for q in qs} for k in clean) or any(q.required and q.key not in clean for q in qs):
        return None
    if any(h["severity"] == "reject" for h in check_rules(all_rules(defn), clean)):
        return None
    return clean


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ accès données

async def active_profiles(db: AsyncSession, survey_id: uuid.UUID, slot: str | None = None) -> list[SurveyProfile]:
    q = select(SurveyProfile).where(SurveyProfile.survey_id == survey_id, SurveyProfile.status == ProfileStatus.active)
    if slot:
        q = q.where(SurveyProfile.slot == slot)
    return list((await db.execute(q.order_by(SurveyProfile.created_at))).scalars())


async def _observed(db: AsyncSession, survey: Survey, slot: str, *, only_residual: bool = False) -> list[dict]:
    q = select(SurveyResponse.answers, SurveyResponse.none_slots, SurveyResponse.overrides).where(
        SurveyResponse.survey_id == survey.id, SurveyResponse.quality_status.in_(_USABLE),
        SurveyResponse.survey_version == survey.current_version).order_by(SurveyResponse.submitted_at.desc()).limit(2000)
    rows = (await db.execute(q)).all()
    out = []
    for answers, none_slots, overrides in rows:
        if only_residual and slot not in (none_slots or []) and not overrides:
            continue
        out.append(answers or {})
    return out


def _new(survey: Survey, tenant_id, slot: str, c: dict, gen: int) -> SurveyProfile:
    return SurveyProfile(tenant_id=tenant_id, survey_id=survey.id, slot=slot, key=uuid.uuid4().hex[:8],
                         label=c["label"][:120], description=(c.get("description") or "")[:300],
                         axes=c.get("axes") or {}, answers=c["answers"], origin=c["origin"],
                         status=ProfileStatus.active, generation=gen, meta=c.get("meta") or {})


def _far_from(vec, pts, thr) -> bool:
    return all(dist(vec, p) >= thr for p in pts)


async def generate_slot(db: AsyncSession, survey: Survey, defn: SurveyDefinition, slot: str, *, n: int | None = None,
                        use_ai: bool = False, rng: random.Random | None = None) -> list[SurveyProfile]:
    from app.services import survey_ai
    rng = rng or random.Random()
    qs = questions_of(defn, slot)
    want = n or sv.SV_PROFILES_PER_SLOT
    existing = await active_profiles(db, survey.id, slot)
    room = max(0, min(want, sv.SV_PROFILES_MAX - len(existing)))
    if room == 0:
        return []
    pts = [encode(qs, p.answers) for p in existing]
    thr = sv.SV_NEW_PROFILE_MIN_DIST * math.sqrt(max(1, len(qs)))
    cands: list[dict] = []
    observed = await _observed(db, survey, slot)
    cands += cluster_candidates(defn, slot, observed, min(room, sv.SV_CLUSTER_K), rng)
    if use_ai:
        cands += await survey_ai.propose_profiles(defn, survey, slot, existing, need=room, observed_n=len(observed))
    if len(cands) < room:
        cands += generate_deterministic(defn, slot, room - len(cands) + 2, rng, pts)
    added: list[SurveyProfile] = []
    for c in cands:
        clean = profile_valid(defn, slot, c["answers"])
        if clean is None:
            continue
        c["answers"] = clean
        c["axes"] = c.get("axes") or axes_of(qs, clean)
        if not c.get("label"):
            c["label"], c["description"] = auto_label(qs, clean)
        v = encode(qs, clean)
        if not _far_from(v, pts, thr):
            continue
        pts.append(v)
        added.append(_new(survey, survey.tenant_id, slot, c, survey.profile_generation))
        if len(added) >= room:
            break
    db.add_all(added)
    await db.flush()
    return added


async def ensure_profiles(db: AsyncSession, survey: Survey, defn: SurveyDefinition, *, use_ai: bool = False) -> int:
    total = 0
    for s in defn.slots:
        if s.mode != "profile":
            continue
        have = len(await active_profiles(db, survey.id, s.key))
        if have < sv.SV_PROFILES_MIN:
            total += len(await generate_slot(db, survey, defn, s.key, n=sv.SV_PROFILES_PER_SLOT - have, use_ai=use_ai))
    return total


async def reconcile_after_update(db: AsyncSession, survey: Survey, defn: SurveyDefinition) -> int:
    """Nouvelle version de l'enquête : les profils devenus invalides sont retirés."""
    retired = 0
    slots = {s.key: s for s in defn.slots}
    for p in await active_profiles(db, survey.id):
        s = slots.get(p.slot)
        if s is None or s.mode != "profile" or profile_valid(defn, p.slot, p.answers) is None:
            p.status, p.retired_at = ProfileStatus.retired, datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            retired += 1
    await db.flush()
    return retired


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ évolution (profils hauts / bas, résidus)

def _coverage_ok(defn: SurveyDefinition, slot: str, profiles: list[SurveyProfile]) -> bool:
    qs = questions_of(defn, slot)
    for q in qs:
        if q.axis and q.kind == "single_choice":
            present = {p.answers.get(q.key) for p in profiles}
            if not {o.value for o in q.options} <= present:
                return False
    return True


async def evolve(db: AsyncSession, survey: Survey, defn: SurveyDefinition, *, use_ai: bool = True,
                 rng: random.Random | None = None) -> dict:
    from app.services import survey_ai
    rng = rng or random.Random()
    gen = survey.profile_generation + 1
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    summary: dict = {"generation": gen, "slots": {}}
    tot_added = tot_retired = 0
    for s in defn.slots:
        if s.mode != "profile":
            continue
        qs = questions_of(defn, s.key)
        profs = await active_profiles(db, survey.id, s.key)
        observed_all = await _observed(db, survey, s.key)
        residual = await _observed(db, survey, s.key, only_residual=True)
        none_rate = len(residual) / max(1, len(observed_all))
        # 1) profils bas : part de choix très inférieure à la part uniforme
        total_picks = sum(p.picked_count for p in profs) or 0
        retired: list[SurveyProfile] = []
        if total_picks >= sv.SV_RETIRE_MIN_SHOWN and len(profs) > sv.SV_PROFILES_MIN:
            uniform = 1 / len(profs)
            for p in sorted(profs, key=lambda p: p.picked_count):
                if p.meta.get("pinned") or p.shown_count < sv.SV_RETIRE_MIN_SHOWN:
                    continue
                if p.picked_count / total_picks >= sv.SV_RETIRE_RELATIVE * uniform:
                    continue
                keep = [x for x in profs if x.id != p.id and x not in retired]
                if len(keep) < sv.SV_PROFILES_MIN or not _coverage_ok(defn, s.key, keep):
                    continue
                retired.append(p)
                profs = keep
        for p in retired:
            p.status, p.retired_at = ProfileStatus.retired, now
        # 2) profils manquants : clusters des réponses résiduelles + (option) IA
        pts = [encode(qs, p.answers) for p in profs]
        thr = sv.SV_NEW_PROFILE_MIN_DIST * math.sqrt(max(1, len(qs)))
        room = max(0, sv.SV_PROFILES_MAX - len(profs))
        cands = cluster_candidates(defn, s.key, residual, min(sv.SV_CLUSTER_K, room), rng) if room else []
        want_ai = use_ai and room and (none_rate >= sv.SV_NONE_RATE_TRIGGER or len(profs) < sv.SV_PROFILES_MIN)
        if want_ai:
            cands += await survey_ai.propose_profiles(defn, survey, s.key, profs, need=min(room, 4),
                                                      observed_n=len(observed_all), residual=cands, none_rate=none_rate)
        if len(profs) + len(cands) < sv.SV_PROFILES_MIN:
            cands += generate_deterministic(defn, s.key, sv.SV_PROFILES_MIN - len(profs), rng, pts)
        added = []
        for c in cands:
            clean = profile_valid(defn, s.key, c["answers"])
            if clean is None:
                continue
            v = encode(qs, clean)
            if not _far_from(v, pts, thr) or len(profs) + len(added) >= sv.SV_PROFILES_MAX:
                continue
            pts.append(v)
            c["answers"] = clean
            c["axes"] = c.get("axes") or axes_of(qs, clean)
            if not c.get("label"):
                c["label"], c["description"] = auto_label(qs, clean)
            added.append(_new(survey, survey.tenant_id, s.key, c, gen))
        db.add_all(added)
        tot_added += len(added)
        tot_retired += len(retired)
        summary["slots"][s.key] = {"none_rate": round(none_rate, 3), "retired": [p.label for p in retired],
                                   "added": [{"label": a.label, "origin": a.origin} for a in added],
                                   "kept": len(profs)}
    survey.profile_generation = gen
    survey.last_evolved_at = now
    n_resp = (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(SurveyResponse).where(
        SurveyResponse.survey_id == survey.id))).scalar_one()
    survey.responses_at_evolution = n_resp
    db.add(ProfileEvent(tenant_id=survey.tenant_id, survey_id=survey.id, generation=gen,
                        method="ai+data" if use_ai else "data", added=tot_added, retired=tot_retired, details=summary))
    await db.flush()
    return summary


async def due_for_evolution(db: AsyncSession, survey: Survey) -> bool:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if survey.last_evolved_at and now - survey.last_evolved_at < timedelta(hours=sv.SV_EVOLVE_INTERVAL_HOURS):
        return False
    n = (await db.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(SurveyResponse).where(
        SurveyResponse.survey_id == survey.id))).scalar_one()
    return n - survey.responses_at_evolution >= sv.SV_EVOLVE_MIN_NEW


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ classement personnalisé

async def _affinity(db: AsyncSession, user_id: uuid.UUID) -> tuple[Counter, int]:
    rows = (await db.execute(select(SurveyResponse.profile_picks).where(SurveyResponse.user_id == user_id)
                             .order_by(SurveyResponse.submitted_at.desc()).limit(20))).all()
    ids = {uuid.UUID(i) for (picks,) in rows for lst in (picks or {}).values() for i in lst}
    if not ids:
        return Counter(), 0
    axes = (await db.execute(select(SurveyProfile.axes).where(SurveyProfile.id.in_(ids)))).scalars().all()
    c: Counter = Counter()
    for ax in axes:
        for a, v in (ax or {}).items():
            c[(a, v)] += 1
    return c, len(rows)


async def ranked_profiles(db: AsyncSession, user_id: uuid.UUID, survey: Survey, slot: str, h3_index: str | None,
                          limit: int | None = None) -> list[SurveyProfile]:
    profs = await active_profiles(db, survey.id, slot)
    if not profs:
        return []
    n = len(profs)
    rng = random.Random(f"{user_id}:{survey.id}:{slot}:{datetime.now(timezone.utc).date()}")
    local: Counter = Counter()
    local_n = 0
    if h3_index:
        since = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=sv.SV_LOCAL_DAYS)
        rows = (await db.execute(select(SurveyResponse.profile_picks).where(
            SurveyResponse.survey_id == survey.id, SurveyResponse.h3_index.in_(ring(h3_index, 1)),
            SurveyResponse.submitted_at >= since, SurveyResponse.quality_status.in_(_USABLE)).limit(500))).all()
        for (picks,) in rows:
            ids = (picks or {}).get(slot) or []
            if ids:
                local.update(ids)
                local_n += 1
    aff, n_resp = await _affinity(db, user_id)
    scored = []
    for p in profs:
        g = rng.betavariate(p.picked_count + 1, max(0, p.shown_count - p.picked_count) + 1)
        lr = min(2.0, ((local[str(p.id)] + 1) / (local_n + n)) * n)
        ax = list((p.axes or {}).items())
        af = (sum(min(1.0, aff[(a, v)] / max(1, n_resp)) for a, v in ax) / len(ax)) if ax and n_resp else 0.0
        scored.append((g * (0.6 + 0.4 * lr) * (0.8 + 0.2 * af), p))
    scored.sort(key=lambda t: t[0], reverse=True)
    return [p for _, p in scored[:limit or sv.SV_SHOWN_MAX]]


def profile_out(defn: SurveyDefinition, p: SurveyProfile) -> dict:
    return {"id": str(p.id), "key": p.key, "slot": p.slot, "label": p.label, "description": p.description,
            "axes": p.axes, "origin": p.origin, "summary": summarize(defn, p.slot, p.answers),
            "support_share": (p.meta or {}).get("support_share")}


async def credit_offer(db: AsyncSession, offered: dict[str, list[str]], picked: dict[str, list[uuid.UUID]],
                       overridden_slots: set[str]) -> None:
    shown = [uuid.UUID(i) for ids in offered.values() for i in ids]
    if shown:
        await db.execute(update(SurveyProfile).where(SurveyProfile.id.in_(shown))
                         .values(shown_count=SurveyProfile.shown_count + 1))
    for slot, ids in picked.items():
        if not ids:
            continue
        vals = {"picked_count": SurveyProfile.picked_count + 1}
        if slot in overridden_slots:
            vals["override_count"] = SurveyProfile.override_count + 1
        await db.execute(update(SurveyProfile).where(SurveyProfile.id.in_(ids)).values(**vals))


################################################################################
# FICHIER: services/survey_quality.py
################################################################################

import statistics
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import func, select, update
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import insert as pg_insert
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.survey_settings import survey_settings as sv
from app.models.enums import EvidenceStatus, QualityStatus
from app.models.survey import (ReferenceValue, RespondentTrust, Survey, SurveyAnswer, SurveyEvidence,
                               SurveyResponse, SurveyResponseQuality, SurveyVersion)
from app.services import catalog
from app.services.acoustics import clamp01
from app.services.geo import cell_center, ring
from app.services.quality import QualityResult
from app.services.quota import haversine_m
from app.services.survey_schema import SurveyDefinition


@dataclass
class PeerStat:
    kind: str                         # num | cat
    n: int
    values: list = field(default_factory=list)
    counts: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass
class RefStat:
    kind: str                         # num | cat
    value: float
    category: str | None = None


@dataclass
class EvFacts:
    key: str
    dup_other: bool = False
    dup_elsewhere_self: bool = False
    time_bad: bool = False
    gps_bad: bool = False
    has_exif: bool = False

    @property
    def ok(self) -> bool:
        return not (self.dup_other or self.time_bad or self.gps_bad)


@dataclass
class Facts:
    values: dict                       # indicator_key -> valeur
    kinds: dict                        # indicator_key -> kind de question
    duration_seconds: float
    expected_seconds: float
    gps_accuracy_m: float
    speed_kmh: float | None
    fingerprint_dups: int
    scale_values: list
    yes_values: list
    own_median_duration: float | None
    own_n: int
    trust: float | None
    trust_n: int
    evidence_required: list
    evidence: dict                     # question_key -> [EvFacts]
    q_by_ind: dict                     # indicator_key -> question_key
    peers: dict
    refs: dict
    rule_warns: list


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ fonctions pures

def peer_agreement(ind: catalog.Indicator, value, st: PeerStat) -> tuple[float | None, dict | None]:
    if st.n < sv.SQ_PEER_MIN_SAMPLES:
        return None, None
    if st.kind == "num":
        med = statistics.median(st.values)
        mad = statistics.median(abs(v - med) for v in st.values)
        sigma = max(1.4826 * mad, 0.1 * abs(med), 0.02 * (ind.hi - ind.lo), 1e-9)
        z = abs(float(value) - med) / sigma
        if z <= sv.SQ_PEER_Z_MAX:
            return clamp01(1 - 0.5 * z / sv.SQ_PEER_Z_MAX), None
        return clamp01(0.5 - 0.1 * (z - sv.SQ_PEER_Z_MAX)), {"code": "peer_outlier", "z": round(z, 2),
                                                             "median": round(med, 2)}
    k = max(2, len(ind.cats)) if ind.kind == "category" else 2
    vals = value if isinstance(value, list) else [value]
    ps = [((st.counts.get(str(v), 0) + 1) / (st.n + k)) for v in vals]
    p = min(ps)
    ag = max(0.6, min(1.0, p * k) ** 0.5)
    info = {"code": "peer_unusual", "p": round(p, 3)} if p < 0.02 and st.n >= 20 else None
    return ag, info


def ref_agreement(ind: catalog.Indicator, value, ref: RefStat) -> float | None:
    import math
    if ref.kind == "num":
        x = float(value)
        if x <= 0 or ref.value <= 0:
            return None
        lr, soft, hard = abs(math.log(x / ref.value)), math.log(2), math.log(sv.SQ_REF_FACTOR)
        return 1.0 if lr <= soft else (0.3 if lr > hard else clamp01(1 - 0.7 * (lr - soft) / (hard - soft)))
    vals = value if isinstance(value, list) else [value]
    if ref.category not in vals and not (ind.kind == "share"):
        return 0.8
    return 1.0


def evaluate(f: Facts) -> QualityResult:
    reasons, sub = [], {}
    hard = cap = False

    def reject(code, **d):
        nonlocal hard
        hard = True
        reasons.append({"code": code, "severity": "reject", **d})

    # cohérence interne (avertissements de règles, les rejets sont bloqués dès la saisie)
    for w in f.rule_warns:
        reasons.append({"code": "rule_warning", "severity": "warn", "rule": w["id"], "message": w["message"]})
    sub["consistency"] = clamp01(1 - 0.25 * len(f.rule_warns))

    # rythme de saisie
    ratio = f.duration_seconds / max(f.expected_seconds, 1e-6) if f.duration_seconds < 7200 else 1.0
    if ratio < sv.SQ_TOO_FAST_HARD:
        reject("too_fast", ratio=round(ratio, 2))
        sub["timing"] = 0.0
    else:
        sub["timing"] = clamp01((ratio - 0.2) / 0.6)
        if sub["timing"] < 0.5:
            reasons.append({"code": "fast_answer", "severity": "warn", "ratio": round(ratio, 2)})

    # effort / uniformité
    eff = 1.0
    if len(f.scale_values) >= sv.SQ_STRAIGHT_MIN_SCALE and len(set(f.scale_values)) == 1:
        eff = 0.3
        reasons.append({"code": "straight_lining", "severity": "warn"})
    if len(f.yes_values) >= 6 and all(f.yes_values):
        eff = min(eff, 0.6)
        reasons.append({"code": "all_yes", "severity": "warn"})
    sub["effort"] = eff

    # lieu
    if f.gps_accuracy_m > sv.SQ_GPS_REJECT_M:
        reject("gps_accuracy_too_low", value=f.gps_accuracy_m)
        loc = 0.0
    elif f.gps_accuracy_m <= sv.SQ_GPS_GOOD_M:
        loc = 1.0
    else:
        loc = clamp01(1 - (f.gps_accuracy_m - sv.SQ_GPS_GOOD_M) / (sv.SQ_GPS_REJECT_M - sv.SQ_GPS_GOOD_M))
    if f.speed_kmh is not None:
        if f.speed_kmh > sv.SQ_SPEED_REJECT_KMH:
            reject("impossible_travel", speed_kmh=round(f.speed_kmh))
        elif f.speed_kmh > sv.SQ_SPEED_WARN_KMH:
            loc *= 0.4
            reasons.append({"code": "impossible_travel", "severity": "warn", "speed_kmh": round(f.speed_kmh)})
    sub["location"] = loc

    # recoupement avec les voisins
    ags = []
    for key, st in f.peers.items():
        ind = catalog.INDICATORS[key]
        ag, info = peer_agreement(ind, f.values[key], st)
        if ag is None:
            continue
        ags.append(ag)
        if info:
            reasons.append({"severity": "warn" if info["code"] == "peer_outlier" else "info", "indicator": key, **info})
            cap = cap or info["code"] == "peer_outlier"
    if ags:
        sub["peers"] = sum(ags) / len(ags)
    else:
        sub["peers"] = 0.7
        reasons.append({"code": "insufficient_peers", "severity": "info"})

    # recoupement avec les valeurs de référence fournies
    rags = []
    for key, ref in f.refs.items():
        ag = ref_agreement(catalog.INDICATORS[key], f.values[key], ref)
        if ag is not None:
            rags.append(ag)
            if ag < 0.5:
                reasons.append({"code": "reference_gap", "severity": "warn", "indicator": key})
    sub["reference"] = sum(rags) / len(rags) if rags else 0.8

    # preuves photo
    allev = [e for lst in f.evidence.values() for e in lst]
    if f.evidence_required:
        have = {k for k in f.evidence_required if any(e.ok for e in f.evidence.get(k, []))}
        share = len(have) / len(f.evidence_required)
        sub["evidence"] = share
        if share < 1:
            cap = True
            reasons.append({"code": "evidence_missing", "severity": "warn",
                            "questions": [k for k in f.evidence_required if k not in have]})
    else:
        sub["evidence"] = 1.0 if any(e.ok for e in allev) else 0.85
    for e in allev:
        if e.dup_other:
            reject("evidence_duplicate", question=e.key)
        if e.time_bad:
            reasons.append({"code": "evidence_time_mismatch", "severity": "warn", "question": e.key})
        if e.gps_bad:
            reasons.append({"code": "evidence_location_mismatch", "severity": "warn", "question": e.key})
        if e.dup_elsewhere_self:
            reasons.append({"code": "evidence_reused", "severity": "warn", "question": e.key})
    if any(not e.ok or e.dup_elsewhere_self for e in allev):
        sub["evidence"] = max(0.0, sub["evidence"] - 0.3)

    # réputation du répondant (calculée sur ses réponses passées uniquement)
    if f.trust is not None and f.trust_n >= 3:
        sub["reputation"] = f.trust
    else:
        sub["reputation"] = 0.6
        reasons.append({"code": "new_respondent", "severity": "info"})

    # comportement personnel et motifs de copie
    beh = 1.0
    if f.own_n >= 5 and f.own_median_duration and f.duration_seconds < 0.4 * f.own_median_duration:
        beh = 0.4
        reasons.append({"code": "behavior_shift", "severity": "warn"})
    if f.fingerprint_dups >= sv.SQ_FINGERPRINT_MAX:
        beh = min(beh, 0.2)
        cap = True
        reasons.append({"code": "copy_pattern", "severity": "warn", "count": f.fingerprint_dups})
    sub["behavior"] = beh

    w = {"consistency": sv.SQ_W_CONSISTENCY, "timing": sv.SQ_W_TIMING, "effort": sv.SQ_W_EFFORT,
         "location": sv.SQ_W_LOCATION, "peers": sv.SQ_W_PEERS, "reference": sv.SQ_W_REFERENCE,
         "evidence": sv.SQ_W_EVIDENCE, "reputation": sv.SQ_W_REPUTATION, "behavior": sv.SQ_W_BEHAVIOR}
    score = sum(w[k] * sub[k] for k in w)
    if hard:
        status, score = QualityStatus.rejected, 0.0
    elif score >= sv.SQ_VALIDATED_MIN and not cap:
        status = QualityStatus.validated
    elif score >= sv.SQ_INDICATIVE_MIN:
        status = QualityStatus.indicative
    else:
        status = QualityStatus.rejected
        reasons.append({"code": "low_score", "severity": "reject", "score": round(score, 3)})
    return QualityResult(status=status, score=round(score, 4),
                         sub_scores={k: round(v, 3) for k, v in sub.items()}, reasons=reasons)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ collecte des éléments de comparaison

async def _peers(db: AsyncSession, r: SurveyResponse, keys: list[str]) -> dict[str, PeerStat]:
    A = SurveyAnswer
    since = r.submitted_at - timedelta(days=sv.SQ_PEER_WINDOW_DAYS)
    rows = (await db.execute(select(A.indicator_key, A.value_num, A.value_cat).where(
        A.tenant_id == r.tenant_id, A.indicator_key.in_(keys), A.h3_index.in_(ring(r.h3_index, sv.SQ_PEER_RING_K)),
        A.status == QualityStatus.validated, A.answered_at >= since, A.response_id != r.id,
        A.contributor_hash != r.contributor_hash).limit(30000))).all()
    out: dict[str, PeerStat] = {}
    for key, num, cat in rows:
        ind = catalog.INDICATORS[key]
        st = out.setdefault(key, PeerStat("num" if ind.kind in ("number", "scale") else "cat", 0))
        if st.kind == "num":
            if num is not None and st.n < 600:
                st.values.append(num)
                st.n += 1
        elif ind.kind == "share":
            if num is not None and st.n < 600:
                st.counts[str(bool(num))] = st.counts.get(str(bool(num)), 0) + 1
                st.n += 1
        elif cat == "__all__":
            st.n += 1
        elif cat is not None:
            st.counts[cat] = st.counts.get(cat, 0) + 1
    return out


def _peer_value(ind, value):
    return value


async def _refs(db: AsyncSession, r: SurveyResponse, keys: list[str]) -> dict[str, RefStat]:
    rows = (await db.execute(select(ReferenceValue).where(
        ReferenceValue.tenant_id == r.tenant_id, ReferenceValue.indicator_key.in_(keys)))).scalars().all()
    import h3 as h3lib
    parents = {res: h3lib.cell_to_parent(r.h3_index, res) for res in range(5, 10)}
    best: dict[str, tuple[int, ReferenceValue]] = {}
    for v in rows:
        if v.scope_h3 is None:
            spec = 0
        elif v.scope_res and parents.get(v.scope_res) == v.scope_h3:
            spec = int(v.scope_res)
        else:
            continue
        cur = best.get(v.indicator_key)
        if cur is None or spec > cur[0] or (spec == cur[0] and (v.period_year or 0) > (cur[1].period_year or 0)):
            best[v.indicator_key] = (spec, v)
    out = {}
    for k, (_, v) in best.items():
        ind = catalog.INDICATORS[k]
        if ind.kind in ("number", "scale") and v.stat == "mean":
            out[k] = RefStat("num", v.value)
    return out


def _ev_facts(r: SurveyResponse, ev: SurveyEvidence, dup_other: bool, dup_self: bool) -> EvFacts:
    meta = ev.meta or {}
    f = EvFacts(ev.question_key, dup_other=dup_other, dup_elsewhere_self=dup_self, has_exif=bool(meta.get("taken_at") or meta.get("lat")))
    if meta.get("taken_at"):
        taken = datetime.fromisoformat(meta["taken_at"])
        delta = r.submitted_at - taken
        f.time_bad = delta < timedelta(minutes=-10) or delta > timedelta(hours=sv.SV_EVIDENCE_MAX_AGE_HOURS)
    if meta.get("lat") is not None and meta.get("lon") is not None:
        clat, clon = cell_center(r.h3_index)
        f.gps_bad = haversine_m(clat, clon, meta["lat"], meta["lon"]) > 900 + r.gps_accuracy
    return f


async def evaluate_response(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, response_id: uuid.UUID,
                            force: bool = False) -> tuple[QualityStatus, uuid.UUID | None] | None:
    r = await db.get(SurveyResponse, response_id, with_for_update=True)
    if r is None or r.tenant_id != tenant_id:
        return None
    qrow = (await db.execute(select(SurveyResponseQuality).where(SurveyResponseQuality.response_id == r.id))).scalar_one_or_none()
    if qrow is not None and qrow.evaluator == "admin":
        return qrow.status, r.user_id
    ver = (await db.execute(select(SurveyVersion).where(
        SurveyVersion.survey_id == r.survey_id, SurveyVersion.version == r.survey_version))).scalar_one()
    defn = SurveyDefinition.model_validate(ver.definition)
    qm = {q.key: q for q in defn.questions}
    values = {qm[k].indicator: v for k, v in r.answers.items() if k in qm}
    kinds = {qm[k].indicator: qm[k].kind for k in r.answers if k in qm}
    q_by_ind = {qm[k].indicator: k for k in r.answers if k in qm}
    ft = r.features or {}
    expected = (sv.SQ_BASE_SECONDS + sv.SQ_SECONDS_PER_SLOT * ft.get("n_slots_picked", 0)
                + sv.SQ_SECONDS_PER_OVERRIDE * ft.get("n_overrides", 0) + sv.SQ_SECONDS_PER_DIRECT * ft.get("n_direct", 0))

    # déplacement impossible depuis la réponse précédente
    speed = None
    if r.user_id:
        prev = (await db.execute(select(SurveyResponse.h3_index, SurveyResponse.submitted_at).where(
            SurveyResponse.user_id == r.user_id, SurveyResponse.submitted_at < r.submitted_at,
            SurveyResponse.id != r.id).order_by(SurveyResponse.submitted_at.desc()).limit(1))).first()
        if prev:
            (a_lat, a_lon), (b_lat, b_lon) = cell_center(prev[0]), cell_center(r.h3_index)
            d = max(0.0, haversine_m(a_lat, a_lon, b_lat, b_lon) - 400.0)
            gap_h = max((r.submitted_at - prev[1]).total_seconds() / 3600.0, 1 / 60)
            speed = d / 1000.0 / gap_h
    # comportement personnel
    durs, trust_row = [], None
    if r.user_id:
        durs = [d for (d,) in (await db.execute(select(SurveyResponse.duration_seconds).where(
            SurveyResponse.user_id == r.user_id, SurveyResponse.id != r.id,
            SurveyResponse.submitted_at < r.submitted_at).order_by(SurveyResponse.submitted_at.desc()).limit(20))).all()]
        trust_row = await db.get(RespondentTrust, r.user_id)
    # empreintes identiques (réponses personnalisées copiées par d'autres répondants)
    dups = 0
    if r.custom_fingerprint:
        dups = (await db.execute(select(func.count(func.distinct(SurveyResponse.contributor_hash))).where(
            SurveyResponse.tenant_id == tenant_id, SurveyResponse.survey_id == r.survey_id,
            SurveyResponse.custom_fingerprint == r.custom_fingerprint, SurveyResponse.id != r.id,
            SurveyResponse.contributor_hash != r.contributor_hash,
            SurveyResponse.submitted_at >= r.submitted_at - timedelta(hours=sv.SQ_FINGERPRINT_WINDOW_HOURS)))).scalar_one()
    # preuves
    evs = list((await db.execute(select(SurveyEvidence).where(SurveyEvidence.response_id == r.id))).scalars())
    evid: dict[str, list[EvFacts]] = {}
    for e in evs:
        dup_other = dup_self = False
        if r.user_id:
            others = (await db.execute(select(SurveyResponse.user_id).join(
                SurveyEvidence, SurveyEvidence.response_id == SurveyResponse.id).where(
                SurveyEvidence.sha256 == e.sha256, SurveyEvidence.id != e.id))).scalars().all()
            dup_other = any(u != r.user_id for u in others)
            dup_self = any(u == r.user_id for u in others)
        evid.setdefault(e.question_key, []).append(_ev_facts(r, e, dup_other, dup_self))
    required = [q.key for q in defn.questions if q.evidence == "required" and q.key in r.answers]
    # voisins et références
    keys = [k for k in values if catalog.INDICATORS[k].kind != "category" or True]
    peers = await _peers(db, r, keys)
    refs = await _refs(db, r, keys)
    scale_vals = [v for k, v in r.answers.items() if k in qm and qm[k].kind == "scale"]
    yes_vals = [v for k, v in r.answers.items() if k in qm and qm[k].kind == "yes_no"]
    extreme = [v in (1, 5) for v in scale_vals]
    facts = Facts(values=values, kinds=kinds, duration_seconds=r.duration_seconds, expected_seconds=expected,
                  gps_accuracy_m=r.gps_accuracy, speed_kmh=speed, fingerprint_dups=int(dups), scale_values=scale_vals,
                  yes_values=yes_vals, own_median_duration=statistics.median(durs) if durs else None, own_n=len(durs),
                  trust=trust_row.trust if trust_row else None, trust_n=trust_row.n_responses if trust_row else 0,
                  evidence_required=required, evidence=evid, q_by_ind=q_by_ind, peers=peers, refs=refs,
                  rule_warns=[h for h in (r.flags or []) if h.get("severity") == "warn"])
    res = evaluate(facts)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    await db.execute(pg_insert(SurveyResponseQuality).values(
        id=uuid.uuid4(), tenant_id=tenant_id, response_id=r.id, status=res.status, score=res.score,
        sub_scores=res.sub_scores, reasons=res.reasons, evaluator="auto", evaluated_at=now,
    ).on_conflict_do_update(index_elements=["response_id"], set_={
        "status": res.status, "score": res.score, "sub_scores": res.sub_scores, "reasons": res.reasons,
        "evaluator": "auto", "override_by": None, "evaluated_at": now}))
    trust_eff = facts.trust if facts.trust is not None else 0.6
    weight = round(res.score * (0.5 + 0.5 * trust_eff), 4) if res.status != QualityStatus.rejected else 0.0
    r.features = {**ft, "extreme_share": round(sum(extreme) / len(extreme), 3) if extreme else None,
                  "yes_share": round(sum(yes_vals) / len(yes_vals), 3) if yes_vals else None, "scale_n": len(scale_vals)}
    r.quality_status, r.weight, r.evaluated_at = res.status, weight, now
    proved = any(e.ok for lst in evid.values() for e in lst) and res.status != QualityStatus.rejected
    await db.execute(update(SurveyAnswer).where(SurveyAnswer.response_id == r.id).values(
        status=res.status, weight=weight, proved=proved))
    for e in evs:
        bad = any(not x.ok for x in evid.get(e.question_key, []))
        e.status = EvidenceStatus.rejected if bad else EvidenceStatus.accepted
    return res.status, r.user_id


async def apply_decision(db: AsyncSession, r: SurveyResponse, status: QualityStatus, admin_id: uuid.UUID,
                         reason: str) -> None:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    q = (await db.execute(select(SurveyResponseQuality).where(SurveyResponseQuality.response_id == r.id))).scalar_one_or_none()
    entry = {"code": "admin_override", "severity": "info", "reason": reason}
    if q is None:
        db.add(SurveyResponseQuality(tenant_id=r.tenant_id, response_id=r.id, status=status, score=0.0, sub_scores={},
                                     reasons=[entry], evaluator="admin", override_by=admin_id, evaluated_at=now))
    else:
        q.status, q.evaluator, q.override_by, q.evaluated_at = status, "admin", admin_id, now
        q.reasons = [*(q.reasons or []), entry]
    weight = {QualityStatus.validated: 1.0, QualityStatus.indicative: 0.5}.get(status, 0.0)
    r.quality_status, r.weight, r.evaluated_at = status, weight, now
    await db.execute(update(SurveyAnswer).where(SurveyAnswer.response_id == r.id).values(status=status, weight=weight))


################################################################################
# FICHIER: services/survey_schema.py
################################################################################

import math
from typing import Any, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, model_validator

from app.core.survey_settings import survey_settings as sv
from app.services import catalog

KEY_RE = r"^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{1,47}$"
SENSITIVE_AXES = {"ethnie", "religion", "opinion_politique", "politique", "appartenance_ethnique", "parti"}
UNITS = ("person", "household", "dwelling", "enterprise", "farm", "facility", "community")
QKind = Literal["single_choice", "multi_choice", "number", "scale", "yes_no"]
_CMP = {"eq", "ne", "lt", "lte", "gt", "gte"}


class OptionDef(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    value: str = Field(pattern=KEY_RE)
    label: str | None = Field(None, max_length=100)
    num: float | None = None


class QuestionDef(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    key: str = Field(pattern=KEY_RE)
    slot: str = Field(pattern=KEY_RE)
    text: str = Field(min_length=3, max_length=240)
    short: str | None = Field(None, max_length=60)
    help: str | None = Field(None, max_length=300)
    kind: QKind
    indicator: str
    required: bool = True
    options: list[OptionDef] = []
    min: float | None = None
    max: float | None = None
    evidence: Literal["none", "optional", "required"] = "none"
    evidence_hint: str | None = Field(None, max_length=200)
    allow_override: bool = True
    anchors: list[float] = []
    axis: str | None = Field(None, pattern=KEY_RE)
    combine: Literal["first", "sum", "mean", "max", "union"] | None = None


class SlotDef(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    key: str = Field(pattern=KEY_RE)
    label: str = Field(min_length=2, max_length=100)
    help: str | None = Field(None, max_length=300)
    multi: bool = False
    allow_none: bool = True
    mode: Literal["profile", "direct"] = "profile"


class RuleDef(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", populate_by_name=True)
    id: str = Field(pattern=KEY_RE)
    when: dict | None = None
    assert_: dict = Field(alias="assert")
    severity: Literal["reject", "warn"] = "warn"
    message: str = Field(max_length=200)


class SurveyDefinition(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    unit: Literal["person", "household", "dwelling", "enterprise", "farm", "facility", "community"] = "household"
    slots: list[SlotDef] = Field(min_length=1)
    questions: list[QuestionDef] = Field(min_length=1)
    rules: list[RuleDef] = []
    dimensions: list[str] = []

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _check(self):
        if len(self.slots) > sv.SV_MAX_SLOTS or len(self.questions) > sv.SV_MAX_QUESTIONS:
            raise ValueError("survey_too_large")
        slot_keys = [s.key for s in self.slots]
        qkeys = [q.key for q in self.questions]
        if len(set(slot_keys)) != len(slot_keys):
            raise ValueError("duplicate_slot")
        if len(set(qkeys)) != len(qkeys):
            raise ValueError("duplicate_question")
        if len({q.indicator for q in self.questions}) != len(self.questions):
            raise ValueError("duplicate_indicator")
        for s in self.slots:
            if not any(q.slot == s.key for q in self.questions):
                raise ValueError(f"empty_slot:{s.key}")
        for q in self.questions:
            if q.slot not in slot_keys:
                raise ValueError(f"unknown_slot:{q.key}")
            ind = catalog.get(q.indicator)
            if ind is None:
                raise ValueError(f"unknown_indicator:{q.indicator}")
            ok = (q.kind == "single_choice" or (q.kind == "multi_choice" and ind.multi)) if ind.kind == "category" \
                else q.kind == {"number": "number", "share": "yes_no", "scale": "scale"}[ind.kind]
            if not ok:
                raise ValueError(f"kind_mismatch:{q.key}")
            if ind.kind == "category":
                codes = {c.code: c for c in ind.cats}
                if not q.options:
                    q.options = [OptionDef(value=c.code) for c in ind.cats]
                if len(q.options) < 2 or len({o.value for o in q.options}) != len(q.options):
                    raise ValueError(f"bad_options:{q.key}")
                for o in q.options:
                    c = codes.get(o.value)
                    if c is None:
                        raise ValueError(f"option_not_in_indicator:{q.key}:{o.value}")
                    o.label = o.label or c.label
                    o.num = c.num if o.num is None else o.num
            elif q.options:
                raise ValueError(f"options_not_allowed:{q.key}")
            if ind.kind == "number":
                if (q.min is not None and q.min < ind.lo) or (q.max is not None and q.max > ind.hi):
                    raise ValueError(f"bounds_outside_indicator:{q.key}")
            if q.axis and q.axis in SENSITIVE_AXES:
                pass   # contrôlé au niveau du service (flag allow_sensitive_axes)
        for r in self.rules:
            if r.when is not None:
                _check_expr(r.when, set(qkeys))
            _check_expr(r.assert_, set(qkeys))
        return self


def _check_operand(o, qkeys, depth=0):
    if depth > 5 or not isinstance(o, dict) or len(o) != 1:
        raise ValueError("bad_operand")
    (k, v), = o.items()
    if k == "q":
        if v not in qkeys:
            raise ValueError(f"rule_unknown_question:{v}")
    elif k == "const":
        pass
    elif k in ("sum", "sub", "mul", "div"):
        if not isinstance(v, list) or not v or (k != "sum" and len(v) != 2):
            raise ValueError("bad_operand")
        for x in v:
            _check_operand(x, qkeys, depth + 1)
    else:
        raise ValueError("bad_operand")


def _check_expr(e, qkeys, depth=0):
    if depth > 6 or not isinstance(e, dict) or "op" not in e:
        raise ValueError("bad_expression")
    op = e["op"]
    if op in ("and", "or"):
        if not isinstance(e.get("args"), list) or not e["args"]:
            raise ValueError("bad_expression")
        for a in e["args"]:
            _check_expr(a, qkeys, depth + 1)
    elif op == "not":
        if not isinstance(e.get("args"), list) or len(e["args"]) != 1:
            raise ValueError("bad_expression")
        _check_expr(e["args"][0], qkeys, depth + 1)
    elif op == "answered":
        if e.get("q") not in qkeys:
            raise ValueError("bad_expression")
    elif op in _CMP:
        _check_operand(e.get("left"), qkeys)
        _check_operand(e.get("right"), qkeys)
    elif op in ("in", "not_in"):
        _check_operand(e.get("left"), qkeys)
        if not isinstance(e.get("right"), list):
            raise ValueError("bad_expression")
    elif op == "has":
        _check_operand(e.get("left"), qkeys)
        if "right" not in e:
            raise ValueError("bad_expression")
    else:
        raise ValueError("bad_expression")


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ évaluation des règles

def _operand(o, a):
    (k, v), = o.items()
    if k == "q":
        x = a.get(v)
        return int(x) if isinstance(x, bool) else x
    if k == "const":
        return v
    vals = [_operand(x, a) for x in v]
    if any(x is None or isinstance(x, (list, str)) for x in vals):
        return None
    if k == "sum":
        return sum(vals)
    if k == "sub":
        return vals[0] - vals[1]
    if k == "mul":
        return vals[0] * vals[1]
    return None if vals[1] == 0 else vals[0] / vals[1]


def eval_expr(e: dict, a: dict) -> bool | None:
    op = e["op"]
    if op in ("and", "or"):
        res = [eval_expr(x, a) for x in e["args"]]
        if op == "and":
            return False if any(r is False for r in res) else (None if any(r is None for r in res) else True)
        return True if any(r is True for r in res) else (None if any(r is None for r in res) else False)
    if op == "not":
        r = eval_expr(e["args"][0], a)
        return None if r is None else (not r)
    if op == "answered":
        return a.get(e["q"]) is not None
    left = _operand(e["left"], a)
    if left is None:
        return None
    if op in ("in", "not_in"):
        r = left in e["right"]
        return r if op == "in" else not r
    if op == "has":
        return isinstance(left, list) and e["right"] in left
    right = _operand(e["right"], a)
    if right is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(left, list) or isinstance(right, list) or isinstance(left, str) or isinstance(right, str):
        if op == "eq":
            return left == right
        if op == "ne":
            return left != right
        return None
    if op == "eq":
        return abs(left - right) < 1e-9
    if op == "ne":
        return abs(left - right) >= 1e-9
    return {"lt": left < right, "lte": left <= right + 1e-9, "gt": left > right, "gte": left >= right - 1e-9}[op]


def builtin_rules(defn: SurveyDefinition) -> list[RuleDef]:
    qk = {q.indicator: q.key for q in defn.questions}
    out = []
    for parts, whole, rel, msg in catalog.PART_RULES:
        if whole in qk and all(p in qk for p in parts):
            total = {"sum": [{"q": qk[p]} for p in parts]} if len(parts) > 1 else {"q": qk[parts[0]]}
            out.append(RuleDef(id=f"b_{parts[0]}_{rel}"[:48], when=None, severity="reject", message=msg,
                               assert_={"op": rel if rel == "eq" else "lte", "left": total, "right": {"q": qk[whole]}}))
    return out


def all_rules(defn: SurveyDefinition) -> list[RuleDef]:
    return [*builtin_rules(defn), *defn.rules]


def check_rules(rules: list[RuleDef], answers: dict) -> list[dict]:
    hits = []
    for r in rules:
        if r.when is not None and eval_expr(r.when, answers) is not True:
            continue
        if eval_expr(r.assert_, answers) is False:
            hits.append({"id": r.id, "severity": r.severity, "message": r.message})
    return hits


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ réponses

def questions_of(defn: SurveyDefinition, slot: str) -> list[QuestionDef]:
    return [q for q in defn.questions if q.slot == slot]


def coerce_answer(q: QuestionDef, raw: Any) -> Any:
    ind = catalog.INDICATORS[q.indicator]
    if q.kind == "number":
        if isinstance(raw, bool) or not isinstance(raw, (int, float)) or not math.isfinite(float(raw)):
            raise ValueError("not_a_number")
        x = float(raw)
        lo = max(q.min if q.min is not None else ind.lo, ind.lo)
        hi = min(q.max if q.max is not None else ind.hi, ind.hi)
        if x < lo or x > hi:
            raise ValueError("out_of_range")
        if ind.int_:
            if x != int(x):
                raise ValueError("not_integer")
            return int(x)
        return x
    if q.kind == "scale":
        if isinstance(raw, bool) or not isinstance(raw, (int, float)) or int(raw) != raw or not 1 <= int(raw) <= 5:
            raise ValueError("out_of_range")
        return int(raw)
    if q.kind == "yes_no":
        if not isinstance(raw, bool):
            raise ValueError("not_a_boolean")
        return raw
    values = [o.value for o in q.options]
    if q.kind == "single_choice":
        if not isinstance(raw, str) or raw not in values:
            raise ValueError("invalid_option")
        return raw
    if not isinstance(raw, list) or not raw or len(set(raw)) != len(raw) or any(x not in values for x in raw):
        raise ValueError("invalid_option")
    return [v for v in values if v in raw]


def validate_answers(defn: SurveyDefinition, answers: dict, *, partial: bool = False) -> dict:
    qm = {q.key: q for q in defn.questions}
    out = {}
    for k, raw in answers.items():
        q = qm.get(k)
        if q is None:
            raise ValueError(f"unknown_question:{k}")
        out[k] = coerce_answer(q, raw)
    return out


def merge_profiles(defn: SurveyDefinition, slot: str, answer_sets: list[dict]) -> dict:
    if len(answer_sets) == 1:
        return dict(answer_sets[0])
    out: dict = {}
    for q in questions_of(defn, slot):
        vals = [a[q.key] for a in answer_sets if q.key in a]
        if not vals:
            continue
        mode = q.combine or ("union" if q.kind == "multi_choice" else "mean" if q.kind in ("number", "scale") else "first")
        if mode == "union":
            lst = vals if q.kind == "multi_choice" else [[v] for v in vals]
            seen = {x for v in lst for x in v}
            out[q.key] = [o.value for o in q.options if o.value in seen]
        elif mode == "sum":
            out[q.key] = sum(vals)
        elif mode == "max":
            out[q.key] = max(vals)
        elif mode == "mean":
            m = sum(vals) / len(vals)
            ind = catalog.INDICATORS[q.indicator]
            out[q.key] = int(round(m)) if (ind.int_ or q.kind == "scale") else round(m, 2)
        else:
            out[q.key] = vals[0]
    return out


def answer_rows(defn: SurveyDefinition, answers: dict) -> list[dict]:
    rows = []
    qm = {q.key: q for q in defn.questions}
    for k, v in answers.items():
        q = qm[k]
        base = {"indicator_key": q.indicator, "question_key": k}
        if q.kind in ("number", "scale"):
            rows.append({**base, "value_num": float(v), "value_cat": None})
        elif q.kind == "yes_no":
            rows.append({**base, "value_num": 1.0 if v else 0.0, "value_cat": None})
        else:
            nums = {o.value: o.num for o in q.options}
            rows.append({**base, "value_num": None, "value_cat": "__all__"})
            for c in ([v] if q.kind == "single_choice" else v):
                rows.append({**base, "value_num": nums.get(c), "value_cat": c})
    return rows


def describe_answer(q: QuestionDef, v: Any) -> str:
    ind = catalog.INDICATORS[q.indicator]
    if q.kind == "yes_no":
        return "Oui" if v else "Non"
    if q.kind == "scale":
        return f"{v}/5"
    if q.kind == "number":
        return f"{v:g} {ind.unit}".strip()
    lab = {o.value: o.label for o in q.options}
    return ", ".join(lab.get(x, x) for x in ([v] if q.kind == "single_choice" else v))


def summarize(defn: SurveyDefinition, slot: str, answers: dict) -> list[dict]:
    return [{"question": q.short or q.text, "answer": describe_answer(q, answers[q.key])}
            for q in questions_of(defn, slot) if q.key in answers]


def definition_errors(defn: SurveyDefinition, domain: str, allow_sensitive: bool) -> list[str]:
    errs = []
    for q in defn.questions:
        ind = catalog.INDICATORS[q.indicator]
        if not catalog.allowed_for_domain(ind, domain):
            errs.append(f"indicator_wrong_domain:{q.indicator}")
        if q.axis in SENSITIVE_AXES and not allow_sensitive:
            errs.append(f"sensitive_axis_not_allowed:{q.axis}")
    for d in defn.dimensions:
        if d in SENSITIVE_AXES and not allow_sensitive:
            errs.append(f"sensitive_axis_not_allowed:{d}")
    return errs


def public_view(defn: SurveyDefinition) -> dict:
    return {"unit": defn.unit,
            "slots": [s.model_dump() for s in defn.slots],
            "questions": [{**q.model_dump(exclude={"anchors", "combine"}),
                           "unit": catalog.INDICATORS[q.indicator].unit,
                           "indicator_label": catalog.INDICATORS[q.indicator].label,
                           "min": q.min if q.min is not None else catalog.INDICATORS[q.indicator].lo,
                           "max": q.max if q.max is not None else catalog.INDICATORS[q.indicator].hi}
                          for q in defn.questions]}

# FICHIER: services/survey_trust.py

import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import insert as pg_insert
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.survey_settings import survey_settings as sv
from app.models.enums import QualityStatus
from app.models.survey import RespondentTrust, SurveyResponse, SurveyResponseQuality

PERSONAS = {"nouveau": "Nouveau répondant", "attentif": "Répondant attentif", "presse": "Répondant pressé",
            "uniforme": "Réponses très uniformes", "extreme": "Réponses tranchées",
            "incoherent": "Réponses souvent incohérentes", "regulier": "Répondant régulier"}
TIPS = {"presse": "Prenez le temps de lire les profils avant de choisir : les réponses trop rapides comptent moins.",
        "uniforme": "Variez vos réponses quand la situation le justifie : répondre pareil partout est signalé.",
        "extreme": "Nuancez : les échelles 1 et 5 ne sont pas toujours les plus justes.",
        "incoherent": "Vérifiez vos chiffres avant d'envoyer (totaux, nombres de personnes…).",
        "nouveau": "Quelques réponses fiables suffisent pour que votre confiance se construise.",
        "attentif": "Merci : vos réponses ont un poids élevé sur les cartes.",
        "regulier": "Merci pour votre régularité."}


def _rate(n, d):
    return n / d if d else 0.0


async def refresh_user(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, user_id: uuid.UUID) -> RespondentTrust | None:
    since = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=sv.ST_WINDOW_DAYS)
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(SurveyResponse.quality_status, SurveyResponse.features, SurveyResponse.submitted_at,
               SurveyResponse.survey_id, SurveyResponseQuality.reasons, SurveyResponseQuality.score)
        .join(SurveyResponseQuality, SurveyResponseQuality.response_id == SurveyResponse.id)
        .where(SurveyResponse.tenant_id == tenant_id, SurveyResponse.user_id == user_id,
               SurveyResponse.submitted_at >= since, SurveyResponse.quality_status != QualityStatus.pending)
        .order_by(SurveyResponse.submitted_at.desc()).limit(300))).all()
    n = len(rows)
    if n == 0:
        return None
    v = sum(1 for r in rows if r[0] == QualityStatus.validated)
    i = sum(1 for r in rows if r[0] == QualityStatus.indicative)
    codes = [{c.get("code") for c in (r[4] or [])} for r in rows]

    def cnt(*names):
        return sum(1 for s in codes if s & set(names))

    fast, uniform = _rate(cnt("too_fast", "fast_answer"), n), _rate(cnt("straight_lining", "all_yes"), n)
    dup_photo = _rate(cnt("evidence_duplicate", "evidence_reused"), n)
    shift, outlier = _rate(cnt("behavior_shift", "copy_pattern", "impossible_travel"), n), _rate(cnt("peer_outlier"), n)
    attempts = int(await redis.get(f"svq:att:{tenant_id}:{user_id}") or 0)
    incoh = min(1.0, attempts / max(5, n))
    ext = [r[1].get("extreme_share") for r in rows if (r[1] or {}).get("extreme_share") is not None]
    extremity = sum(ext) / len(ext) if ext else 0.0
    s, p0 = sv.ST_STRENGTH, sv.ST_PRIOR
    rel = (v + 0.5 * i + s * p0) / (n + s)
    penalty = 1 - min(0.6, 0.35 * uniform + 0.25 * fast + 0.3 * dup_photo + 0.3 * incoh + 0.2 * shift + 0.15 * outlier)
    trust = max(0.05, min(1.0, rel * penalty))
    if n < 3:
        persona = "nouveau"
    elif incoh > 0.4 or _rate(sum(1 for r in rows if r[0] == QualityStatus.rejected), n) > 0.4:
        persona = "incoherent"
    elif uniform > 0.3:
        persona = "uniforme"
    elif fast > 0.3:
        persona = "presse"
    elif extremity > 0.7 and len(ext) >= 5:
        persona = "extreme"
    elif trust >= 0.75 and len({r[3] for r in rows}) >= 3:
        persona = "regulier"
    else:
        persona = "attentif" if trust >= 0.6 else "nouveau"
    scores = [r[5] for r in rows]
    trend = (sum(scores[:5]) / len(scores[:5]) - sum(scores[5:15]) / len(scores[5:15])) if len(scores) >= 10 else 0.0
    feats = {"fast_rate": round(fast, 3), "uniform_rate": round(uniform, 3), "dup_photo_rate": round(dup_photo, 3),
             "shift_rate": round(shift, 3), "outlier_rate": round(outlier, 3), "inconsistent_attempts": attempts,
             "extremity": round(extremity, 3), "validated_rate": round(_rate(v, n), 3)}
    flags = [k for k, x in (("fast", fast > 0.3), ("uniform", uniform > 0.3), ("photo", dup_photo > 0.1),
                            ("shift", shift > 0.2), ("incoherent", incoh > 0.4)) if x]
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    await db.execute(pg_insert(RespondentTrust).values(
        user_id=user_id, tenant_id=tenant_id, trust=round(trust, 4), n_responses=n, persona=persona,
        trend=round(trend, 4), features=feats, flags=flags, updated_at=now,
    ).on_conflict_do_update(index_elements=["user_id"], set_={
        "trust": round(trust, 4), "n_responses": n, "persona": persona, "trend": round(trend, 4),
        "features": feats, "flags": flags, "updated_at": now}))
    return await db.get(RespondentTrust, user_id)


def view(t: RespondentTrust | None) -> dict:
    if t is None:
        return {"persona": "nouveau", "label": PERSONAS["nouveau"], "trust_band": "en construction",
                "tip": TIPS["nouveau"], "responses": 0}
    band = "élevée" if t.trust >= 0.75 else "bonne" if t.trust >= 0.55 else "à renforcer"
    return {"persona": t.persona, "label": PERSONAS.get(t.persona, t.persona), "trust_band": band,
            "trust": round(t.trust, 2), "trend": "en hausse" if t.trend > 0.03 else "en baisse" if t.trend < -0.03 else "stable",
            "tip": TIPS.get(t.persona), "responses": t.n_responses}

# FICHIER: services/tenant.py
################################################################################

from fastapi import HTTPException
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.security import hash_email, hash_password, make_pseudonym
from app.db.session import bind_tenant
from app.models.collection import TenantAlgorithmState
from app.models.enums import Role
from app.models.tenancy import Tenant, User
from app.schemas.schemas import TenantCreate, TenantUpdate
from app.services.collection import base_weights
from app.services.audit import audit
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import uuid


async def create_tenant(db: AsyncSession, payload: TenantCreate) -> Tenant:
    tenant = Tenant(id=uuid.uuid4(), slug=payload.slug, name=payload.name,
                    allow_registration=payload.allow_registration)
    db.add(tenant)
    try:
        await db.flush()
    except IntegrityError:
        await db.rollback()
        raise HTTPException(409, "slug_already_used")
    await bind_tenant(db, tenant.id)
    admin_id = uuid.uuid4()
    db.add(User(id=admin_id, tenant_id=tenant.id, email_hash=hash_email(tenant.id, payload.admin_email),
                password_hash=hash_password(payload.admin_password), role=Role.tenant_admin,
                pseudonym=make_pseudonym(tenant.id, admin_id)))
    db.add(TenantAlgorithmState(tenant_id=tenant.id, weights=base_weights(), samples_used=0,
                                updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc)))
    await audit(db, None, "tenant.create", "tenant", tenant.id, tenant_id=tenant.id)
    await db.commit()
    return tenant


async def list_tenants(db: AsyncSession) -> list[Tenant]:
    return list((await db.execute(select(Tenant).order_by(Tenant.created_at))).scalars())


async def update_tenant(db: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, payload: TenantUpdate) -> Tenant:
    tenant = await db.get(Tenant, tenant_id)
    if tenant is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "tenant_not_found")
    for field, value in payload.model_dump(exclude_none=True).items():
        setattr(tenant, field, value)
    await db.commit()
    return tenant
