# FICHIER: services/thresholds.py
################################################################################

from dataclasses import dataclass

from app.core.config import settings


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Thresholds:
    validated_min_score: float
    indicative_min_score: float
    gps_good_m: float
    gps_reject_m: float
    min_duration_ratio: float
    max_saturation_ratio: float
    max_movement_m: float
    laeq_min_db: float
    laeq_max_db: float
    level_tolerance_db: float
    future_tolerance_s: int
    max_age_hours: int
    neighbor_z_max: float
    neighbor_min_samples: int
    neighbor_window_days: int
    neighbor_sigma_floor_db: float
    uncalibrated_subscore: float
    neighbor_neutral: float
    allow_uncalibrated_validated: bool
    public_min_devices: int
    weights: dict


def build_thresholds(overrides: dict | None = None) -> Thresholds:
    o = overrides or {}

    def pick(key, default):
        v = o.get(key)
        return default if v is None else v

    return Thresholds(
        validated_min_score=pick("validated_min_score", settings.VALIDATED_MIN_SCORE),
        indicative_min_score=pick("indicative_min_score", settings.INDICATIVE_MIN_SCORE),
        gps_good_m=settings.GPS_GOOD_M,
        gps_reject_m=pick("gps_reject_m", settings.GPS_REJECT_M),
        min_duration_ratio=settings.MIN_DURATION_RATIO,
        max_saturation_ratio=settings.MAX_SATURATION_RATIO,
        max_movement_m=pick("max_movement_m", settings.MAX_MOVEMENT_M),
        laeq_min_db=settings.LAEQ_MIN_DB,
        laeq_max_db=settings.LAEQ_MAX_DB,
        level_tolerance_db=settings.LEVEL_CONSISTENCY_TOLERANCE_DB,
        future_tolerance_s=settings.TIMESTAMP_FUTURE_TOLERANCE_SECONDS,
        max_age_hours=settings.MAX_MEASUREMENT_AGE_HOURS,
        neighbor_z_max=settings.NEIGHBOR_Z_MAX,
        neighbor_min_samples=settings.NEIGHBOR_MIN_SAMPLES,
        neighbor_window_days=settings.NEIGHBOR_WINDOW_DAYS,
        neighbor_sigma_floor_db=settings.NEIGHBOR_SIGMA_FLOOR_DB,
        uncalibrated_subscore=settings.QUALITY_UNCALIBRATED_SUBSCORE,
        neighbor_neutral=settings.QUALITY_NEIGHBOR_NEUTRAL,
        allow_uncalibrated_validated=pick("allow_uncalibrated_validated", settings.ALLOW_UNCALIBRATED_VALIDATED),
        public_min_devices=pick("public_min_devices", settings.PUBLIC_MIN_DEVICES),
        weights={"gps": settings.QW_GPS, "duration": settings.QW_DURATION, "saturation": settings.QW_SATURATION,
                 "movement": settings.QW_MOVEMENT, "calibration": settings.QW_CALIBRATION,
                 "neighbor": settings.QW_NEIGHBOR},
    )


################################################################################
# FICHIER: services/timelapse.py
################################################################################

import hashlib
import json
from datetime import timedelta

from fastapi import HTTPException
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from sqlalchemy import Float, func, literal_column, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
import uuid
from sqlalchemy import or_
from app.models.measurement import Measurement, MeasurementQuality
from app.core.config import settings
from app.models.enums import DayPart
from app.models.geo import GeographicCell, NoiseAggregation
from app.models.measurement import Measurement
from app.schemas.schemas import TimeFilter
from app.services.geo import cell_margin_degrees, cell_polygon, period_dates, resolve_period
from app.services.map import statuses

_TEN = literal_column("10.0")
PARTS = [p.value for p in DayPart]


async def build(db: AsyncSession, redis: Redis, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, tf: TimeFilter, step: str,
                include_indicative: bool, bbox, min_devices: int) -> dict:
    start, end = resolve_period(tf)
    d_from, d_to = period_dates(start, end)
    days = [d_from + timedelta(days=i) for i in range((d_to - d_from).days + 1)]
    if step == "daypart":
        parts = [tf.day_part] if tf.day_part != "all" else PARTS
    else:
        parts = [None]
    if len(days) * len(parts) > settings.TIMELAPSE_MAX_FRAMES:
        raise HTTPException(422, "too_many_frames")

    raw = json.dumps([str(tenant_id), d_from.isoformat(), d_to.isoformat(), tf.day_part, step,
                      include_indicative, bbox, min_devices])
    key = f"t:{tenant_id}:tl:{hashlib.sha256(raw.encode()).hexdigest()}"
    hit = await redis.get(key)
    if hit:
        return json.loads(hit)

    stats = statuses(include_indicative)
    en, du = func.sum(NoiseAggregation.energy_sum), func.sum(NoiseAggregation.duration_total)
    cols = [NoiseAggregation.bucket_date, GeographicCell.h3_index,
            func.sum(NoiseAggregation.measurement_count), _TEN * func.log(en / du, type_=Float)]
    group = [NoiseAggregation.bucket_date, GeographicCell.h3_index]
    if step == "daypart":
        cols.insert(1, NoiseAggregation.day_part)
        group.insert(1, NoiseAggregation.day_part)
    stmt = (select(*cols).join(GeographicCell, GeographicCell.id == NoiseAggregation.cell_id)
            .where(NoiseAggregation.tenant_id == tenant_id, NoiseAggregation.bucket_date >= d_from,
                   NoiseAggregation.bucket_date <= d_to, NoiseAggregation.quality_status.in_(stats))
            .group_by(*group).limit(settings.TIMELAPSE_MAX_ROWS))
    if tf.day_part != "all":
        stmt = stmt.where(NoiseAggregation.day_part == DayPart(tf.day_part))
    if bbox:
        m = cell_margin_degrees()
        stmt = stmt.where(func.ST_Intersects(GeographicCell.center, func.ST_MakeEnvelope(
            bbox[0] - m, bbox[1] - m, bbox[2] + m, bbox[3] + m, 4326)))

    """
    eligible = None
    if min_devices > 1:                                   # k-anonymat : même règle que la carte
        conds = [Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.measured_at >= start,
                 Measurement.measured_at < end, Measurement.quality_status.in_(stats)]
        if tf.day_part != "all":
            conds.append(Measurement.day_part == DayPart(tf.day_part))
        sub = (select(Measurement.h3_index).where(*conds).group_by(Measurement.h3_index)
               .having(func.count(func.distinct(Measurement.contributor_hash)) >= min_devices))
        eligible = set((await db.execute(sub)).scalars())
    """
    eligible = None
    if min_devices > 1:
        conds = [Measurement.tenant_id == tenant_id, Measurement.measured_at >= start,
                 Measurement.measured_at < end, Measurement.quality_status.in_(stats)]
        if tf.day_part != "all":
            conds.append(Measurement.day_part == DayPart(tf.day_part))
        sub = (select(Measurement.h3_index)
               .outerjoin(MeasurementQuality, MeasurementQuality.measurement_id == Measurement.id)
               .where(*conds).group_by(Measurement.h3_index)
               .having(or_(
                   func.count(func.distinct(Measurement.contributor_hash)) >= min_devices,
                   func.bool_or(MeasurementQuality.evaluator == 'admin')
               )))
        eligible = set((await db.execute(sub)).scalars())
    frames: dict = {(d, p): [] for d in days for p in parts}
    index: dict[str, int] = {}
    lo, hi = 1e9, -1e9
    for row in (await db.execute(stmt)).all():
        if step == "daypart":
            d, part, h, n, laeq = row
            part = part.value
        else:
            d, h, n, laeq = row
            part = None
        if laeq is None or (eligible is not None and h not in eligible) or (d, part) not in frames:
            continue
        idx = index.setdefault(h, len(index))
        v = round(float(laeq), 1)
        lo, hi = min(lo, v), max(hi, v)
        frames[(d, part)].append([idx, v, int(n)])

    out = {"step": step, "min_db": None if lo > hi else lo, "max_db": None if lo > hi else hi,
           "cells": [{"h3": h, "polygon": cell_polygon(h)} for h in index],
           "frames": [{"t": d.isoformat(), "part": p, "cells": frames[(d, p)]} for d in days for p in parts]}
    await redis.set(key, json.dumps(out), ex=settings.TIMELAPSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS)
    return out


################################################################################
# FICHIER: services/tomtom.py
################################################################################

import hashlib
import json
import logging
import math
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from urllib.parse import quote

import httpx
import jwt
from fastapi import HTTPException
from redis.asyncio import Redis

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.security import decode_token

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# couches de tuiles autorisées (liste blanche : le front ne choisit jamais une URL TomTom)
TILE_LAYERS = {
    "flow": "/traffic/map/4/tile/flow/relative/{z}/{x}/{y}.png",
    "incidents": "/traffic/map/4/tile/incidents/s3/{z}/{x}/{y}.png",
}

_http: httpx.AsyncClient | None = None


class TomTomUnavailable(HTTPException):
    def __init__(self, detail: str = "tomtom_unavailable"):
        super().__init__(503, detail)


def _client() -> httpx.AsyncClient:
    global _http
    if _http is None:
        _http = httpx.AsyncClient(base_url=settings.TOMTOM_BASE_URL, timeout=settings.TOMTOM_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
                                  limits=httpx.Limits(max_connections=50, max_keepalive_connections=20))
    return _http


async def close_tomtom() -> None:
    global _http
    if _http is not None:
        await _http.aclose()
        _http = None


def _key() -> str:
    key = settings.TOMTOM_API_KEY.get_secret_value()
    if not key:
        raise TomTomUnavailable("tomtom_not_configured")
    return key


async def _request(path: str, params: dict | None = None, allow: tuple[int, ...] = ()) -> httpx.Response:
    key = _key()
    try:
        resp = await _client().get(path, params={**(params or {}), "key": key})
    except httpx.HTTPError:
        # on ne propage jamais l'exception httpx : son message contient l'URL donc la clé
        raise TomTomUnavailable("tomtom_unreachable")
    if resp.status_code in allow:
        return resp
    if resp.status_code in (401, 403):
        log.error("tomtom_key_rejected", extra={"task": path.split("/")[1] if "/" in path else path})
        raise TomTomUnavailable("tomtom_key_rejected")
    if resp.status_code == 429:
        raise TomTomUnavailable("tomtom_rate_limited")
    if resp.status_code >= 400:
        raise TomTomUnavailable("tomtom_upstream_error")
    return resp


async def _cached_json(redis: Redis, raw_key: str, ttl: int, loader):
    k = "tt:" + hashlib.sha256(raw_key.encode()).hexdigest()
    hit = await redis.get(k)
    if hit is not None:
        return json.loads(hit)
    data = await loader()
    await redis.set(k, json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False), ex=ttl)
    return data


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ tuiles
def issue_tile_session(user_id: uuid.UUID, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> dict:
    """Jeton opaque, court, limité aux tuiles (typ=tile : inutilisable comme access token)."""
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    exp = now + timedelta(seconds=settings.TOMTOM_TILE_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS)
    token = jwt.encode(
        {"sub": str(user_id), "tid": str(tenant_id), "typ": "tile", "jti": uuid.uuid4().hex, "iat": now, "exp": exp},
        settings.JWT_SECRET_KEY.get_secret_value(), algorithm=settings.JWT_ALGORITHM)
    prefix = settings.API_V1_PREFIX
    return {"expires_at": exp,
            "layers": {name: f"{prefix}/tomtom/tiles/{name}/{{z}}/{{x}}/{{y}}.png?t={token}" for name in TILE_LAYERS}}


def verify_tile_token(token: str) -> dict:
    try:
        return decode_token(token, "tile")
    except jwt.PyJWTError:
        raise HTTPException(401, "invalid_token")


async def fetch_tile(layer: str, z: int, x: int, y: int) -> bytes | None:
    template = TILE_LAYERS.get(layer)
    if template is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "unknown_layer")
    if not (0 <= z <= 22) or not (0 <= x < 2 ** z) or not (0 <= y < 2 ** z):
        raise HTTPException(422, "invalid_tile")
    resp = await _request(template.format(z=z, x=x, y=y), allow=(204, 404))
    return resp.content if resp.status_code == 200 else None


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ trafic
async def flow_point(redis: Redis, lat: float, lon: float) -> dict:
    async def load():
        resp = await _request("/traffic/services/4/flowSegmentData/absolute/10/json",
                              {"point": f"{lat},{lon}", "unit": "KMPH"}, allow=(400,))
        if resp.status_code == 400:                 # point trop éloigné d'une route
            return None
        f = resp.json()["flowSegmentData"]
        cur, free = float(f.get("currentSpeed", 0)), float(f.get("freeFlowSpeed", 0))
        congestion = max(0.0, min(1.0, 1 - cur / free)) if free > 0 else 0.0
        return {"current_speed_kmh": cur, "free_flow_speed_kmh": free,
                "current_travel_time_s": f.get("currentTravelTime"),
                "free_flow_travel_time_s": f.get("freeFlowTravelTime"),
                "confidence": f.get("confidence"), "road_closure": bool(f.get("roadClosure", False)),
                "congestion": round(congestion, 3),
                "level": "free" if congestion < 0.2 else "moderate" if congestion < 0.5 else "heavy"}

    data = await _cached_json(redis, f"flow:{lat:.4f}:{lon:.4f}", settings.TOMTOM_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS, load)
    if data is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "no_traffic_data")
    return data


_INCIDENT_FIELDS = ("{incidents{type,geometry{type,coordinates},properties{id,iconCategory,magnitudeOfDelay,"
                    "events{description,code,iconCategory},startTime,endTime,from,to,length,delay,roadNumbers}}}")


async def incidents(redis: Redis, bbox: tuple[float, float, float, float]) -> list[dict]:
    min_lon, min_lat, max_lon, max_lat = bbox
    if max_lon - min_lon > 0.8 or max_lat - min_lat > 0.8:
        raise HTTPException(422, "bbox_too_large")
    # bbox calée sur une grille de 0,05° : meilleur taux de cache, requête identique à la clé
    snap_lo = lambda v: math.floor(v * 20) / 20
    snap_hi = lambda v: math.ceil(v * 20) / 20
    b = (snap_lo(min_lon), snap_lo(min_lat), snap_hi(max_lon), snap_hi(max_lat))

    async def load():
        resp = await _request("/traffic/services/5/incidentDetails", {
            "bbox": f"{b[0]},{b[1]},{b[2]},{b[3]}", "fields": _INCIDENT_FIELDS,
            "language": settings.TOMTOM_LANGUAGE, "timeValidityFilter": "present"})
        out = []
        for f in resp.json().get("incidents", []):
            g, p = f.get("geometry") or {}, f.get("properties") or {}
            coords = g.get("coordinates") or []
            line = [coords] if g.get("type") == "Point" and coords else coords
            line = [[c[1], c[0]] for c in line if isinstance(c, list) and len(c) >= 2]
            if not line:
                continue
            mid = line[len(line) // 2]
            events = p.get("events") or []
            out.append({"id": p.get("id"), "category": p.get("iconCategory"),
                        "delay_magnitude": p.get("magnitudeOfDelay"),
                        "description": events[0].get("description") if events else None,
                        "from": p.get("from"), "to": p.get("to"), "delay_s": p.get("delay"),
                        "length_m": p.get("length"), "road_numbers": p.get("roadNumbers") or [],
                        "start": p.get("startTime"), "end": p.get("endTime"), "lat": mid[0], "lon": mid[1]})
        return out

    return await _cached_json(redis, "inc:" + ":".join(f"{v:.2f}" for v in b),
                              settings.TOMTOM_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS, load)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ recherche / géocodage
async def search(redis: Redis, query: str, lat: float | None, lon: float | None, limit: int) -> list[dict]:
    q = query.strip()
    params = {"limit": limit, "language": settings.TOMTOM_LANGUAGE, "countrySet": settings.TOMTOM_COUNTRY_SET}
    if lat is not None and lon is not None:
        params.update({"lat": round(lat, 3), "lon": round(lon, 3)})

    async def load():
        resp = await _request(f"/search/2/search/{quote(q, safe='')}.json", params)
        out = []
        for r in resp.json().get("results", []):
            pos, addr, poi = r.get("position") or {}, r.get("address") or {}, r.get("poi") or {}
            if "lat" not in pos or "lon" not in pos:
                continue
            out.append({"id": r.get("id"), "name": poi.get("name") or addr.get("freeformAddress") or q,
                        "address": addr.get("freeformAddress"), "municipality": addr.get("municipality"),
                        "category": (poi.get("categories") or [None])[0], "lat": pos["lat"], "lon": pos["lon"],
                        "distance_m": r.get("dist")})
        return out

    return await _cached_json(redis, f"s:{q.lower()}:{params.get('lat')}:{params.get('lon')}:{limit}",
                              settings.TOMTOM_SEARCH_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS, load)


async def reverse_geocode(redis: Redis, lat: float, lon: float) -> dict:
    async def load():
        resp = await _request(f"/search/2/reverseGeocode/{lat:.5f},{lon:.5f}.json",
                              {"language": settings.TOMTOM_LANGUAGE}, allow=(400, 404))
        if resp.status_code != 200:
            return None
        addrs = resp.json().get("addresses") or []
        if not addrs:
            return None
        a = addrs[0].get("address") or {}
        return {"address": a.get("freeformAddress"), "street": a.get("streetName"),
                "municipality": a.get("municipality"), "country": a.get("country")}

    data = await _cached_json(redis, f"rev:{lat:.4f}:{lon:.4f}", settings.TOMTOM_SEARCH_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS, load)
    if data is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "address_not_found")
    return data


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ itinéraire
async def route(o_lat: float, o_lon: float, d_lat: float, d_lon: float, mode: str) -> dict:
    resp = await _request(f"/routing/1/calculateRoute/{o_lat:.6f},{o_lon:.6f}:{d_lat:.6f},{d_lon:.6f}/json", {
        "traffic": "true", "travelMode": mode, "routeType": "fastest",
        "computeTravelTimeFor": "all", "language": settings.TOMTOM_LANGUAGE}, allow=(400,))
    if resp.status_code == 400:
        raise HTTPException(404, "route_not_found")
    routes = resp.json().get("routes") or []
    if not routes:
        raise HTTPException(404, "route_not_found")
    r = routes[0]
    pts = [[p["latitude"], p["longitude"]] for leg in r.get("legs", []) for p in leg.get("points", [])]
    step = max(1, len(pts) // 800)
    pts = pts[::step] + ([pts[-1]] if pts and (len(pts) - 1) % step else [])
    s = r.get("summary") or {}
    return {"length_m": s.get("lengthInMeters"), "travel_time_s": s.get("travelTimeInSeconds"),
            "traffic_delay_s": s.get("trafficDelayInSeconds", 0), "departure": s.get("departureTime"),
            "arrival": s.get("arrivalTime"), "points": pts}


################################################################################
# FICHIER: services/user.py
################################################################################

import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from fastapi import HTTPException
from sqlalchemy import delete, distinct, func, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.security import verify_password
from app.models.collection import CollectionProposal, CollectionSchedule
from app.models.enums import ConsentType, QualityStatus
from app.models.measurement import Measurement
from app.models.tenancy import User, UserConsent
from app.schemas.schemas import ConsentOut, ConsentUpdate, ContributionsOut, ScheduleIn
from app.services.privacy import round_locations


async def latest_consents(db: AsyncSession, user_id: uuid.UUID) -> dict[ConsentType, UserConsent]:
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(UserConsent).where(UserConsent.user_id == user_id)
        .order_by(UserConsent.consent_type, UserConsent.created_at.desc())
        .distinct(UserConsent.consent_type))).scalars()
    return {r.consent_type: r for r in rows}


async def list_consents(db: AsyncSession, user: User) -> list[ConsentOut]:
    current = await latest_consents(db, user.id)
    return [ConsentOut(consent_type=t, granted=c.granted, policy_version=c.policy_version, updated_at=c.created_at)
            for t, c in current.items()]


async def set_consent(db: AsyncSession, user: User, payload: ConsentUpdate) -> list[ConsentOut]:
    db.add(UserConsent(tenant_id=user.tenant_id, user_id=user.id, consent_type=payload.consent_type,
                       granted=payload.granted, policy_version=settings.CONSENT_POLICY_VERSION))
    await db.commit()
    return await list_consents(db, user)


async def require_consents(db: AsyncSession, user: User) -> None:
    current = await latest_consents(db, user.id)
    for t in (ConsentType.geolocation, ConsentType.microphone):
        if t not in current or not current[t].granted:
            raise HTTPException(403, "consent_required")


async def upsert_schedule(db: AsyncSession, user: User, payload: ScheduleIn) -> CollectionSchedule:
    schedule = (await db.execute(select(CollectionSchedule).where(CollectionSchedule.user_id == user.id))).scalar_one_or_none()
    data = payload.model_dump()
    data["timezone"] = data["timezone"] or settings.TIMEZONE
    try:
        from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
        ZoneInfo(data["timezone"])
    except Exception:
        raise HTTPException(422, "invalid_timezone")
    if schedule is None:
        schedule = CollectionSchedule(tenant_id=user.tenant_id, user_id=user.id, **data)
        db.add(schedule)
    else:
        for k, v in data.items():
            setattr(schedule, k, v)
    await db.commit()
    return schedule


async def get_schedule(db: AsyncSession, user: User) -> CollectionSchedule:
    schedule = (await db.execute(select(CollectionSchedule).where(CollectionSchedule.user_id == user.id))).scalar_one_or_none()
    if schedule is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, "schedule_not_set")
    return schedule


async def contributions(db: AsyncSession, user: User) -> ContributionsOut:
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(Measurement.quality_status, func.count()).where(
            Measurement.tenant_id == user.tenant_id, Measurement.user_id == user.id)
        .group_by(Measurement.quality_status))).all()
    by_status = {s: 0 for s in QualityStatus}
    by_status.update({s: n for s, n in rows})
    cells, last = (await db.execute(
        select(func.count(distinct(Measurement.h3_index)), func.max(Measurement.measured_at)).where(
            Measurement.tenant_id == user.tenant_id, Measurement.user_id == user.id,
            Measurement.quality_status != QualityStatus.rejected))).one()
    return ContributionsOut(total_measurements=sum(by_status.values()), by_status=by_status,
                            cells_covered=cells or 0, last_measurement_at=last)


async def erase_account(db: AsyncSession, user: User, password: str) -> None:
    """Droit à l'effacement : position arrondie, liens rompus, puis suppression du compte et des données associées."""
    if not verify_password(user.password_hash, password):
        raise HTTPException(403, "invalid_password")
    await round_locations(db, user.tenant_id, [Measurement.user_id == user.id])
    from sqlalchemy import update
    await db.execute(update(Measurement).where(
        Measurement.tenant_id == user.tenant_id, Measurement.user_id == user.id
    ).values(user_id=None, device_id=None, proposal_id=None))
    await db.execute(delete(CollectionProposal).where(CollectionProposal.user_id == user.id))
    from app.services.survey_ingest import erase_user_survey_data
    await erase_user_survey_data(db, user)
    await db.delete(user)   # consentements, appareils, planning, statistiques : suppression en cascade
    await db.commit()


################################################################################
# FICHIER: services/weather.py
################################################################################

import json
import math

import httpx
from redis.asyncio import Redis

from app.core.field_settings import field_settings as fs


async def wind_at(redis: Redis, lat: float, lon: float) -> dict | None:
    """Vent courant (Open-Meteo, sans clé), calé sur 0,1° et mis en cache : identique pour tous les utilisateurs."""
    la, lo = round(lat, 1), round(lon, 1)
    key = f"wx:{la}:{lo}"
    hit = await redis.get(key)
    if hit:
        return json.loads(hit)
    if await redis.exists(key + ":fail"):
        return None
    try:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=fs.WIND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS) as client:
            r = await client.get(fs.OPEN_METEO_URL, params={
                "latitude": la, "longitude": lo, "current": "wind_speed_10m,wind_direction_10m",
                "wind_speed_unit": "kmh", "timezone": "auto"})
        r.raise_for_status()
        cur = r.json()["current"]
        speed_kmh, dir_from = float(cur["wind_speed_10m"]), float(cur["wind_direction_10m"])
    except Exception:
        await redis.set(key + ":fail", "1", ex=60)
        return None
    to = math.radians(dir_from + 180.0)       # le vent souffle vers dir_from + 180
    ms = speed_kmh / 3.6
    data = {"speed_kmh": round(speed_kmh, 1), "speed_ms": ms, "dir_from": dir_from,
            "dir_to": (dir_from + 180.0) % 360.0, "u": math.sin(to) * ms, "v": math.cos(to) * ms,
            "observed_at": cur.get("time")}
    await redis.set(key, json.dumps(data), ex=fs.WIND_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS)
    return data

from app.core.redis import new_redis
from app.services import ai as ai_service
from app.tasks.celery_app import celery_app
from app.tasks.runtime import for_each_tenant, run


@celery_app.task(name="ai.anomaly_digest")
def anomaly_digest():
    async def _all():
        redis = new_redis()
        try:
            async def _job(db, tid):
                return await ai_service.anomaly_digest(db, redis, tid)
            return await for_each_tenant(_job)
        finally:
            await redis.aclose()
    return run(_all())

# FICHIER: tasks/measurement_tasks.py
################################################################################

import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import select

from app.core.config import settings
from app.models.enums import QualityStatus
from app.models.measurement import Measurement
from app.services import quality as quality_service
from app.tasks.celery_app import celery_app
from app.tasks.runtime import for_each_tenant, run, task_db


@celery_app.task(name="measurements.evaluate", autoretry_for=(Exception,), retry_backoff=True,
                 retry_kwargs={"max_retries": 5})
def evaluate(tenant_id: str, measurement_id: str):
    async def _job():
        tid = uuid.UUID(tenant_id)
        async with task_db(tid) as db:
            status = await quality_service.evaluate_measurement(db, tid, uuid.UUID(measurement_id))
            await db.commit()
        if status in (QualityStatus.validated, QualityStatus.indicative):
            celery_app.send_task("reviews.dispatch", args=[tenant_id, measurement_id], countdown=2)
        return status.value if status else None
    return run(_job())


@celery_app.task(name="measurements.sweep_pending")
def sweep_pending():
    """Reprend les mesures restées 'pending' (file perdue, worker arrêté)."""
    async def _job(db, tid):
        cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(seconds=60)
        ids = (await db.execute(select(Measurement.id).where(
            Measurement.tenant_id == tid, Measurement.quality_status == QualityStatus.pending,
            Measurement.created_at < cutoff).limit(500))).scalars().all()
        for mid in ids:
            await quality_service.evaluate_measurement(db, tid, mid)
            await db.commit()
        return len(ids)
    return run(for_each_tenant(_job))


@celery_app.task(name="measurements.reevaluate_model")
def reevaluate_model(tenant_id: str, device_model_id: str):
    """Réévalue les mesures récentes d'un modèle après changement de calibration (décisions admin et avis conservés)."""
    async def _job():
        tid = uuid.UUID(tenant_id)
        since = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=settings.REEVALUATION_DAYS)
        async with task_db(tid) as db:
            ids = (await db.execute(select(Measurement.id).where(
                Measurement.tenant_id == tid, Measurement.device_model_id == uuid.UUID(device_model_id),
                Measurement.measured_at >= since))).scalars().all()
            for mid in ids:
                await quality_service.evaluate_measurement(db, tid, mid, force=True)
                await db.commit()
            return len(ids)
    return run(_job())

from app.core.redis import new_redis
from app.services import missions as missions_service
from app.services import quota as quota_service
from app.tasks.celery_app import celery_app
from app.tasks.runtime import for_each_tenant, run


@celery_app.task(name="quota.rebalance")
def rebalance():
    async def _all():
        redis = new_redis()
        try:
            async def _job(db, tid):
                return await quota_service.rebalance_tenant(db, redis, tid)
            return await for_each_tenant(_job)
        finally:
            await redis.aclose()
    return run(_all())


@celery_app.task(name="quota.cleanup")
def cleanup():
    async def _job(db, tid):
        return await quota_service.cleanup_tenant(db, tid)
    return run(for_each_tenant(_job))


@celery_app.task(name="missions.generate")
def generate_missions():
    async def _all():
        redis = new_redis()
        try:
            async def _job(db, tid):
                await missions_service.expire(db, tid)
                return await missions_service.generate_for_tenant(db, redis, tid)
            return await for_each_tenant(_job)
        finally:
            await redis.aclose()
    return run(_all())


@celery_app.task(name="missions.reconcile")
def reconcile_missions():
    async def _job(db, tid):
        await missions_service.expire(db, tid)
        return await missions_service.reconcile(db, tid)
    return run(for_each_tenant(_job))

# FICHIER: tasks/report_tasks.py
################################################################################

import uuid

from app.core.redis import new_redis
from app.services import report as report_service
from app.tasks.celery_app import celery_app
from app.tasks.runtime import run, task_db


@celery_app.task(name="reports.generate", autoretry_for=(Exception,), retry_backoff=True,
                 retry_kwargs={"max_retries": 3})
def generate(tenant_id: str, report_id: str):
    async def _job():
        tid = uuid.UUID(tenant_id)
        redis = new_redis()
        try:
            async with task_db(tid) as db:
                await report_service.generate(db, redis, tid, uuid.UUID(report_id))
        finally:
            await redis.aclose()
    return run(_job())


################################################################################
# FICHIER: tasks/road_tasks.py
################################################################################

from app.core.redis import new_redis
from app.core.road_settings import road_settings as rs
from app.services import roads as roads_service
from app.tasks.celery_app import celery_app
from app.tasks.runtime import for_each_tenant, run, task_db

_LOCK = "roads:overpass:lock"


@celery_app.task(name="roads.ensure_network")
def ensure_network(force: bool = False):
    """Planifie l'import initial (une seule fois) puis relance les tuiles manquantes, échouées ou bloquées."""
    async def _job():
        redis = new_redis()
        try:
            if not await redis.set("roads:ensure:lock", "1", nx=True, ex=120):
                return {"status": "locked", "queued": []}
            async with task_db() as db:
                return await roads_service.ensure_network(db, redis, force)
        finally:
            await redis.aclose()
    res = run(_job())
    keys = res.get("queued", [])
    for i, key in enumerate(keys):
        import_tile.apply_async(args=[key], countdown=i * rs.ROAD_TILE_SPACING_SECONDS)
    return {**{k: v for k, v in res.items() if k != "queued"}, "queued": len(keys)}


@celery_app.task(name="roads.import_tile")
def import_tile(key: str):
    """Importe une tuile Overpass (une seule requête Overpass à la fois, toutes instances confondues)."""
    async def _job():
        redis = new_redis()
        try:
            if not await redis.set(_LOCK, "1", nx=True, ex=290):
                return {"deferred": True}
            try:
                async with task_db() as db:
                    return await roads_service.run_tile(db, redis, key)
            finally:
                await redis.delete(_LOCK)
        finally:
            await redis.aclose()
    res = run(_job())
    if res.get("deferred"):
        import_tile.apply_async(args=[key], countdown=15)
    if res.get("retry_in"):
        import_tile.apply_async(args=[key], countdown=res["retry_in"])
    for i, child in enumerate(res.get("children", [])):
        import_tile.apply_async(args=[child], countdown=rs.ROAD_TILE_SPACING_SECONDS * (i + 1))
    return {k: v for k, v in res.items() if k != "children"}


@celery_app.task(name="roads.match")
def match():
    """Rattache les mesures au tronçon de route le plus proche (pour chaque tenant)."""
    async def _job(db, tid):
        return await roads_service.match_measurements(db, tid)
    return run(for_each_tenant(_job))


################################################################################
# FICHIER: tasks/runtime.py
################################################################################

import asyncio
import logging
import uuid
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from sqlalchemy import select

from app.db.session import task_session_factory
from app.models.tenancy import Tenant

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def run(coro):
    return asyncio.run(coro)


@asynccontextmanager
async def task_db(tenant_id: uuid.UUID | None = None):
    engine, factory = task_session_factory()
    try:
        async with factory() as session:
            if tenant_id is not None:
                session.info["tenant_id"] = str(tenant_id)
            yield session
    finally:
        await engine.dispose()


async def for_each_tenant(fn) -> dict:
    """Exécute fn(session, tenant_id) pour chaque tenant actif, avec une session liée à ce seul tenant (RLS)."""
    engine, factory = task_session_factory()
    results: dict[str, object] = {}
    try:
        async with factory() as s:
            tenant_ids = (await s.execute(select(Tenant.id).where(Tenant.is_active.is_(True)))).scalars().all()
        for tid in tenant_ids:
            async with factory() as session:
                session.info["tenant_id"] = str(tid)
                try:
                    results[str(tid)] = await fn(session, tid)
                    await session.commit()
                except Exception:
                    await session.rollback()
                    log.exception("tenant_task_failed", extra={"tenant_id": str(tid)})
    finally:
        await engine.dispose()
    return results

# FICHIER: tasks/social_tasks.py
################################################################################

import uuid

from app.core.redis import new_redis
from app.services import accounts as accounts_service
from app.services import leaderboard as lb_service
from app.services import reviews as reviews_service
from app.tasks.celery_app import celery_app
from app.tasks.runtime import for_each_tenant, run, task_db


@celery_app.task(name="reviews.dispatch", autoretry_for=(Exception,), retry_backoff=True, retry_kwargs={"max_retries": 2})
def dispatch(tenant_id: str, measurement_id: str):
    async def _job():
        tid = uuid.UUID(tenant_id)
        redis = new_redis()
        try:
            async with task_db(tid) as db:
                res = await reviews_service.dispatch(db, redis, tid, uuid.UUID(measurement_id))
                await db.commit()
                return res
        finally:
            await redis.aclose()
    return run(_job())


@celery_app.task(name="reviews.close")
def close():
    async def _all():
        redis = new_redis()
        try:
            async def _job(db, tid):
                return await reviews_service.close_expired(db, redis, tid)
            return await for_each_tenant(_job)
        finally:
            await redis.aclose()
    return run(_all())


@celery_app.task(name="leaderboard.refresh")
def refresh():
    async def _job(db, tid):
        n = await lb_service.refresh_tenant(db, tid)
        s = await reviews_service.audit_analysts(db, tid)
        return {"entries": n, "suspended": s}
    return run(for_each_tenant(_job))


@celery_app.task(name="leaderboard.xp")
def xp():
    async def _job(db, tid):
        return await lb_service.xp_catchup(db, tid)
    return run(for_each_tenant(_job))


@celery_app.task(name="accounts.cleanup")
def cleanup():
    async def _job(db, tid):
        return await accounts_service.cleanup(db, tid)
    return run(for_each_tenant(_job))


################################################################################
# FICHIER: tasks/survey_tasks.py
################################################################################

import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import select

from app.core.redis import new_redis
from app.core.survey_settings import survey_settings as sv
from app.models.enums import QualityStatus, SurveyStatus
from app.models.survey import Survey, SurveyResponse
from app.services import survey_ingest, survey_profiles, survey_quality, survey_trust
from app.services.survey_admin import ai_ready
from app.services.survey_ingest import definition_of
from app.tasks.celery_app import celery_app
from app.tasks.runtime import for_each_tenant, run, task_db


@celery_app.task(name="surveys.evaluate", autoretry_for=(Exception,), retry_backoff=True, retry_kwargs={"max_retries": 5})
def evaluate(tenant_id: str, response_id: str):
    async def _job():
        tid = uuid.UUID(tenant_id)
        redis = new_redis()
        try:
            async with task_db(tid) as db:
                res = await survey_quality.evaluate_response(db, tid, uuid.UUID(response_id))
                await db.commit()
                if res and res[1]:
                    await survey_trust.refresh_user(db, redis, tid, res[1])
                    await db.commit()
                return res[0].value if res else None
        finally:
            await redis.aclose()
    return run(_job())


@celery_app.task(name="surveys.sweep_pending")
def sweep_pending():
    """Reprend les réponses restées en attente (file perdue, photos jamais finalisées)."""
    async def _job(db, tid):
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        R = SurveyResponse
        ids = (await db.execute(select(R.id).where(
            R.tenant_id == tid, R.quality_status == QualityStatus.pending, R.created_at < now - timedelta(seconds=60),
            (R.needs_evidence.is_(False)) | (R.evidence_finalized_at.is_not(None))
            | (R.created_at < now - timedelta(minutes=sv.SV_EVIDENCE_WINDOW_MINUTES + 1))).limit(300))).scalars().all()
        for rid in ids:
            await survey_quality.evaluate_response(db, tid, rid)
            await db.commit()
        return len(ids)
    return run(for_each_tenant(_job))


@celery_app.task(name="surveys.reevaluate")
def reevaluate():
    """Les voisins et les références évoluent : les réponses récentes sont réévaluées (décisions admin conservées)."""
    async def _job(db, tid):
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        R = SurveyResponse
        ids = (await db.execute(select(R.id).where(
            R.tenant_id == tid, R.quality_status.in_([QualityStatus.indicative, QualityStatus.validated]),
            R.submitted_at >= now - timedelta(days=21),
            R.evaluated_at < now - timedelta(hours=sv.SQ_REEVAL_AFTER_HOURS)).order_by(R.evaluated_at).limit(300))).scalars().all()
        for rid in ids:
            await survey_quality.evaluate_response(db, tid, rid, force=True)
            await db.commit()
        return len(ids)
    return run(for_each_tenant(_job))


@celery_app.task(name="surveys.evolve_profiles")
def evolve_profiles():
    """Fait évoluer les profils des enquêtes actives selon les choix réels des répondants."""
    async def _job(db, tid):
        done = 0
        surveys = (await db.execute(select(Survey).where(Survey.tenant_id == tid, Survey.status == SurveyStatus.active))).scalars().all()
        for s in surveys:
            if await survey_profiles.due_for_evolution(db, s):
                await survey_profiles.evolve(db, s, await definition_of(db, s), use_ai=ai_ready())
                await db.commit()
                done += 1
        return done
    return run(for_each_tenant(_job))


@celery_app.task(name="surveys.refresh_trust")
def refresh_trust():
    async def _all():
        redis = new_redis()
        try:
            async def _job(db, tid):
                since = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=3)
                users = (await db.execute(select(SurveyResponse.user_id).where(
                    SurveyResponse.tenant_id == tid, SurveyResponse.user_id.is_not(None),
                    SurveyResponse.submitted_at >= since).distinct().limit(5000))).scalars().all()
                for u in users:
                    await survey_trust.refresh_user(db, redis, tid, u)
                return len(users)
            return await for_each_tenant(_job)
        finally:
            await redis.aclose()
    return run(_all())


@celery_app.task(name="surveys.privacy")
def privacy():
    async def _job(db, tid):
        return await survey_ingest.privacy_sweep(db, tid)
    return run(for_each_tenant(_job))
